You replied with very pleasant smile: “I will come to Bhuvaneswar when the Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Temple will be opened”! So, Your Divine Grace was so merciful that you came here (in your *mūrti* form) on January 23, 1991, just five days before the inauguration day of the temple of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, and Jagannātha-Baladeva-Subhadrā-devī. In spite of the poor financial status of the temple, about ten thousand people were given Kṛṣṇa *prasādam* on that occasion. Since then we have been giving *prasādam* to the general public on all the festival days, though the number of guests has increased considerably. So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you will be pleased to see that your order to distribute *prasādam* to everybody who comes to the temple is being carried out to our maximum ability.

The flags and *cakras* with *kalaśas* on top of the five domes are attracting hundreds of people every day. The Western devotees, deprived of getting *darśana* of Lord Jagannātha in Purī (which is the most tragic state of affairs), are now dancing and singing in ecstasy before the Deities of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, and Jagannātha-Baladeva-Subhadrā-devī in your Bhuvaneswar temple. Your heart must be filled with pleasure since this temple opened.

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu preached the *harināma-yajña* in Orissa for nearly eighteen years, but unfortunately the people of Orissa have not taken up this process to the full extent of Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s desire. And as the most merciful Lord Nityānanda liberally preached this *harināma,* begging door to door on Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s instruction, it seems that perhaps you wanted to emphasize Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu’s mercy in this temple at Bhuvaneswar by laying the cornerstone of the last temple you personally founded on His auspicious appearance day in 1977.

The *Sādhaka-kaṇṭhamāla* quotes Śrī Vāsudeva Ghosh as saying that Lord Gaurāṅga is Śrī Kṛṣṇa and Lord Jagannātha is Lord Gaurāṅga. So according to our very limited knowledge, we may be so bold as to say that one of the reasons you had for enjoining us to establish a temple here of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, and Jagannātha-Baladeva-Subhadrā-devī was to establish this truth. Now this truth is being spread from this center all over the world through your dear devotees. The day will come when the prediction of Your Divine Grace will come true and this temple will be one of the best temples you founded.

You are so merciful that you have given shelter to unqualified devotees like us in order to allow us to serve Their Lordships under the guidance of the senior, elevated devotees and *gurus* here.

Looking forward to your mercy, we remain,  
Your sons, grandsons, and grand-daughters in Bhuvaneswar, India.

## Birmingham

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances thrown in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

None of the devotees in Lord Caitanya’s preaching center in the city of Birmingham were so fortunate as to have the blessing of your personal association, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Even though you are no longer present to mundane vision, you are still among us in the form of your transcendental books. Wherever your books are considered to be the basis of all success, there you are present, even more so than before. Further consideration of this point reveals something of your transcendental glories.

Just as you traveled constantly and tirelessly preached all over the world, so your books are now traveling everywhere, and thus you are attending thousands of new preaching engagements every day. Just as you inspired all the devotees to preach with your expert personal guidance, so your books give us all strength and are our very means of conquest in this difficult ocean of Kali. Somehow or other your purports are always relevant to the situation at hand, and they provide the necessary nourishment and guidance for each one of us, for you are living forever within them. Your books, which are actually worshipable Deities, even maintain our material bodies, for where Lord Kṛṣṇa is present, Lakṣmī-devī is also there.

Just as you led *kīrtana* with great expertise and devotion in the past, so you have built a *bṛhat mṛdaṅga,* and now your *kīrtana* is more powerful than ever. The whole *guru paramparā* must be feeling ever-increasing satisfaction from this sweet *kīrtana,* which could not be stopped by even the mightiest of Kali’s atheistic social systems, which lie crumbled in its wake. All the devotees are dancing for joy simply because of this *kīrtana,* which is so thoroughly purifying that it is only a matter of time before the whole world will fall at your feet with great enthusiasm in gratitude for what you have done.

We offer thanks to you from the core of our hearts, and we rightly offer thanks on behalf of all the millions of people who have received your books but have not yet realized that it is to you that they owe everything. You are so kind that even without their asking, you have come to them, healed their gaping wounds, and lovingly mended their injuries by engaging them in the *saṅkīrtana-yajña,* which extinguishes the fire of material existence. You have compassionately entered into the lives of so many people and have imperceptibly destroyed the fate that was awaiting them, and instead, made them fortunate. Indeed, by your mercy you have changed history, and your inconceivable opulences will not be concealed for much longer. Even though you humbly shunned the credit for your achievements, declaring everything to be the mercy of your spiritual master, let us not forget that still, of all the living entities in existence, Kṛṣṇa personally chose you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to save the world.

We are praying to you on this day for a benediction. May our sole desire become to satisfy you always by following strictly in your footsteps and chiming sweetly as completely unresisting instruments in your eternal *kīrtana*. Although we are personally far from this stage, we think it not impossible, for you are even more merciful than we are fallen.

Begging for the shelter of your lotus feet, we remain  
Your devotees in Birmingham, England.  
(written by Parantapa dāsa)

## Bogota

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

I am offering these words on this glorious occasion of your divine appearance among us. You came here just to fulfill the supreme desire of the Supreme Person, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, or Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Assessing your unique standard of devotional service, your extraordinary preaching, the learned Vaiṣṇavas have concluded that you have been especially empowered by the Lord with his *bhakti-śakti*. Those who are able to view things through the eyes of scripture behold you as an eternally liberated soul, more than simply a bona fide *guru—*rather, a true *śaktyāveśa-avatāra*. And to the people in general you are known as the founder-*ācārya* of the International Society of Krishna Consciousness.

Due to material conditioning, we tend to lose sight of Your Divine Grace; we sometimes fail to hear the words emanating from your lotus mouth. Our neophyte mood hampers our appreciation of how your instructions, though a bit inconvenient for our material sense gratification, are in reality our escape hatch from the realm of birth and death, our grand entrance into Vaikuṇṭha. How shameful it is that we still tend to balk at this precious opportunity.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I’m writing on behalf of your struggling Bogota Yātrā and in the process expressing my personal agony and ecstasy, my individual struggle to improve my life of service to Your Divine Grace. I am the only resident here who had the wonderful good fortune to meet you personally. You knew me at my worst, during those crazy autumn days of 1966, and you know me now.

A distant memory surfaces at this point—I was present when you once told your earliest Western followers that they would be traveling to London, etc., to spread your vital mission. I remember how much that excited me, to fantasize that I, too, would soon board some ship and preach (?) abroad. And now, 26 years after our 26-2nd-Avenue encounters, I actually find myself in a foreign land, in a difficult, rather complicated environment. The Kali-yuga in its intensity—bombs, blackouts—all social anomalies are rampant.

In this milieu your small band of faithful ISKCON followers is attempting to maintain and improve the Pañca-tattva Mandir so as to keep Kali and his friends as far distant as possible. It is a real fight—we push on while Māyā and her emissary (the personality of Kali) etch out their inauspicious inroads in our midst, thus undermining our determination. Nonetheless, there are at least a group of us who remain very confident of success. We know that we are not the doers. We just have to remain faithful and sincere.

In closing, I pray to Your Divine Grace to let me keep working very hard for you. I also beg to remind the others of your immeasurable stature and your unlimited mercy. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your servants in Bogota, Colombia.  
(written by Prabhupāda dāsa)

## Bombay

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

It is our greatest good fortune to be given this opportunity to glorify you on this auspicious day. However, this glorification will ring true only if we have been constantly hearing from, thinking about, remembering, and following you and your instructions.

We bow down flat at your lotus feet and beg for a little mercy for all of us here in Bombay, your favorite city and the location of the temple housing your beloved Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rasabihārījī, Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga, and Śrī Śrī Sītā-Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, and Hanumān. There are no words to describe Their charm and cheer. They are our strength, our hope, in preaching to the modern society.

By your great vision you arranged to build such spectacular temples and in-stitutions to attract all the conditioned souls and give them their only chance to get out of the eternal wheel of *saṁsāra* and the pain of living in oblivion.

Your GBC members, *sannyāsīs,* disciples, grand-disciples, householders, *brahmacārīs*, life members, congregational members, friends, visitors, and many others worked together and accomplished so much in the last year.

Many special events were organized to commemorate the incorporation of ISKCON twenty-five years ago; your bold global preaching plans, through ISKCON, are coming true step by step.

Here in Bombay, by far the best and biggest Janmāṣṭamī was celebrated. It was a landmark preaching effort! With that kind of start, there was no question of scaling down for the events to follow later in the year. After Janmāṣṭamī, we stood the real test of our commitment to your mission, and we acquitted ourselves honorably in that too. The mighty Bombay *yātrā* went all out and distributed 75,000 *Bhagavad-gītās* during the book marathon in December 1991.

Before the end of the Silver Jubilee Year, we hope to organize a mammoth *harināma* party and let everyone in Bombay hear and chant the *mahā-mantra* and make their lives sublime.

Your servants in Bombay, India.  
(written by Amiyā Vilāsa Swami)

## Boston

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances, and please forgive us for any offenses we may have committed at your divine lotus feet.

We are experiencing your presence with us on a regular daily basis in so many different ways. We hear you chanting the beautiful songs of the *ācāryas* with such devotion and gravity. You strain to chant the holy name for all to hear while checking your ecstatic symptoms. Your *japa* cuts through the modes and reminds us to focus on the holy name during the morning *japa* period. “He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die when thou art living still in sound! The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living, try to spread the holy name around.”

The video productions of Your Divine Grace allow us who were not so fortunate to see you personally to experience your elegant movements, powerful gestures, and penetrating glance. We praise modern technology as much as it facilitates our meditations on you, our devotional service, etc.

We worship your *mūrti* form, offering flowers and *daṇḍavats* and trying to feelingly chant *Śrī Guru-vandana.* In this way we develop our reverence and affection for you, as the Guru-pūjā practically forces us to contemplate and appreciate all you have done for us. “*Jaya* Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Prabhupāda!” is the climax of our morning program.

The best way to experience your presence, however, as you stated yourself, is by following your instructions. By hearing and following your instructions one can easily understand your magnanimous transcendental position, and one can also, by your grace, understand how great and wonderful Kṛṣṇa is! How He is the Supreme Personality of Godhead! We believe, in fact, that no ordinary *jīva,* at least on this planet in this Kali-yuga, can actually understand these things except by your instructions. Therefore we have dedicated our lives to distributing your sublime instructions, in their many forms, as far and wide as possible.

You’ve given us all the essential *śāstras* with elaborate, concise, and very comprehensible purports. And now we have, in written form, countless letters, essays, lectures, interviews, conversations, biographical accounts, and memoirs. You’ve given us an incredible arsenal to fight Māyā with! So even if this so-called civilization, with all its technology, collapses, as you said it would, we’ll still be well equipped to carry on with our mission, even if we have to preserve every one of your words with hammer and chisel in stone.

All glories to the BBT! All glories to the Bhaktivedanta Archives! All glories to the Bhaktivedanta Institute! All glories to the BTG and the IWR! All glories to the GBC! All glories to Gurukula, the Vedic College, the Vaiṣṇava music institute! All glories to *prasādam* distribution! All glories to simple living and high thinking, cow protection, and self-sufficiency! All glories to ISKCON! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīvallabha! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! You’ve given us everything we need, and then some.

Everything that we have, everything that is developing, and everything that is yet to come should serve to remind us just how indebted we are to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Yet, because of our rascal tendency, we become forgetful of our debt to you and take things for granted. Certainly if we neglect your lotus feet, or your instructions, Kṛṣṇa will reciprocate by diminishing our facility for devotional service. Therefore we pray that we will always be able to keep you in the center of our lives. We also pray for the intelligence to use everything we have very carefully and frugally, in Kṛṣṇa’s service, as you so perfectly exemplified.

In Boston we have a special facility for remembering you, because it was here that you first touched down your lotus feet in the West. The first European settlers in America also landed near here, but unlike them, you had no personal desire to fulfill. They were searching for freedom, but you were already liberated. They wanted material prosperity and expansion, but you were quite content with the simple Vṛndāvana lifestyle. It was fathomless compassion that drove such a swan-like person as yourself, who were accustomed to the pure waters of the Yamunā, to come and dwell among ugly ducklings fond of sporting in polluted water. You were so faithful and dedicated to the order of your spiritual master that you were willing to give up anything and do anything on his behalf. So we think it is befitting for us to aspire to achieve that same faithful dedication to you and try our best to fulfill your desires.

We beg to remain  
Your humble servants and the servants of your servants at New Guṇḍicā Mandir, Boston, Massachusetts.  
(written by Jaya Gaurasundara dāsa)

## Boulder

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to you.

We of ISKCON Boulder, your aspiring grandchildren, are eternally indebted to you for giving us the chance to serve our spiritual fathers in your mission. Please help us in our feeble attempt to spread the glories of the holy name.

*Patita-pāvana hetu tava avatāra.*

Your grandchildren in Boulder, Colorado.

## Brihuega

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer you our humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your lotus feet.

May our bodies always bow down before you and our words properly describe your wonderful personality. May our minds cultivate a deep attachment to your transcendental instructions and always seek the fresh shelter of service to you.

The spiritual master is always ready to bless his disciples, and this is the good fortune of the servants, but today is a very special day for all of us, because we can remember better than ever the wonderful opportunity you gave us: Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the perfect process for leaving this miserable material world and going back to the spiritual world to be with you and to serve Lord Kṛṣṇa and His eternal devotees with pure love. So today we feel especially absorbed in the pure and attractive glorification of your glories.

You created a huge spiritual revolution all over the world by translating, publishing, and distributing books that establish scientifically the existence and the qualities of the all-attractive Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa.

The sincere souls who read your books will change their lives and join the ranks of your followers, increasing the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu in different places. We hanker to be among those souls who want to distribute your matchless gifts in bigger and bigger quantities.

You founded and organized ISKCON so that people of all nationalities could have the means to practice the science of *bhakti-yoga* and in this way reach the level of pure devotional service and become eternally perfect.

Also, your compassion impelled you to accept any difficult situation for training and helping those who developed faith in you; you never avoided those who approached you in search of understanding and love.

Your great desire to expand the glories of Kṛṣṇa was far beyond any comparison, and you never got tired of fighting to accomplish an “impossible mission”: to firmly establish pure devotional service all over this planet in this dark and abominable Age of Kali.

Nevertheless, in spite of having such a good and qualified leader as you, we feel unfortunate because most of us do not have the necessary qualifications to carry out your orders as they should be accomplished. Many of us try to follow your orders a little mechanically and neglectfully, and thus we do not cultivate with sincerity the devotional character and sentiments you wanted in us. Sometimes we do not feel the strength and inspiration to surrender to your desires, and we are even tempted by illusions of Māyā. In so many ways we are so less intelligent and lacking in sincerity, and sometimes even offensive.

As a father forgives the nonsense of his sons, please forgive all our weakness and incongruities. We only hanker to follow in your footsteps, to never forget you, and to remain in your wonderful *saṅkīrtana* movement for the rest of our lives.

We pray for your benedictions and ask you to never let us leave your lotus feet. Please give us your protection.

Your servants at New Vrajamandala, Brihuega, Spain.

## Brisbane

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

How would the sages have recorded this day?  
What would the mystics have had to say?

Did Lord Śiva come?  
Did Nārada sing?  
Did the heavens send their queens and things?

Was your appearance announced in touchstone halls  
To the tumultuous sounds of haribols?

From Kṛṣṇa to Calcutta—it’s difficult to envision.  
One thing’s for certain: only an Abhay could make the transition.

So you came and began Lord Kṛṣṇa’s story,  
Of Kṛṣṇa’s beauty, of Kṛṣṇa’s glory,

But we know only you, and love is what you deserve,  
We will broadcast your work without compromise or reserve—

How in so short a time you did so many miraculous things,  
Giving us lost souls transcendental wings  
To fly to Kṛṣṇa at the speed of mind,  
With lectures and purports perfectly designed.

Mundane poets we, what could we say,  
Even if we had a year and a day?

How can we enshrine what you have done—  
Opening Vaikuṇṭha’s doors to every mother’s son?

All we can really say is thanks  
For Vṛndāvana on the Yamunā’s banks,  
For explaining Lord Rāma and Mother Sītā,  
And the true purports of *Bhagavad-gītā*.

Thanks for the brightness in a devotee’s eye,  
And thanks for the news of the spiritual sky.

Thanks for ISKCON, which grows daily,  
Pure and large.  
Yes, thank you, thank you, thank you  
For our Guru Mahārāja.

Your servants in Brisbane, Australia.  
(written by Sākṣī-Gopāla dāsa)

## Budapest

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances!

All glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!  
All glories to Your ISKCON and BBT!

Another year has passed, and your movement has expanded in Hungary. By your transcendental potency you empowered our devotees to bring more new devotees to your lotus feet, to translate and publish new books in Hungarian, to open a new temple, to have a wonderful festival, and to preach widely. This was all possible only by your divine grace, which comes to us through following your movement and devotees. So all the glories go to you and to your representatives.

Our only desire is that you may please continue to empower us through your books and devotees, so that we may fulfill the true meaning of the Vyāsa-pūjā, which is constant glorification of Your Divine Grace through our services.

ŚRīLA PRABHUPĀDA KĪ JAYA!!!

Your servants in Budapest, Hungary.

## Bulgaria

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please eternally accept our most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Another year has passed and we are again on the eve of your appearance day. Now we stand before you a little more experienced and mature in our service for your satisfaction. It is a great privilege for us to assist our spiritual masters in their mission to please you, the great founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON and savior of the world. Although some of us were just being born at the time when you enacted your most sublime *saṅkīrtana* pastimes in the West, we are happy that we tread the same earth touched by your lotus feet, and we are trying to follow in your footsteps. We were so enlivened when Suhotra Swami revealed that you were thinking of the preaching in Bulgaria and that you encouraged and instructed one of your disciples who wanted to come here and preach. Many years passed before we could start the *saṅkīrtana-yajña* in this godless land, but by the mercy of Lord Caitanya and by the pure desire of your representatives to satisfy you, we have two temples visited by about one hundred guests every Sunday, and many of them are coming regularly for the morning and evening programs. Many young people join, realizing that the utmost necessity of human life is to become Kṛṣṇa conscious, and now you have a small army of enthusiastic *saṅkīrtana* devotees who distribute your transcendental books. Those with a demoniac mentality do not like our activities and use the newspapers of the ex-communist party to attack us in various ways. But we know this is a test our faith and determination and see it as a chance to fight for Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please enable us to work more and more to push forward the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement for your satisfaction. There are many innocent souls who are not degraded yet by the opulent sense gratification gradually infecting our country. Please help us save them. Please grant your mercy upon us so that we can go on producing and vigorously distributing your books, which are so needed in human society. People are becoming especially receptive to your books now that the old communist structures and concepts are no longer accepted; they are looking for something else to turn to. Now we are producing your biography in Bulgarian so that everybody can relish the “nectarine” of your pastimes. Please help us become more qualified so that we can fully cooperate for the execution of our spiritual master’s order to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

On this most auspicious day we bow down to your most glorious lotus feet, grateful for your extraordinary compassion and the labor of love you performed for the deliverance of the fallen souls. In all the three worlds nobody can be a greater devotee than you, O lord and master of the devotees.

Your unworthy and still very young grandchildren in Bulgaria.

## Canberra

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most glorious day of your appearance, we offer our humble obeisances at your lotus feet again and again. We are not able to glorify your transcendental qualities, as we are mere conditioned souls.

It is said that no one can repay the debt one owes his spiritual master for saving him from the quagmire of material existence. We can also understand that as we perform devotional service to please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we also increase that debt even more. The debts in this material world cause anxiety, but because this debt is of a spiritual nature, trying to pay it back is a source of great spiritual bliss and knowledge. You are the friend of the poor and the lord and master of the devotees. O master! Be merciful unto us. Give us the shade of your lotus feet. Your fame is spread all over the three worlds.

You have given us everything needed to go back home to Godhead. Every moment of your time was spent for our benefit—giving us so many books, temples, and a worldwide family of devotees where one can feel welcome at any time. You have made ISKCON the spiritual world, a place where there is no anxiety. We just have to hang on to your lotus feet by following your instructions. Then success is guaranteed.

Once I was asked, “How did Śrīla Prabhupāda survive when he first arrived in America, with only fifty rupees?” This person couldn’t perceive how it would be possible, but we can understand through your mercy. You are Kṛṣṇa’s dearest servant, and Kṛṣṇa made all arrangements for you to spread this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

So we can only beg for your mercy to engage us in the service of your dearest Lord, and we can only pray that one day we will be empowered to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness by serving the dearest servant of Kṛṣṇa, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your most fallen servants in Canberra, Australia.

## Cape Town

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Living in the present situation in South Africa, we see very clearly how the bodily concept of life causes so much suffering. In ignorance, people try to rectify the situation by some material arrangement, but they succeed only in pouring more fuel on the fire of conditioned life.

In small corners of this burning land your ISKCON centers are giving the cooling shelter of your lotus feet, thus providing relief for persons wanting to escape from this material world. It is only in your ISKCON that the entire world can live together in unity, free of all material differences—one spiritual family, with you as the center.

Please do not let us forget what is most pleasing to you: the distribution of your books and the preaching of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We pray that these will always be the essence and foundation of your Society here.

Every day, as your army of preachers goes out to inject Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the form of your books, *prasādam,* and the holy name, your mercy becomes very apparent to us all.

One person recently called the temple to say that he had received one of your books on the street. He confessed that when he reads a book, he always tries to find fault with what is written. But after reading your *Science of Self-Realization,* he said, he admitted to himself that he could find no fault with it. And he also found that he fully agreed with everything you were saying. Days later he came to the Gaura Pūrṇimā festival and got a full dose of transcendental bliss.

At the local university many students are being attracted to Kṛṣṇa consciousness by your teachings.

In Africa you said that by *harināma* and *prasādam* distribution we can attract the local people. Following these instructions, we are getting deep realization that if we only follow your directions, then all success will come. When we visit the squatter camps with our Food for Life van, the residents eagerly greet the devotees and amaze us with their enthusiasm to chant the holy names and take *prasādam*.

Under your direction, this movement is giving people hope. We, your followers, pray that we can always have complete faith in your instructions and that every living being can take shelter in the house that you have built.

Please allow us to remain  
Your humble servants in Cape Town and at the Bhakti-yoga Society in the University of Cape Town.

## Caracas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most joyful and blessed day of your transcendental appearance, I can remember your transcendental words and activities. This is a blessing from you so that I may become purified from material contamination and in this way become attached to the sacred dust of your lotus feet, which are the only secure shelter for fallen souls like myself.

Your appearance in our lives is like the sun appearing at midnight, making everything visible, taking away the fear of ignorance that covers our real identity as eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa.

I remember the example you gave that if you want to be always in the sunshine, you always have to follow the sun, and in this way one will not experience darkness. So, in the same way, by always following your brilliant instructions, which are like *cintāmaṇi* gems, we will always keep ourselves in the blissful light of your loving and divine presence.

Please keep me at your lotus feet and correct me when, following my mind, I deviate from your instructions. This ISKCON movement is an extension of your body, and your disciples and grand-disciples are your expansions as well. Only by your mercy can I understand this and engage myself in the preaching of your glories and teachings, which alone can deliver the whole world from repeated birth and death. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are my eternal father. Please forgive this foolish son of yours and accept me again and again. Otherwise I have no shelter.

Your aspiring servants in Caracas, Venezuela.

## Caxias do Sul

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet!

No one can find the limit of your glories. We try to offer you some worship at your lotus feet. We do have a few flower petals, and we would like to offer ourselves to you as completely surrendered souls.

The spiritual master acts like the divine sun, revealing the rays of transcendental knowledge to the living entity who is confused by the darkness of ignorance. By your mercy divine knowledge is revealed. After passing through many births and traveling all over the universe, one can get the great good fortune of meeting a pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa, who can destroy our ignorance.

You have the power to dissipate all the nescience in our lives, just as the sun dissipates the darkness of night.

We pray to always remain in the dust of your lotus feet.

Your servants in Caxias do Sul, Brazil.

## Charkor

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu!

This year we have the good fortune to offer the Vyāsa-pūjā homage to Your Divine Grace and beg for your blessings. The Lord and His pure devotees come to this material world with the sole purpose of giving blessings to the devotees, because the only desire of the devotees is to attain pure devotional service, and without these blessings they are unable to do that.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, everything that we have now is your mercy. You have given us the shelter of Kṛṣṇa conscious life and saved us from material bewilderment.

You have given us the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra,* the shelter of the holy name, and the teachings of Lord Caitanya, all of which cleanse the heart from all dirt accumulated for many years and lifetimes. You have given us ecstatic *kīrtanas* and nectarean *bhajanas*.

You have brought to us the knowledge of the spirit soul—that we are not this body but eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa. You have given us the shelter of your books, Vedic knowledge, which surpasses all other philosophies. Your books are giving us the shelter of the Absolute Truth. You have given us the shelter of these wonderful classes: *Bhagavad-gītā, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and *Caitanya-caritāmṛta.*

You have given us the shelter of the temples in the midst of this chaos of Kali-yuga. Actually, they are your temples, because distribution of your books maintains all other programs.

You have given us the shelter of the Deities. Even the Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi Deities we are worshiping in our temple were donated to ISKCON by a rich person who has deep respect for you. You have given us a very simple way of creating the Vaikuṇṭha atmosphere by worshiping the Deity.

You have given us the holy *dhāmas,* since it is only by your mercy that we have an opportunity now to visit these places. You personally built the temples in Bombay, Vṛndāvana, and Māyāpura to give shelter to all present and future devotees from all over the world, so that they could be enlivened by the atmosphere of these holy places. In India the sense of dependence upon your grace is very strong.

You have given us the easiest way to attain Kṛṣṇa’s mercy—*prasādam*. Everyone can experience the higher taste of Kṛṣṇa consciousness by taking *prasādam,* the first step in purification of the senses.

You have given us wonderful festivals, which are really joyous, the way of celebration in spiritual life.

And finally you have given us the shelter of your devotees. Before you came to the West there were no devotees, only so many frustrated people, such as the hippies. But you performed a miracle by turning them into shining devotees. You gave us spiritual masters so that we have a strong connection with Kṛṣṇa through the disciplic succession and proper guidance.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have built a house in which the whole world can live. Your position as founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON is unique. When you first came to the West, you had nothing; nobody helped you, not even your Godbrothers. But you had your books and the great desire to fulfill the order of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. You also had firm faith in the words of your spiritual master. And out of this three things, everything has come.

Therefore, on this auspicious day we beg you to give us the shelter of your lotus feet so we can attain these three things: your transcendental books, the desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and faith in your words and your representatives.

Please give us a deeper and deeper understanding of the potency of your transcendental books. Please give us just a drop of your compassion toward the conditioned souls, who are suffering in the material world without knowing about their real home in the spiritual world with Kṛṣṇa. And please bless us so that we may become faithful servants of your servants in fulfilling your mission.

We are completely dependent upon Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

The servants of your servants in Charkor, Ukraine.  
(written by Īśvara Purī dāsa)

## Chicago

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please do accept our obeisances.  
We fall at your lotus feet.  
To worship Your Divine Grace today  
we have decided to meet.

We offer you our deepest respects  
on this most auspicious day.  
We wish to praise and glorify you,  
though we know not what to say.

They say that it takes one to know one.  
Therefore who are we to say?  
Unless you empower us to write,  
we’ll just throw this pen away.

You appeared on Nadotsava day  
to fulfill Caitanya’s plan  
that His holy name would be chanted  
in each and every land.

Like a lotus flower in water  
does remain completely dry,  
You lived in this world unaffected.  
Others could not, though they tried.

Though so many glamorous “gurus”  
had many disciples come,  
Mahāprabhu had a plan in mind;  
time showed you to be the one.

You’ve been chosen by Śrī Caitanya  
to bring forth the Golden Age.  
Only you can dispel ignorance  
and free all from Māyā’s cage.

Wishing to take the whole world back home,  
wanting to give all a chance,  
you ventured first to America  
praying, “Lord, just make me dance.”

You found hippies and social drop-outs  
on New York’s Lower East Side  
willing to pioneer your movement  
and to help it spread worldwide.

Similarly you found sincere souls  
in the Haight-Ashbury daze.  
Your transcendental attractiveness  
did free them from Māyā’s maze.

In our useless lives we were thinking,  
“There has to be more than this.”  
You kindly came to save us, showing  
the path to eternal bliss.

You accepted us without caring  
who was fit and who was not.  
If devoid of your causeless mercy,  
in this world we’d simply rot.

With transcendental vision you saw  
temples and devotees come.  
You pushed on the saṅkīrtana movement  
knowing what it would become.

You preached according to one wise rule—  
time and place and circumstance.  
You thus saved all classes of people,  
teaching them to chant and dance.

You didn’t give mere philosophy,  
which anyone else could give.  
Rather, you built an entire house  
in which the whole world can live.

You taught that if we fought with Māyā,  
determined to never budge,  
that soon the whole world would surely see  
tilaka on the high court judge.

Chanting the holy names, you taught,  
is the one and only way  
for getting free from Māyā’s clutches,  
which delude us day by day.

You spread chanting of the holy names  
to ev’ry town and village.  
Thus the storehouse of love of Godhead  
you did so kindly pillage.

You delivered this love of Godhead  
not to just a select few,  
but to all people across the world—  
Christian, Hindu, Muslim, Jew.

You conquered students and atheists  
with your transcendental foods.  
Even they began surrendering  
as they quickly changed their moods.

Your Festival of the Chariots—  
such a colorful array!  
The impersonalists and voidists  
just looked on in great dismay.

White elephants and black elephants,  
yellow ones and even red—  
They came proudly dancing down the streets  
despite what others said.

When they saw the harinām party,  
they just said, “They must be high!”  
But now the world’s people are chanting  
“Kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana kī jai!”

Smashing fools and impersonalists,  
you put them all in their place.  
Your books alone reign victorious.  
Glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your book sales keep on skyrocketing.  
No one can perceive the end.  
Your books are now read throughout the world  
by women, children, and men.

The whole world’s atmosphere is changing  
by the presence of your books.  
Doubtful critics and historians  
must now take another look.

Let all continue hearing your words—  
like nectar they taste so sweet.  
Because of your books the whole world shall  
bow to Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet.

You are thus our beloved savior,  
as you showed us our real self.  
The only way of paying you back—  
we’ll put books on each man’s shelf.

You distributed love of Godhead;  
to all it was given free.  
You are thus the most important branch  
of Caitanyadeva’s tree.

We offer you our thanks and gratitude  
on this most wonderful day,  
for you have generously given  
that for which we cannot pay.

Your mercy flows unlimitedly;  
thus we wish to beg for more.  
We’ll take large quantities of it and  
distribute it door to door.

One can lead a horse to the water,  
but one cannot make him drink.  
Please force-feed us your causeless mercy,  
for without it we will sink.

You are thus the captain of our ship,  
and we are your motley crew.  
Your mercy is all we are made of.  
Without it, what can we do?

We offer you our obeisances  
on this most glorious day.  
We’re mere atoms at your lotus feet,  
and there we’ll forever stay.

Your humble servants in Chicago, Illinois.

## Christchurch

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine lotus feet, which give shelter to all the fallen souls in this dark Age of Kali.

Another year has passed in your service, O merciful master, and so the time has come again to put pen to paper and glorify your unlimited transcendental qualities. Just as Ananta Śeṣa never ceases to glorify the Lord, in the same way there is no limit to your glorious pastimes.

Where do we begin to tell the story of a *sādhu* of your caliber? This year many very striking features of your greatness have become apparent, the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is spreading rapidly in every country of the world due to the distribution of millions of transcendental books, *harināma saṅkīrtana* with thousands of eager chanters—in Poland, Russia, and all around the world. Food for Life is serving millions worldwide. School programs, TV shows, cooking courses, Sunday feasts—unlimited preaching goes on. In awe we observe that your intense desire to save the planet from the grips of Kali has become an absolute reality.

It won’t be long before the Golden Age of Śrī Caitanya is in full swing. The credit belongs to you—because if it weren’t for you and your unlimited compassion, we would all be drowning in the ocean of material existence, forced by our own foolish desires into all kinds of abominable species of life. You said once that you would consider your mission successful if you made even one pure devotee. All we can say is that you have been more than a success, because we don’t even have to leave this little old town of Christchurch to find so many pure souls dedicating their lives cent percent to the preaching mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Dedicated *pūjārīs,* empowered preachers, ecstatic *kīrtana* leaders, devotees in all departments giving their hearts to the service of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gauracandra and your divine self.

His Holiness Tamāl Krishna Goswami, always peaching, teaching, saving souls from all corners of the globe. Showing by his example the mood of a dedicated disciple.

Harikeśa Swami, book distributor supreme. Beloved grandson of Bhakti-siddhānta Sarasvatī, surrendered completely to you.

Jagadīśa Goswami, gentle learned *brāhmaṇa,* compassionate, caring, and kind. Maintaining devotees, protecting and nurturing those who have taken shelter in your house.

Lokanāth Swami, Padayātrā personified. Taking the mercy of Śrī Caitanya to every town and village.

Hridayānanda dāsa Goswami, diligently translating Vedic literature for the benefit of all mankind.

Mukunda Goswami, ISKCON communicator. Making Hare Kṛṣṇa a household word.

Indradyumna Swami, shining brightly, radiating enthusiasm. Preaching vigorously in New Guinea, Poland, Russia, South Africa, Australasia. Wherever he goes always enthusing those around him to engage in Kṛṣṇa’s service.

The list goes on and on—so many of your children and grandchildren taking on the qualities of the Param Guru of ISKCON. You must be so proud when you look around and see these serious students dedicate their lives 100% to following your instructions. You must be very pleased with them, and Kṛṣṇa must be very pleased with you. The greatness of the *guru* is shown by the greatness of his disciples. You were once asked about your mystic powers, and you explained that your mystic potency was that you could turn dogs into *brāhmaṇas*. This we see happening before our very eyes as a testimony to your unlimited spiritual potency. May we continue to serve the lotus feet of all those great souls empowered by you. In this way the unbroken chain of disciplic succession remains potent to deliver Kṛṣṇa to the heart of every conditioned soul.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, savior of the whole world!

We beg to remain  
Your eternal servants, the devotees in Christchurch, New Zealand.

## Columbus

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please, accept our humble obeisances. Thank you very much! Your sacrifice to help this world is so great that even a wretched fool like myself can take up the process of devotional service and become attached to Lord Kṛṣṇa. What would I be without Your Divine Grace? You are the real *mahātmā,* and service to you is *dvāram āhur vimukteḥ,* the gate to liberation. You sacrificed your life for us, to save us from this material entanglement. You offered us attachment to Lord Kṛṣṇa and a higher taste in spiritual life. You gave us so many gifts. Indeed, they are “matchless gifts.” This is your appearance day and we love you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Now we are standing here on your behalf, serving Lord Kṛṣṇa and preaching. With your books in our hands, we beg door to door, “Please, read these books and be blessed!” “Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and be happy.” Śrīla Prabhupāda, you touched our lives with your blessings and your gifts, changed our hearts. I wonder at times, Why me? I am not worthy of your matchless gifts! But you encourage me to go on and do my service on your behalf.

While engaged in preaching work, we meet many opposing elements. At times we are ill-treated, and preaching seems quite a thankless task! There are so many problems because the conditioned souls are not prepared to receive the transcendental knowledge of devotional service. Sometimes devotees are violently attacked. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are facing hard times! We miss you so much—your gentle smile, your voice, your very personality—and in separation we search to follow you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught us how to find you in your books and instructions. It is now up to us to become battle-hardened and to follow you by fulfilling your desires. For the upcoming year we will try even harder to do just that. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pledge to continue door-to-door preaching with your books! We will continue to witness to the world your glories in serving Lord Kṛṣṇa. This we promise as we worship you on this day, the anniversary of the most blessed event, the appearance day of our beloved spiritual master, His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

Your servants in Columbus, Ohio.  
(written by Khañjanalocana dāsa)

## Copenhagen

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your holy lotus lotus feet. You are the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead, and we are trying to act as your assistants in your eternal service of spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

You were satisfied in Goloka.  
To you Kṛṣṇa spoke,  
“Go down to Martyaloka  
To deliver those poor folks.”

On His order  
You appeared in Gauḍa-desh.  
On guru’s order  
You came to the West.

If you had not come,  
We can’t imagine our fates.  
Māyā would have had us under her thumb—  
Destiny far worse than AIDS.

Souls lost in darkness,  
Prisoners of illusion,  
Acting in complete craziness,  
Wandering around in confusion

Before you appeared—  
We thought we were God.  
Our speculations we held so dear:  
“Instead of God you are dog!”

Senses dull and numb,  
Flesh of animals we considered food.  
You introduced prasādam—  
“Oh, it tastes so good!”

Devotees from different lands  
You brought all together;  
The true brotherhood of man;  
We have a common father.

Mundane music and dancing  
Before we met—  
You taught us Hare Kṛṣṇa chanting  
And also the Swami-step.

Your spiritual emotions  
Expressed in every page,  
Books are your main contribution  
To drive away the Kali age.

Your books, like the sun,  
All darkness dispelled;  
In their presence  
The end of material existence spelled.

Festivals, books, and harinām,  
Scientific preaching, farms, and gurukula,  
Distribution of prasādam,  
Deity worship, cows, and bulls,

Producing and distributing books,  
A transcendental city in Māyāpur,  
A great project you undertook—  
You mercifully gave us so much to do.

So much you did create  
In so short a time.  
Your glories will be spread  
With all our efforts combined

A house you have built,  
Big enough for all,  
Serving together free from guilt,  
Answering to Gaurāṅga’s call.

You have a heart of molten gold,  
A true servant of the Lord,  
Reclaiming the fallen souls  
Back to the Supreme God.

What would have happened  
if you had not come?  
We’d be running like headless chickens,  
Remaining stupid and dumb.

We are forever thankful  
That you took on the task,  
Caring and guiding us fools  
Out of this ocean so vast.

Diving at your lotus feet,  
We smear our body with dust.  
May we show our love by deeds,  
And may your merciful glance be on us forever cast.

We beg to remain the servants of your servants,  
Your devotees in Copenhagen, Denmark.  
(written by Cirañjīva dāsa)

## Costa Rica

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet, where all the souls of the universe can take shelter.

*yadā yadā hi dharmasya glānir bhavati bhārata*  
*abhyutthānam adharmasya tadātmānaṁ sṛjāmy aham*

**“Whenever and wherever there is a decline in religious practice, O descendant of Bharata, and a predominant rise of irreligion—at that time I descend Myself” (*****Bhagavad-gītā*** **4.7).**

Definitely it’s you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who have come to fulfill Kṛṣṇa’s promise, with the same purpose to redeem the pious and annihilate the wicked materialistic philosophers and atheistic scientists, as well as to reestablish the principles of religion. You are doing this with your unparalleled books and your glorious movement, which is taking to all the corners of the planet the message of pure love for Kṛṣṇa. Just as the rays of the sun illuminate everything, so your marvelous books are carrying the light of knowledge to everybody, in the streets and houses of every town and village.

All the media and technological advances have received your mercy by being used in the service of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. This is the proof of your greatness as a pure servant of Kṛṣṇa.

If we stay surrendered to your lotus feet, we are guaranteed to go back home to the spiritual world, which, as you explain in your books, is a place where the anxiety that floods these material worlds does not exist, but where there is only transcendental happiness, where each step is a dance and each word a song.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! Your life is a constant *līlā* without any anxiety, and while you were on the planet, when difficulties came, you took advantage of them to show us your countless good qualities.

As the time passes by, we better appreciate the wonderful world of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to which you have invited us, although we are unworthy of it.

You have left practically everything done, like the farmer who works the land, sows the seed, and invites his sons to pick and distribute the fruits.

The constant growth of Lord Caitanya’s movement is proof of your presence in it. Just as a body grows and reproduces or as a tree grows new branches and fruits each year because of the presence of the soul, so in your International Society for Krishna Consciousness we see the tree trunk get stronger every year and producing fruits abundantly. Your only interest is to see us advancing spiritually, and this is also the interest of your disciples, who are now taking care of our spiritual life like our eternal fathers.

In your books we have the necessary instructions to know the truth about Kṛṣṇa and serve Him with love, and also to teach it to others.

You are not different from Śrī Nityānanda, who is the *ādi-guru.* He is *avadhūta;* nobody can understand Him. By your mercy we can see in practice that the most important thing is not to understand but to serve, serve, serve. If we serve, automatically we understand Him and love Him, even if we don’t notice it. The debt we owe you cannot be repaid even in many lifetimes, but on this occasion we promise to give our lives and souls to your noble mission. Uddhava describes your glories to Kṛṣṇa in the Eleventh Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam:*

“O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the long life of Brahmā, for You appear in two features—externally as the *ācārya* and internally as the Supersoul—to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to approach You.”

All glories to the divine day of your transcendental appearance!

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to you, who saved the whole world! All glories to you, who came to give us the great treasure of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the priceless gem of devotional service!

The humble servants of your servants in Costa Rica.

## Coventry

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

In writing this offering we again have this wonderful opportunity to glorify you and to remember the limitless mercy of your coming to the West. We know that you are the representative of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and that you have given us the nice process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. All glories to the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, which you have so wonderfully served, following the instructions of your beloved spiritual master. It is your dedication and your devotees that have made the mercy of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa available even to us here in Coventry, worthless as we are.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are eternally our inspiration, and we struggle to follow your instructions and example. You live for us in your books, your conversations, and your tapes. Even those activities of yours which you might consider trivial or mundane are sources of enjoyment and instruction for us, for in you we see only the acts of Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotee. We marvel at your scholarship and industriousness for Kṛṣṇa. We feel for you when we remember your days of hardship when you first arrived in America, and we rejoice with you as we recollect how your new movement took hold and began to flourish. Through your words and the recollections of your first disciples we can share in your mercy and compassion toward those unfortunate souls deep in the miseries of *māyā*. Only in hindsight can we truly appreciate the great vision of a world organization which you chalked out so early and which has come to pass. Your mission has reached out to us here in Coventry, and we thank you again and again.

In comparison to your great achievements, our achievements are nothing, and we struggle even to follow your instructions. Yet by your grace and mercy we can venture to say that our center continues to flourish and improve as we attempt to follow the process you have brought us.

During the Christmas marathon we successfully approached local businessmen and householders to sponsor the placement of *Bhagavad-gītās* in as many homes as possible in this area. Our children continue to perform instructive and enjoyable musical dramas, both in our own center and in other local community programs. We are continually improving our facilities, with the vision of gradually turning our center into a full ISKCON temple. Please continue to look kindly upon us and bless us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we thank you enough for being our well-wisher? You are always in our thoughts and our hearts. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Help us to be worthy of your mercy and allow us to remain under the shelter of your divine lotus feet.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
Your affectionate grandchildren in Coventry, England.

## Croatia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

You have never been in Yugoslavia personally, but you blessed it by mentioning it a few times and by enjoying listening to the promising preaching reports from it. In one particular lecture it seems that you even predicted that it would split into several countries. When told about how enthusiastic the people here were for chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, you said that in “Yugo-slovania,” which is a communistic country, people are so enthusiastic for chanting. You were corrected—”Yugoslavia”—but you again repeated “Yugo-slovania.” Today we have a part of our country which is called Yugoslavia, and also other parts, one of which is called Slovenia. Even when we were listening to these words years ago, we were feeling that they might be a kind of a prediction. Everything connected to you is so deep, supramundane, and prophetic. It is not possible to understand and appreciate you fully. Kṛṣṇa is revealing your opulences gradually, and it is becoming more and more clear that you are a very special personality. Indeed, very special! Although we did not see you personally, which we regret very much, we know that we cannot praise the Lord enough for being so kind to allow us to be born at this auspicious moment, when we can still feel your recent presence and associate with your immediate followers and intimate disciples. Just the thought of the possibility of being born a hundred years ago in this materialistic country and not getting a chance to take part in this most wonderful pastime of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness around the world is terrifying.

We thank you for giving us such wonderful guidance and association, which prevent us from misunderstanding your mission and in this way spoiling both your wonderful work and our good luck. Thank you millions of times for leaving your comfortable spiritual situation just for our sake. Thank you for giving us everything worth possessing, and please excuse us for not being able to repay you properly.

Your thankful grand-disciples from Croatia.

## Curitiba

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the true representative of Lord Kṛṣṇa. It is recommended that one honor the spiritual master as having equal status with the Supreme Personality of Godhead: *sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstraiḥ.* This is enjoined in every scripture: *ācāryaṁ māṁ vijānīyām*. And the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (7.15.26) states, “The spiritual master should be considered to be directly the Supreme Lord because he gives transcendental knowledge for enlightenment.”

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious and wonderful day. You appeared in this world in order to bring the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa to the fallen conditioned souls. Preaching, working day and night, translating literature, establishing temples and farms, you arranged for the printing and distribution

of your transcendental books. Only you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, could achieve all this.

You are so great that you accepted us. Even though we can barely follow your simplest instructions, you have given us the shelter of your lotus feet. You are an ocean of mercy, the bona fide representative of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You are our only shelter. Without your grace there is no hope of our developing love of Godhead.

So on this day we fervently pray to you for the strength to be perfect disciples and to fulfill your instructions, and for the strength to spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu for the welfare of all living beings in this material world.

Please accept our humble offering on this most blessed event of your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Your servants in Nova Goloka Dhāma (Curitiba), Nova Jagannātha Purī (Morretes), and the gurukula.

## Czechoslovakia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Where would we be, had it not been for thee?  
Lost, lost in the oblivion of futility.

What would we do, had it not been for you?  
Doomed, doomed—for eternity!

What would we see, had it not been for thee?  
Illusion, yes, only illusions of bodily identity.

What would we hear, had it not been for your being real?  
Just useless rubbish, garbage, refuse from the demoniac society.

By your mercy, O most munificent Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
we are members of ISKCON, the sublime movement of Lord Caitanya.

By your humble and dedicated efforts, O most kind Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
we are at present engaged in devotional service, our eternal *svarūpa*.

By your unflinching faith in guru and Kṛṣṇa, O eternally liberated Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
we can see reality—we are not the body! We can see the mūrtis, the supermost  
transcendental forms of eternal beauty.

By your graceful words, O most learned Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can hear the most nectarean vibrations  
of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*,  
of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*.

Yes, we are factually *bhāgyavān jīvas*, most fortunate souls.

On this auspicious day of your transcendental appearance,  
we can only bow our heads in deep gratitude,  
prostrate our bodies and surrender our souls.

Where would we be, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
where would we be if it weren’t for thee?  
Lost, lost in the oblivion of futility.

Your humble and ever-indebted servants in Czechoslovakia.  
(written by Jaya Gurudeva dāsa)

## Dallas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our spiritual master, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, deserves all praise. He is a pure devotee of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Kṛṣṇa. By his vigorous preaching, he has successfully defeated all atheistic and non-devotional philosophies and has inundated the entire world with knowledge of the science of love of God. He sets the standard for transcendental behavior. He is the embodiment of perfection. Due to his missionary work, the most recent and important incarnation of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, has become internationally famous, although heretofore the Lord was hardly known even within India. He has given back to all Indians the self-esteem denied them by British propaganda and has given the rest of us the wealth of Vedic culture. Through His books and instructions, the lives of thousands have been—and are continuing to be—transformed from base mundane metal to sublime spiritual gold. He has given freely the key to unlimited peace and happiness and has thrown open the gates to the kingdom of God by inviting everyone to take shelter of the chanting of the Lord’s Holy Name:

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare*

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in Dallas, Texas.  
(written by Dṛṣṭa dāsa)

## Danish Farm

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Thank you for allowing me this nice opportunity to praise you on your Vyāsa-pūjā day. The world is full of philosophers, professors, scientists, artists, and teachers of all kinds, and their ideas are printed in tons of books placed in libraries all over the world.

The intellectuals of today are completely under the spell of these books. And all action in society takes place according to their fundamental conclusion:

*asatyam apratiṣṭhaṁ te jagad āhur anīśvaram*  
*aparaspara-sambhūtaṁ kim anyat kāma-haitukam*

**“They \[the demons\] say this world is unreal, with no foundation, no God in control. They say it is produced of sex desire and has no cause other than lust” (Bg. 16.8).**

Of course, they could never put it down so precisely as Kṛṣṇa has done, but this is their understanding—there is no God, there is no life after death, we should simply enjoy our senses.

The variations on this theme are endless and boring, but somehow or other they have managed to persuade everybody by massive propaganda. This has brought nothing but endless pain and suffering to mankind (and animalkind).

Why?

The answer to this simple question never really dawned on me until I read your books and your completely clear explanations and thorough analysis of our activities in this world: eating, sleeping, mating, and defending.

Nothing more…

That’s it…

No more discussion. This is their philosophy, thousands of them, millions! No matter how they wrap it up, it’s just eating, sleeping, mating, and defending. This simple understanding of the illusions of modern materialistic society would never have been possible if not for Your Divine Grace and your books. And that understanding is just the beginning. It is just the needlepoint bringing in the endless gigantic plow of knowledge and bliss in a true life of service to Kṛṣṇa.

*rāja-vidyā rāja-guhyaṁ pavitram idam uttamam*  
*pratyakṣāvagamaṁ dharmyaṁ su-sukhaṁ kartum avyayam*  
*(Bg. 9.2)*

For this knowledge and for showing us how to practice it and enjoy it, we have to thank you again and again and offer you our service by following the orders of your bona fide followers giving this causeless mercy to others, considering that we ourselves were also just eating, sleeping, mating, and defending until we met your *saṅkīrtana* devotees on the street.

Falling in the dust of your servants’ lotus feet, we remain  
Your grandchildren on the Danish farm.  
(written by Trayumaya dāsa)

## Debe

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

You came to this world, O Prabhupāda,  
With a mission divine and pure:  
To set in the heart of mankind  
In this age a spiritual cure.

You bore the mantle of mercy  
And shared it far and wide.  
You retrieved so many fallen souls  
And placed them on Kṛṣṇa’s side.

You are a towering master of *bhakti*  
Who spread Lord Caitanya’s grace  
To every town and village  
And to the entire human race.

You built the house of ISKCON  
And gave all souls the light  
To go back home to Godhead,  
In books so pure and bright.

Now, from the core of our hearts, O Prabhupāda,  
Let the *karatālas* and *mṛdaṅgas* play  
In joyful rhythm and melody  
On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Your servants in Debe, Trinidad.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa-kṛpā dāsa)

## Debrecen

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our most respectful and humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we regard you in the same way as King Rahūgaṇa regarded Jaḍa Bharata. The king said to him (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 5.10.19), “You have come to give spiritual knowledge and you are a direct representative of Kapiladeva, the incarnation of God and the plenary portion of knowledge.” Let us meditate on your most merciful, eternal, blissful teachings.

You wrote the most wonderful, everlasting, compassionate books for our deliverance. Even a drop of your nectarean teachings is sufficient to flood our hearts with the desire to serve the lotus feet of those pure devotees who are most happily engaging themselves in the service of the eternally fresh Young Couple, who so charmingly glance at each other, and at you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your words should be our life and soul, the object of our *bhajana* and *pūjā,* the treasure of our hearts. On board the ship *Jaladuta,* in September 1965, you wrote, “How will they understand the mellows of devotional service? … I wish that You may deliver them…. How will I make them understand this message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness?”

Thank you, O dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda. Thank you for everything.

Your eternal servants in Debrecen, Hungary.

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We become awestruck when reflecting upon your unlimited transcendental achievements. How you came to America, an elderly gentleman in frail physical health, risking everything for the eternal benefit of suffering humanity, is the perfect example of selfless service to the message of your Guru Mahārāja.

Undeviated and uninterrupted devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa was the very fabric of your existence.

You translated “the lawbooks for mankind” through the book *bhāgavata,* and just as Lord Caitanya did five hundred years ago, you gave us the perfect example of the proper mentality of a Kṛṣṇa conscious devotee through association with you, the devotee *bhāgavata*.

As you became the architect and general of the world’s greatest movement, you never took credit for yourself, but unceasingly glorified your spiritual master, His Divine Grace Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī, as the great, empowered personality making it all possible.

Your every breath emanated the reality that the instruction of the pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa is everything for the disciple:

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ‘pi*

**“By the mercy of the spiritual master one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement.”**

Please bless us with the benediction that we can always remember that we are very small parts in your very large mission, and that even though you left our vision fifteen years ago, you are continuing daily to guide your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Here in Detroit we are very much aware that our diverse preaching programs are continuing only by your mercy. You have kindly sent many devotees with sincere service attitudes who are helping to increase the programs at the Bhaktivedanta Cultural Center and Temple.

Please help us to always understand and remember that these programs are going on because of your great desire to serve Lord Caitanya, and that somehow you are allowing us to participate for our purification and benefit.

As we all mature in age, please help us mature in our attitude of service to your transcendental mission. Your life’s work was dedicated to serving your Guru Mahārāja’s instructions to print the timeless Vedic literature in English and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the Western world.

Just as it is inconceivable how the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, is continuously expanding Himself, it is also inconceivable that although you are no longer physically present before us, you continue to expand your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Your glorious life is the perfect testimony to your pure devotion to the mission of your Guru Mahārāja. In the same way, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us that we may make our lives perfect by spending the rest of our days in this temporary material world serving your eternal spiritual mission.

Without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are nothing. With your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lives become significant and meaningful, and going back to Godhead becomes a distant but tangible reality.

Your humble, indebted servants in Detroit, Michigan.  
(written by Bhūśāya dāsa)

## Donetsk

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We beg you with great humility to accept our lowest obeisances to the dust of your lotus feet, and despite our ineptitude to accept our miserable attempts at glorifying you. It is impossible for us, who are not even able to speak, to touch upon your glory and that of your devotees!

But having gotten the chance to glorify you by writing this Vyāsa-pūjā homage, this very insignificant devotee may attract your merciful attention. We simply hope that you, our almighty grandfather, will bestow your divine mercy upon us, your grandsons.

To get the opportunity to glorify you, a great Vaiṣṇava, means to get the favor of the whole disciplic succession and that of Lord Śrī Hari. This is a great fortune for those longing to take refuge at His lotus feet.

To glorify you on this momentous day, your appearance anniversary, is not at all a hard business, because you are a very humble devotee of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Therefore we hope to obtain the benediction of our *gurus,* our eternal fathers, that our imperfect attempt at describing your glory can at least in some degree attract your most valuable attention, and may Lord Keśava help us in this.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, there is no beginning of your glory, nor is there an end. It is as everlasting as the knowledge in your books.

We are all fruits of your mercy, and your mercy is unlimited. Just look: we are all happy now due to your favor.

We suffered in this material world, which cheated and severely struck us, the disunited. But you came to us and gathered us by your side, and you protected and comforted us and taught us how to be happy. Why? Because you bestow your grace upon all living entities. You pointed out what is to be done and helped is in so many ways. Because of your intense love for the suffering conditioned souls, you undertook the immensely difficult task of distributing this *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya, and thanks to your instructions we became as we are now: happy, very happy.

What are your instructions, which we should strictly follow? They are very merciful: chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare, dance in ecstasy, and feast on *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* only. These are all your demands. What can be easier! And you inspired us by your own example, clearing away all our doubts by your preaching.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you! Your divine grace is infinite. You gave us the very perfection of life: the holy name for chanting, and the temples for worshiping Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Rādha-Mādhava, and Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, where everybody may contemplate Their divine beauty. You gave us your sons, your strict followers, bred on your mercy. They became our masters, our eternal fathers, pouring your mercy upon our heads. Thanks to them we may perfect our service to the Lord to gladden you. You gave us real knowledge and the power to preach, and any devotee may taste this nectar. And to make everything well grounded, you gave us your books, these many-volumed giants filled with the transcendental nectar of the most confidential knowledge.

Being an eternal witness of the events of the spiritual realm, you described them in detail for our highest benefit! Being absorbed in the ocean of love of Kṛṣṇa, you came to us. To give us Kṛṣṇa became your greatest need. Your heart became as large as the whole cosmic manifestation, and you were ready to accept anybody, whether they were qualfied not. You placed everyone into the core of your magnanimous heart, and although you were absorbed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you felt dissatisfied as long as everyone wasn’t Kṛṣṇa conscious. You revealed your feelings on every page of your books. Whoever came to you could not go away: he was captivated by Kṛṣṇa. And you constantly called and called, and continue to call now. We are very much obliged to you, and therefore we want to help you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda! We beg you to bestow on us silly little chaps the happiness of the most confidential service: calling people back home to God by distributing your books.

All glories to your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your grandsons at the Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi Mandir in Donetsk, Ukraine.

## Dublin

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Since the last Vyāsa-pūjā offering, you have appeared in Dublin in your *mūrti* form, this time to stay. This is your great mercy. Wherever there is even a slight attempt at preaching, you come. This energy and enthusiasm of yours is coming from Kṛṣṇa’s internal potency.

Already the symptoms of your presence are manifesting: we are closer to getting a full-time temple, and our door-to-door book distribution, our festivals, and our *prasādam* distribution are all increasing!

However, we are still failing you here in our irregular morning program and our lack of full-time devotees in the temple.

We can hardly understand your greatness, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Otherwise, we would surrender more to your wishes. Please forgive our neophyte offenses. Please take us in hand. Please engage us in your service. Only by your mercy may we be swept along in the loving service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and thereby attain the goal of going back to Godhead.

Begging for your mercy on this all-auspicious day, we remain  
Your servants in Dublin, Ireland.

## Durban

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your merciful lotus feet.

The process of devotional service begins with hearing and chanting about Kṛṣṇa. Then remembrance can come and the devotee can be absorbed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness while engaging in so many devotional activities.

In the same way, we should hear and chant about you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and develop remembrance of you, always remembering why we are doing what we do and that we are simply your instruments.

If we absorb ourselves in this remembrance, then a certain energy descends on us. Our dullness clears, inspiration and enthusiasm arise within our hearts, and Kṛṣṇa consciousness ceases being merely a “philosophy” and becomes a vibrant transcendental reality full of deep meaning and realizations.

But it is not simply “an energy” which helps us. Kṛṣṇa consciousness is never impersonal. It is a fully personal process. Behind every energy is an energetic person directing it, whether it be Māyādevī directing the material energy, Yogamāyā directing the internal energy, or whoever.

In this case it is you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who direct your own *kṛpā-śakti* to the devotees who strive to remember you and serve you. As said in the *śāstras:*

*yasya deve parā bhaktir yathā-deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

**“Only unto one who has implicit faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the truths of the Vedic scriptures automatically revealed.”**
