You are still here, Śrīla Prabhupāda, watching over and guiding your sincere followers, just as your own Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, was with you while you performed your preaching pastimes among the conditioned souls here on earth.

By your divine grace such inspired devotees can successfully preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness far and wide, organizing the spreading of your movement to all corners of the world very nicely.

By your divine grace the devotees can grasp the most difficult points of transcendental philosophy and thereby enter the deepest realms of Kṛṣṇa conscious knowledge.

For a devotee who remembers you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, even the most difficult things become very easy. What can stand in his way? Your mercy is as great as an ocean, and just one drop will certainly overwhelm our petty material desires and leave us drowning in the nectar of your service.

We are very weak and unqualified, so for us to remember you always and serve you in this mood is not easy. Therefore today we stand before you and pray to you to please be kind to us. What alternative do we have? You are our only shelter.

Your servants in Durban, South Africa.

## El Salvador

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances unto your divine lotus feet. All glories to the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya.

On this very special day, which commemorates your advent in this world, we would like to thank almighty God, because He has manifest His love and causeless mercy by sending His intimate associate, Your Divine Grace, to redeem the misguided conditioned souls.

*guru kṛṣna-rūpa nānā śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees” (Cc.** ***Ādi*** **1.45).**

On this auspicious day, we are very glad to have the opportunity to offer our grateful prayers in plenitude to our most great benefactor, His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad Abhaya Caraṇarāvinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. It is not easy to glorify such a great soul as you, since your unlimited qualities are beyond the comprehension of worldly minds. To glorify Your Divine Grace in an appropriate way, it is essential to be a bona fide recipient of your causeless mercy.

It is said that a loving father is very satisfied when his small children express their love and give him presents, although they were bought with his own money. In the same way, to write this humble Vyāsa-pūjā offering we have used the transcendental intelligence that you yourself have given us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. This intelligence comes as a result of our little development of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, of which you are the great master and possessor.

Although these lines are not eloquent or poetic (because we are not bona fide recipients of Kṛṣṇa consciousness), they still express with all sincerity from the core of our hearts our gratitude for the matchless gifts you have given us. For these we can never repay you.

In this *mahā* celebration we want to express to you that we are eternally indebted to you and that we are conscious of the change we have experienced in our lives. Please bestow upon us forever and ever your mercy so that we can use all our faculties and our whole lives in the loving devotional service of your great movement of love of God.

Your humble servants in El Salvador.

## Florianopolis

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most blessed of all days! We offer our respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

Like the effulgent sun, in the presence of which all darkness is dissipated, your message has removed the darkness of ignorance from our hearts. Śrīla Prabhupāda! Now please be merciful unto us and grant us the boon of constant love and devotion to your lotus feet.

You have kindly left us the task of realizing your desires to spread the ocean of Lord Caitanya’s mercy. And as the confidential servant of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, you have propagated the *saṅkīrtana* movement throughout the entire planet, bringing the most valuable gift in all three worlds: the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.

You, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, are changing the course of history, but in your humility you stated, “I am simply following the instructions of my *gurudeva*.” We pray that we may also become as determined to faithfully carry out your instructions.

Begging to be able to please and satisfy you eternally, as well as to remain the servants of your servants, we remain  
Your devotees in Florianopolis, Brazil.

## Gītā-nāgarī

**Prabhupāda Mūrti**

We come through the temple room door and you are there—*Jaya* Srila Prabhupada!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Little ways of serving you in your *mūrti* form become welcome reminders that we each have a personal relationship with you. Available to anyone, these small, spontaneous acts of appreciation reassure us that yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda is here. On chilly nights someone will remember to wrap you in your big wool *chadar*. Our hearts become warm to see you like that.

The conch blows for *ārati*. Would you like your *karatālas* or your gong, Śrīla Prabhupāda? At *japa* time, a devotee carefully places your hand in your bead bag. Your recorded chanting, “Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare,” reminds us that you have planted the rare and delicate seed of *bhakti* in our hearts. We pray to become attentive hearers of Kṛṣṇa’s holy name.

*Oṁ namo bhagavate vāsudevāya …* It’s *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class. Your bookstand is put before you with the *Bhāgavatam* opened to the verse for the day; your reading glasses are placed on you. A pitcher of fresh spring water is brought to you. In these little ways a common love for you is shared among ourselves. We hear the speaker recite, “Translation and purport by Śrīla Prabhupāda.” One verse has the potency to lift us from the modes.

At Deity greeting time, our family of devotees comes together before Their Lordships. *Jaya* Rādhā-Dāmodara, Rādhā-Kālachandjī, Jagannātha, Baladeva, Lady Subhadrā! *Jaya* Nitāi-Gaurasundara. Although situated in a simple setting, They are always a feast for the eyes (and here are your binoculars) and a shelter for the heart. An opulence at Gītā-nāgarī is the *pūjārīs’* sincere and loving dedication to their Lords.

A myriad of verses, translations, prayers, and songs—nonstop nectar—is heard at the back of the temple room. Sureśvara is absorbed in his service of bathing you and changing your cloth. Afterwards, your loving reciprocation is seen on his face; and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, look especially wonderful and indescribably serene.

Thank you for being here and so kindly reassuring us in our small efforts. But mostly thank you for waiting for us. Your order to organize *varṇāśrama* astounded us here at Gītā-nāgarī, to put it mildly. Our attempts have allowed us to see first-hand our foolishness, incompetence, torrents of mistakes, reluctance to serve, and lack of qualification on all levels. But in spite of all that, it has resulted in clearing new ground for our internal and external growth. We are feeling new hope.

Now our attempts are becoming steadier and gaining momentum. You’ll see when we take you on *parikrama* on your palanquin by ox-cart down through the *dhāma*. See how the farm is repaired so nicely? The cows and oxen graze in the pastures most of the year now. And we are definitely going toward increasing our utilization of the “bulls.” We know prosperity does not depend on our own efforts, but rather on our attempts to actually please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Without you, we cannot do anything.

So individually we fight the good fight, trying to follow your instructions. And collectively we are trying to learn to cooperate—that last, most difficult instruction you charged us with—for without cooperation, how can we speak of love? We are remembering your acute desire, expressed in your last days, to come to this “Pennsylvania Farm” for the sole purpose of teaching *daivī-varṇāśrama,* the ultimate in alternative civilizations.

You have provided our lives’ full purpose and given us potent means for our purification. Please guide us and correct us, help us make your will manifest in a wonderful way. May we always remember that our lives, like the many small services we render unto your *mūrti* form, are meant to be an offering unto you.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at Gītā-nāgarī dhāma, Port Royal, Pennsylvania.  
(written by Svarga-devī dāsī)

## Greece

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Dear Master, you are the life and soul of all ISKCON devotees. You are the most pure, most majestic person. You propagate the essence of the knowledge of the *Vedas*. You have unlimited love, pure love and compassion for us conditioned souls. To all, even the most fallen, you are freely giving Kṛṣṇa’s mercy and love of Kṛṣṇa. Anyone who wants to can buy these from you. And not only that, but you send your mercy to all directions in the world through your big *mṛdaṅga*. “Books, books, Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books!” The eyes of anyone who reads are opened, and he can see the spiritual reality through your *śāstra,* your instructions. You must be very, very dear to Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu, for you re helping Him distribute these sweet fruits of love of Godhead to the whole world.

Here in Greece, for the first time in history the Pulindas will soon have the opportunity to read your pure *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. They are starting to be printed, one volume after another.

Your books are a great hope for this miserable world; they will change it into Vaikuṇṭha. We saw what happened in Russia due to your book distribution. And we are eager to see more surprises coming due to the propagation of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Thank you very much for sending two of your dear sons here as GBC’s to help guide the preaching in this country. Our association here has become very thin. Therefore we beg you to please shower your mercy on us and empower us to go on and to increase our realization of our deep eternal obligation to Your Divine Grace.

Let us really learn how to work together as a family with your divine instructions always in our hearts and memories. Please help us to not become fools and deviate from your instructions, which are the only way to get free from the clutches of Māyā and fix ourselves steadily above the modes of material nature as the eternal servants of your servants. There is no other way, no other way, no other way.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, help us become more intelligent and bold. Enable us to cut through these people’s foolishness. Thus they will see that there is no real happiness in embracing each other in family and nationalistic ties. Their ignorance can be dispelled through the chanting of the holy names, which you have kindly introduced in this age of quarrel and hypocrisy.

Please excuse our childish mistakes and offenses and kindly help us increase our weak desire to become Kṛṣṇa conscious.

Thank you unlimitedly.

Your servants in Greece.  
(written by Nanda Gopa dāsa)

## Guarulhos

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet on this divine day of your appearance in this material world.

You came to this world with the mission of awakening the conditional souls to their pure spiritual position as eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa. So you started this great Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Everybody was identifying themselves with this material body, trying to enjoy the material resources, but you made us see the truth and cured our diseased condition just by your realized teachings and your pure love.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are fallen and weak, but by your causeless mercy and your expert and powerful preaching, we can understand the ultimate goal of life, for you are directly empowered by Lord Kṛṣṇa. Because of this we are now engaged in devotional service unto the Supreme Lord.

You came to give us faithless souls of Kali-yuga *kṛṣṇa-bhakti,* pure love of Godhead. And you also showed us how to develop this devotional love—which is natural to the soul—in our daily, routine activities of eating, chanting, speaking, thinking, etc.

We were dying, but now we are coming back to life by engaging ourselves in devotional service to Kṛṣṇa, although we do not have the necessary qualifications.

Your glory has no limit; there is no doubt about this. You have made it possible for the most intricate transcendental knowledge to be understood by people who until recently were absorbed in the material modes of passion, ignorance, and still lower than that.

Your greatness cannot be understood by conditioned souls; that is why we cannot properly worship you.

Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself says that He and the spiritual master are nondifferent and that He doesn’t accept as His devotee anyone who says he is His direct devotee, but rather He accepts someone as His devotee who says that he is the servant of the servant of His servant.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the love a disciple has for his spiritual master is shown by how strictly he follows his orders. Trying to please you, we simply pray to become the servant of the servant of your servants.

Your servants in Guarulhos, Brazil.

## Guwahati

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. On your auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day, please accept these few words as our humble offering.

**The Return of the Jaladuta**

We were swimming in the midst of the ocean of nescience, and we were becoming too tired to swim. There was every possibility of being drowned. With folded hands we prayed to God. Then a ship became visible. When the ship came nearby, we saw something written on the body of the ship: I-S-K-C-O-N. ISKCON? What does it mean? We heard the crew chanting “Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.” They helped us get into the ship. They are our saviors. We asked, “Who is the captain?” They said all at once, “Prabhupāda! Śrīla Prabhupāda!”

Your servants in Guwahati, India.

## Hare Krishna Land, Mauritius

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

As the years quickly pass and we continue on in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, it becomes increasingly obvious that our spiritual lives are completely dependent on pleasing you. However, when we think of all the programs and projects you wanted developed, we are fearful that we may fail you. Thus we pray for your mercy to succeed.

Many of us foolishly think that the goals you set for our personal devotional standards and for the movement are impractical, something that could only be achieved while you were personally with us. Fortunately for us, you didn’t have the same perception of your spiritual master’s desires. By the combination of this faith, your staunch determination, and his mercy, you perfectly achieved those seemingly unattainable goals. Thus you astonished your Godbrothers, well-wishers, and friends. This example gives us hope.

By minimizing your orders, we can only hurt ourselves, although sometimes we neglect them due to a lack of understanding. When you visited Mauritius, for example, you said that this country could become the first Kṛṣṇa conscious nation in the world. Obviously you want us to try for this today. Therefore this order weighs heavy on our heads. On the other hand, because of our lack of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we doubt that your vision can be realized. We thus prefer to peacefully execute our humble services according to what we believe is practically attainable.

By your mercy you keep reminding us of your desire, for whenever devotees visit Mauritius, they always mention that you said Mauritius could become the first Kṛṣṇa conscious country. Still we feel hopeless. We are not great managers, preachers, or advanced devotees. We have no qualifications to achieve this goal. Please give us the faith to believe that this is possible and the qualifications to achieve it.

You once had a personal secretary who was rendering valuable service but could not regularly finish his rounds and properly execute his *sādhana*. He explained this to you, and because you wanted him to remain your secretary, you told him to continue and that you would maintain his spiritual life. The next day during *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class, you looked directly at him and said, “Don’t try to make spiritual advancement; just try to please your spiritual master.” In spite of this, he felt he would be better situated in another service. Thus you conceded to his leaving your personal service. Unfortunately, shortly after leaving, he found that he had lost his enthusiasm for spiritual life. Consequently he gave up his service to ISKCON, whereas by previously doing what you had really wanted, he was able to continue in devotional service. This proves the inconceivable power of your mercy. Without it we are nothing.

This reminds me of the story of a personal servant of yours who one day wanted to distribute books, although you were dependent on his regular services. When asked if he could go out, you told him he could go, but without your blessings. So what is the use of anything we do, even if it appears to be the topmost spiritual activity, if we don’t have your blessings?

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, may we always remember your vivid example of trying to do the impossible simply out of love for your spiritual master. With this example in front of us, I think we will be able to cross mountains even though we are lame, speak poetry even though we are dumb, and see stars in the sky even though we are blind. If we learn how to love you, then anything can be achieved.

On the occasion of ISKCON’s twenty-fifth anniversary we baked a cake and decorated it as a world map, placing a flag wherever there was an ISKCON center. Then we cut out the section with Mauritius on it and offered it to you. We hope that some day we can offer you the real thing.

Aspiring to be your servants, we remain  
The devotees and congregation of Hare Krishna Land, Phoenix, Mauritius.

## Heidelberg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Just as mother earth will always supply food, the waves of the ocean will constantly hit the shore, the wind will always blow over the fields, the sun will constantly turn in its orbit, the cooling moon will always spread its light, and eternal time will change everything in this world, without ever stopping—in the same way, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, your glories will be praised forever, without end.

Your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā day is also eternal, like you.

Where is the beginning of your preaching, where is the middle, and where is the end? In which worlds have you been yesterday, where are you now, and where will you be tomorrow?

The attempt to describe your activities in terms of time and space is foolish.

Your appearance and disappearance in this world are similar to the appearance and disappearance of the sun. The sun is always shining, either in India or in America. Just as the sun sets in this half of the world while at the same time rising in the other, so when you leave this world you begin your preaching in another world at the same time.

*Satataṁ kīrtayanto mām … nitya-yukta upāsate*. Without interruption you praise the glories of the Lord and unendingly worship Him with great devotion.

This is your eternal *līlā,* your never-ending pastime, which you enact again and again for the pleasure of your beloved Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Unfortunately, it seems that you have finished your pastime on this planet. The sun of your glories seems to have disappeared from our sight, and you have left us alone, back in the dark shadow of hoplessness.

But that is not so. One can always stay in the bright light of the sun when one travels with the sun. To stand still means that very soon one will be surrounded by darkness.

In a similar way, we can stay in the shining light of your association when we travel on with you—in other words, when we follow your instructions and preach.

Preaching—that is what you have always done, what you always do, and what you always will do.

Preaching—that is what you ask of us, and if we do it you will never disappear from our sight and we will never be in the shadow of illusion.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the strength and the inspiration to serve you always in your eternal pastime.

Your humble servants in Heidelberg, Germany.  
(written by Hṛdaya-Govinda dāsa)

## Hong Kong

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. And please accept this poem as our offering on this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You are the most merciful and benevolent,  
Having preached Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

From your teachings we have learned  
That the soul of the living entities,  
Though free by nature,  
Is now encaged in material bodies  
Because of his rebellious nature  
Of forgetfulness and envy of Kṛṣṇa’s glories.

Yet Kṛṣṇa consciousness is so nice  
That despite our having bodies  
Foreign to the Vedic land and culture,  
We can still perform our activities  
For guru, Kṛṣṇa, and Gaurāṅga.

Now life is more than a struggle and competition.  
Before it was a struggle to exist.  
Now life is a competition to beseech your mercy  
And a struggle to resist sense gratification—  
a struggle to find the spiritual vision to offer service,  
and find the most suitable service one can render.

Sense gratification is temporary,  
Resulting in miseries—  
Of this we have experience lifetimes long.  
Devotional service is permanent  
and joyfully performed—  
Of this we learn in ISKCON all along.

Recipients of your mercy and benevolence,  
Spiritual offspring we are,  
Including newborn grandsons and granddaughters in China,  
Chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa and dancing.  
This is all due to your glories,  
For you have preached Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

Your servants in Hong Kong.

## Honolulu

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most fallen obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

It is the greatest miracle how so many unworthy conditioned souls of this Kali-yuga are receiving Lord Kṛṣṇa’s mercy and being allowed to take part in Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. It is simply your causeless mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the one that broke open this storehouse of love and brought it to the West. You took this great risk and received all of the Lord’s mercy. We will never understand how dear you are to Lord Kṛṣṇa. We were all rotting in this horrible material world, with no knowledge of the goal of life. Simply suffering in the name of so-called happiness. We will never be able to repay you.

This International Society for Krishna Consciousness is your movement. You are the founder-*ācārya* as well as maintainer and eternal guide. By your mercy this movement will continue. You have given us this complete society to live in, and all we have to do is live by the “house rules” and spread this mission cooperatively. Because of our independent nature and false egos, this is sometimes hard to do.

Recently here in your Honolulu temple we have gone through some tough times, but by your mercy and the efforts of your sincere devotees we have always come out victorious. You said that this movement could not be stopped from the outside but only from within. And we are seeing that this is true. Our biggest problems have come from disciples who, in the name of serving, have created difficulties for the devotees living in your ISKCON temples. One such incident has reduced the temple’s income by half. Although we were able to squeeze by financially, we were still in anxiety because we were not able to send any money to your book fund. In desperation, we tried to have a big Christmas Marathon and made a promise to the BBT of $8,000, not knowing how it was going to come out. Then on almost the last day of the marathon we received a surprise donation. The next day we sent a check to the BBT for $36,000, coming in second in North America. We were behind New Dwārakā by only a few hundred dollars.

Actually these problems that arise from time to time are a blessing in disguise, because without them we would only become complacent. Our preaching is increasing with our newly opened restaurant in the temple. Many new people are coming and are receiving *prasādam*. Some devotees say the temple has never been better. This is only by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Thank you for saving us from hurtling down the merry road to hell and for giving our lives meaning. Please always allow me to render you some little service. Do not kick me away.

Some devotees say that I have no connection with you because I was not personally initiated by you and my initiating spiritual master has left the movement. But I feel that it is only by your mercy upon me that I am able to continue with my devotional service.

My closing words are a prayer of desperation to please always help us here in Honolulu. Material sense gratification is always tempting us, so we need your mercy to help us stay strong spiritually and preach vigorously, just as you did. This is the only way in which we could try to repay you for the wonderful miracle you have bestowed upon us.

Your servants in New Navadvīpa Dhāma, Honolulu, Hawaii.

## Inisrath Island

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious day of the appearance of our Param Guru, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, we glorify you…

Who like the royal swan did descend to the dark cesspool of Kali-yuga to teach the black crows of this age,

Who, glowing like molten gold, did light up the dense darkness of this age,

Who, overflowing with pure love for Śrī Govinda, did melt the rocklike hearts of this age,

Who with unlimited humility did attract the ruffians of this age,

Who with waves of ecstasy and compassion did destroy the miseries of this age,

Who with unflinching faith did convince the sceptics of this age,

Who with supreme intelligence did destroy the impersonalists of this age,

Who’s coming saved the people of this age,

Who’s expert management did assure the continuation of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement through this age,

Who is the personification of lord Gaurāṅga’s mercy for this age!

Who’s books are the only hope for this age!

Your servants at Inisrath Island, Northern Ireland.

## ISKCON Vedic Farm, Mauritius

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your appearance day in this material world. Again the time has come to rededicate ourselves to your service, to take shelter at your lotus feet.

You are the representative of Lord Nityānanda, and therefore you are making His mercy available to us fallen, conditioned souls. In one of your lectures you mentioned that the reality is “Nitāi-caraṇa, the lotus feet of Nityānanda Prabhu.” For us you are the extension of Lord Nityānanda’s mercy. If we follow your instructions sincerely, then we can avail ourselves of your mercy, even if sometimes we stumble and become victimized by the influence of the material energy. Therefore, for us the reality is Prabhupāda-caraṇa.

We have seen many of your disciples leave the service of your lotus feet, and as a result they have become entangled in Māyā’s painful embrace Now they always appear very unhappy and are suffering greatly from the repeated kicks of the modes of material nature. But those disciples who somehow or other remained under your shelter are blissful and are advancing toward the ultimate goal of life. This simply proves that Nitāi-caraṇa is the reality of life.

Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura sings, *vaiṣṇava ṭhākura, tomara kokura, boliyā janaba more:* “My dear Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura, kindly accept me as your dog. I am a dog already, but I am Māyā’s dog. I am not a Vaiṣṇava dog. So kindly accept me.”

A dog is supposed to have a master. If he doesn’t have a master his position is critical. At any time he may be vanquished. We pray to become your dog, Śrīla Prabhupāda, sitting at your feet wherever you are, tagging along wherever you go, watching your every move just to get some remnants of mercy. With great patience we shall stick to you, trying to satisfy you, because we are fed up being Māyā’s dog. We want to take shelter of Nitāi-caraṇa and Prabhupāda-caraṇa. Please kindly keep us under your shelter. We are very unhappy, but under the shelter of your lotus feet we shall be happy.

Your unqualified servants at the ISKCON Vedic Farm in Mauritius.

## Israel

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to Lord Caitanya’s divine *saṅkīrtana* movement!

In 1971 you wrote several letters to two of your disciples instructing them to preach in Israel. From these letters it is clear that you strongly desire the *saṅkīrtana* movement to spread in this “far-off land.” We have to admit, we haven’t been successful yet in achieving great accomplishments. However, by your mercy we are following some of your instructions, as indicated in these letters.

For example, you wrote, “Yes, you can set up a Guru-Gauranga altar, and if you can offer full arotika ceremony, that is nice. Pancha-Tattva are most merciful.” We have a Guru-Gaurāṅga altar, and we offer full *ārati* ceremony to our Gaura-Nitāi Deities.

You write, “You can stay in your apartment, have discourses, and hold kirtana, and whoever voluntarily may join, no one can object. Whoever comes, give him nice prasadam.” We regularly hold *Bhagavad-gītā* discourses in our apartment, hold *kīrtana,* and distribute *prasādam* to our visitors. Furthermore, we are operating a catering business and, by your mercy, we hope to distribute *prasādam* to many conditioned souls here.

You wrote, “Just try to give this Krsna philosophy to the intelligent class of men, and distribute our books as many as possible,” and “you have to introduce the matter of God scientifically, and that will be appreciated by any reasonable man.” We have started a lecturing program aimed at the intelligent class of men and women. The basic book taught and related to is your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*. So far we have spoken before over a thousand people in this program and have distributed dozens of *Bhagavad-gītās* to the audience. Beside that, for your pleasure we also have an Arab book distribution program.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are low, fallen, and devoid of any good qualification, and our service is humble. However, we beg to remain serving your ISKCON under any circumstance, without cessation.

Your servants in Amirim, Israel

## Karṇātaka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya* 10.137) it is said,

*prabhu kahe,—īśvara haya parama svatantra*  
*īśvera kṛpā nahe veda-paratantra*

**“Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu said, ‘Both the Supreme Personality of Godhead and the spiritual master, Īśvara Purī, are completely independent. Therefore the mercy of the Supreme Personality of Godhead and Īśvara Purī is not subject to any Vedic rules and regulations.’”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy is neither restricted by the jurisdiction of *varṇa* and *āśrama* nor guided by the codes of *dharma*. *Kalau śūdrā sambhavāḥ.* We had no *saṁskāras* or previous qualifications to be recipients of your causeless mercy. Only your mercy and affection sustain our lives and qualify us for service to Kṛṣṇa.

As our humble offering of affection, we are aspiring to put form and shape to your visions of self-sufficient, Vedic *varṇāśrama* implementation based in Kṛṣṇa conscious *kṛṣi-gorakṣa* principles. Please guide us in our attempts to hold fast to our determination, and please remove the obstacles being presented by Kṛṣṇa’s ever-devoted illusory energy. May your books be our basis and preaching your *vāṇī* be the essence of our community.

Your servants at the ISKCON Karṇātaka Farm Project, India.

## Kiev

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

All glories to your mission.

On this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day, first of all we would like to express our gratitude to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa for your appearance within this world. Kṛṣṇa Himself certainly empowered you to save the fallen souls of this Kali-yuga. But it was up to you to decide to shed the mercy of the pure devotee over one place or another. You have chosen this planet. Who can appreciate our great fortune!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, your greatness is proved by the fact that you created a new branch of Lord Caitanya’s tree. Your activities were wholly dedicated to fulfilling His desire to distribute the glories of the holy name to every town and village. Thank you for this. This ISKCON branch of the Caitanya tree is full of small branches, flowers, and leaves.

Another aspect of your greatness is that you could change men’s minds, turning them away from the service of Māyā and toward the service of Kṛṣṇa. We dare suppose that the reason you were able to do this was that your warm Vaiṣṇava heart was filled to the brim with love of Godhead. All glories to your heart, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Because of your pure Vaiṣṇava vision, you saw the whole world as full of happiness, for you lived with Kṛṣṇa—and what to speak of the future, when the seeds of Kṛṣṇa consciousness sown by you would sprout. We dare suppose you to have foreseen how this entire earth would be decorated by the temples of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. This vision, as well as the will of your Guru Mahārāja, led you forward. All glories to your attachment for the orders of your spiritual master and the clarity of your vision, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

We offer our humble obeisances unto your lotus feet and beg you always to inspire us to distribute books and preach wherever we are, even to an audience of one, or even if no one seems to be present. And let us never have the thought to appropriate for ourselves the fruits of our preaching, always remembering to whom they really belong. And when our *saṅkīrtana* army passes from one end of the earth to the other, sowing so many seeds left by you, please bless us that we may not forget you, the founder-*ācārya* of this movement, and that we may always chant your glories…

*Jaya* Prabhupāda!  
*Jaya* Prabhupāda!  
*Jaya* Prabhupāda!

Your devotees in Kiev, Ukraine.  
(written by Yajñapriya dāsa)

## Laguna Beach

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Trying to write something in praise of you, we feel ourselves very fallen and unfit. Yet we know that there is all good fortune awaiting us, your aspiring servants.

You said once that this service you have given us is all we know of love. We have practical experience that this is true. We thank you for this service, which is a tangible link to you.

We are trying to make the atmosphere in the temple more and more pure—to see that the devotees are nicely engaged in service and absorbed in learning the arts and philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are trying to learn how to encourage more people to engage in service, to accommodate all sincere souls. We would like to develop a college that could teach everything that we have learned from you.

We are praying to completely and correctly understand your instructions and impart them in full to the next generation. Thus they will be strong and happy in spiritual life, and the movement of Lord Caitanya will continue and increase in potency and purity.

Thank you for sending so many sincere devotees to help spread this movement. Their enthusiasm to preach, their desire to be attached to you, and their determination to progress in spiritual life are a great inspiration to us. We pray to live always in the association of your servants who love you so much.

We are happy to see the increased mood of cooperation among all of the devotees. As the family mood grows, we become more enlivened and everyone is attracted.

Gradually so many wonderful things are unfolding, more than we could have imagined. This is all your mercy, and we feel joyful to see you smiling at us at each turn.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, faith in you is the only real wealth in this world. From this faith, all realization comes. Without this there is only a vain search for truth and happiness, which can never take one beyond the mental plane to the lotus feet of the Lord.

We pray that we may not be separated from you by anything, internal or external. We pray that you will give us the intelligence to know how to please you, and we pray that you will give us the strength to give our whole life in your service.

Your aspiring servants of New Jhārikhaṇḍa Mandir, Laguna Beach, California.

## Lansing

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We were drowning in the ocean of material existence when you arrived as the captain on the lifeboat and rescued us. What can be said of your compassion in coming to this Western land? Never before has such mercy been bestowed. Even Lord Caitanya Himself gave up extensive preaching in Navadvīpa because of the opposition from the intellectuals who refused to accept His teachings. Instead he preferred to travel through South India, where the people were more pious and receptive. But you came to preach where the people were most impious.

“In a country where everyone is trained from childhood to indulge in sinful activities, who will accept such restrictions—no meat, fish, and eggs, no intoxicants, no gambling, and no illicit sex. These things are the life and soul of the Western people, I never imagined that even one person would accept it, but by the mercy of my spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa the thing has taken shape” (letter to Śivarāma dāsa, Oct. 7, 1975).

Because of your transcendental qualities and your love for us, we accepted. And since devotional service brings immediate relief, we were most grateful. We eagerly made our promises to keep our initiation vows. It was a small price to pay for the end result—*kṛṣṇa-prema*. Besides, you made it easy for us because you created a society of devotees, and as long as we remained in their blissful association and strictly followed the rules, sinful life stayed far away. And we had unlimited engagement in assisting you in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and in this way preventing ourselves from falling back into *māyā*.

“Do you have sufficient engagement there? Because if we are not full time engaged, the mind is free to do its fickle business of rejection and acceptance for sense gratification. Māyā will immediately enter—we do not even have to call her—the moment we’re not absorbed in Kṛṣṇa’s devotional service” (letter to Govinda dāsī, July 20, 1973).

Well, Śrīla Prabhupāda, it’s been nearly two decades and your ISKCON continues to provide incredible facility, but I realize that *kṛṣṇa-prema* is still a long way off. Back then I thought that it must be right around the corner. At least I have a little faith, but it seems as though the struggle to reach *anartha-nivṛtti* will never end. Are you disappointed that I am taking so long?

I become impatient with my slow advancement. I am too much indebted to you, so I stick with my vows, taken when I was much younger. And I am quite afraid of *māyā,* of that vast ocean of material existence where I wandered for so many lifetimes. But still, as if haunted by a ghost, my heart swells with material desires. Even by contemplating them I am tormented, and I lose my enthusiasm for a bright future in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. If I allow desires for sense gratification to remain, I become grief-stricken with the thought of becoming separated from you. Once I thought that I might have become disqualified to continue receiving your mercy. I felt as though I had a gaping wound in my heart. I couldn’t find you in your books. My power of concentration left me. I couldn’t hear you on your tapes. Your voice seemed foggy and distant, echoing but not penetrating my wounded heart anymore. So fearful of losing you, I could not trust the ground beneath my feet. I felt as though it might cave in, or that I might lose my balance at any moment, or that my knees might suddenly buckle. I shook all over. Even though hungry, I lost all appetite. Although thirsty, I didn’t care to drink. Though totally exhausted, I was unable to sleep. All I could do was cry to you and beg you to accept me and maintain me and not allow me to fall away from your shelter. Chanting sixteen rounds was my prayer for stability.

Well, I know now that you never really left me. I have to pay the price if I want to stay with you. And it’s still true that you are only asking a small token in return for what you are promising. Thank you for ruining my material life. I am depending on you and have no other shelter.

Your eternal servants in Lansing, Michigan.  
(written by Kamalinī-devī dāsī)

## Lirv, Ukraine

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Will you be so kind as to accept our humble bows? All glory to Śrī Śrī Guru-Gaurāṅga! The devotees of the Lirv *yātrā* are offering this homage to you. Certainly we aren’t able to describe in full our gratitude and love for your lotus feet. But, nevertheless, on this celebrated day of your appearance let us try to find the words which will express our feelings to you.

You appeared in this material world like a rising sun illuminating the road leading back to Kṛṣṇa. We are like the materialistic scientists: as it is difficult for them to understand the reason and the majesty of the sun, it is difficult for us, your naive, silly grandchildren, to understand and to praise the glory of your lotus feet.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thanks to your hard and selfless labor, we already have now in Ukrainian your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is, Śrī Īśopaniṣad,* and *Easy Journey to Other Planets,* and we are trying to carry this knowledge into every house, as Lord Caitanya asked us. We feel that our success in Kṛṣṇa consciousness depends on our selling this literature. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we aren’t worthy of your endless kindness. Owing to your direct participation, we have a center here in the city of Lirv, where we are trying to serve Lord Gaurāṅga. We rent a building where we conduct Sunday programs for guests.

You show your compassion for us by sending many sincere souls to help us spread Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, concluding this imperfect homage, we pray with great zeal to be always engaged in the service of your lotus feet.

Your servants in Lirv, Ukraine.

## Liverpool

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you help us to become cleansed in heart  
So in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and service we can make a start.  
Your mercy is so golden pure and free,  
But we sometimes ask for your help whilst we cling to a tree.  
Please help us to let go of every wooden false desire;  
Instead help us to hug spiritual desire trees and stop serving Māyā.  
Without your mercy we are lost in Māyā’s entrancing maze.  
As your purity is the force which cuts through the haze,  
Please help us to become true devotees in the core of our hearts.  
From your pure teachings and example let us never depart.

To us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very, very dear.  
Without you only mundane sounds would enter our ears.  
You are a transcendental touchstone, bestowing pure bhakti upon all.  
We have forgotten how to serve Lord Kṛṣṇa, but you help us to recall.  
You came to deliver us fallen, conditioned souls.  
Pleasing Kṛṣṇa and your spiritual master are your pure and selfless goals.

Our interest is to love the Lord, but we have been misled.  
You take away gross and subtle *māyā*, you give us Kṛṣṇa instead.  
Mundane sounds, wasted time, false ego and desire—  
You take them all away and burn them in the fire.  
You show us how to chant and be happy by your personal example.  
We only have to take the lid off the honey jar if we want a sample.

Your preaching against impersonalists’ teachings is so strong and bold.  
Your warmth and love for Kṛṣṇa show other philosophies to be cold.  
The benediction of invoking in us the sense of service each day  
Is the priceless gift for which we can never pay.  
You help us to come closer to Kṛṣṇa and His pastimes so sweet.  
May we always desire to be the servants of your servants’ lotus feet.

*Your servants in Liverpool, England.*  
(written by Cakra-nādī-devī dāsī)

## London

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

You’ve shown us the way,  
You’ve given us light,  
You’ve saved the whole world  
From a nasty plight

Now we must follow  
The blueprint you left.  
Then pure love of Kṛṣṇa  
No longer bereft.

Our heartfelt desire,  
Revealed by you:  
Distribute to others;  
Then all dreams come true.

God is a person  
With feelings sublime,  
An unlimited ocean  
Beyond all time.

In eighteen days Kurukṣetra  
Was finished,  
The culmination of the  
Pāṇḍavas’ penance.

But Kṛṣṇa was there  
Every step of the way,  
Providing them guidance,  
Day by day.

Arjuna had Kṛṣṇa,  
Someone might say,  
A tremendous advantage  
Compared with today.

But the Lord is still present,  
You inform us in *Gītā*,  
If only we had but the eyes  
To see Him.

Hearing and chanting,  
Recommended by sages;  
Then soon we’ll see Kṛṣṇa  
There in the pages.

*Bhagavān uvāca*—  
What proof do we need?  
Realization will come  
When your words we do heed.

You’re more than the  
Gaṅgā, Yamunā, and others,  
The shade of your feet  
Will save us from bother.

Please allow us to serve you,  
Free from desire,  
Thereby extinguishing  
Māyā’s great fire.

In this terrible desert  
Of Kali we play,  
Feigning happiness,  
Day after day.

For just one drop of  
Your mercy we pray.  
Then back to Goloka  
Forever to stay.

Your servants at the Soho temple in London, England.  
(written by Raghunātha dāsa dāsa)

## Longdenville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to Your Divine Grace. Please accept this little offering on this auspicious occasion of your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as one of your first disciples would say, “When we associate with devotees, we should share your gems.”

We aspirants have to take this very seriously, because every one of your actions, your writings, your lectures, the things you did to train up your disciples—your every step, Śrīla Prabhupāda—entail what being a perfect servant of Kṛṣṇa actually is. If we do anything differently, we would fail in our attempt to go back to Godhead.

Can we really understand the importance of your presence, Śrīla Prabhupāda?

If we can, then that is life’s perfection.

You gave everything so ideally.

Many would say that pure devotees of God are something of the past. But you are most contemporary for the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

And as you said, “The only prayer to make to Kṛṣṇa is ‘Dear Lord, please give me the strength to serve You.’”

So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, if our only prayer is to serve you and Kṛṣṇa, we will always be happy, our life will be successful, and we will go back home, back to Godhead. Words are too limited to glorify you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Your aspiring servants in Longdenville, Trinidad.

## Malaysia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please kindly accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious occasion of the anniversary of your appearance in this world, on behalf of your many grand-disciples here in Malaysia I beg you to please accept this offering and kindly shower your mercy on all, so that your mission may be successfully fulfilled.

Since the bona fide spiritual master in our line is the representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, the original compiler of the *Vedas,* this day is known as Vyāsa-pūjā. Who in our line has more perfectly represented Śrīla Vyāsadeva than you? In the form of the *Vedas,* Śrīla Vyāsa compiled all the truths pertaining to the Absolute, yet still the absolute reality, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, remained a mystery to all but a few. The first-class disciple must know the spiritual master’s real desire, and even without directly being asked, fulfill just that. Śrīla Vyāsadeva compiled the *Vedas* in order to alleviate the effects of this age and establish the Absolute Truth as Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. No one in this age has done more than you to reveal the real mission of Śrīla Vyāsadeva. Like it or not, the whole world now is aware of Kṛṣṇa. Even Vyāsadeva himself felt dissatisfied because of not having sufficiently glorified the Lord, so what to speak of the common man of this age? Vyāsa’s depression was removed by the mercy of Nārada, and similarly you are showering your mercy to remove the depression caused by speculation, materialism, atheism, impersonalism, and so on.

Still, one may find it difficult to accept and assimilate the teachings of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. That difficulty also can only be overcome by your grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda. “To receive the transcendental knowledge, we must completely surrender ourselves to the real *ācārya* in a spirit of ardent inquiry and service. Actual performance of service to the Absolute under the guidance of the *ācārya* is the only vehicle by which we can assimilate the transcendental knowledge.” Although the knowledge is there before us, still we cannot taste or understand a drop without your mercy. Like bees licking the outside of a bottle of honey, we will miss the point. The disciple must properly worship his spiritual master in order to gain that mercy.

What is that proper worship? In your own Vyāsa-pūjā offering to your spiritual master, you wrote, “But simply a festival of flowers and fruits does not constitute worship. The one who serves the message of the *guru* really worships him.… Practical devotion is the secret to understanding the *śāstras.*… For one who is not engaged in devotional service, reading all of the books is simply like licking the outside of the honey jar.”

Although to mundane vision you have departed, in actual fact you are still constantly with us. While writing this offering I feel you chastising me for my foolish ways. The memory of your instructions is removing the dirt from my mind, just as Kṛṣṇa’s instructions cleansed Arjuna’s mind. All problems escalate when we forget you and your instructions and think we are advanced enough to do it all on our own. Lord Brahmā prayed that in the course of performing his material activities he would not be deviated from the vibration of the Vedic hymns. We should also pray that we may not deviate from your instructions and be carried away by whim. In this way we can always have your company. “The disciple and the spiritual master are never separated, because the spiritual master always keeps company with the disciple as long as the disciple follows strictly the instructions of the spiritual master.”

It is our duty to protect the treasure you have given. “The *ācārya,* the authorized representative of the Supreme Lord, establishes these principles \[of Kṛṣṇa consciousness\], but when he disappears, things once again become disordered. The perfect disciples of the *ācārya* try to relieve the situation by sincerely following the instructions of the spiritual master.”

Please give us the shade of your lotus feet, and may we reside there forever. “Touching the lotus feet of the spiritual master means giving up one’s false prestige and unnecessarily puffed-up position in the material world.” Please guide us so that we may really be able to touch your lotus feet and not just sentimentally place some flowers on your *vyāsāsana,* say some flowery words, even physically stretch out our arms daily to “touch.” Please wake us up so we can realize the greatness of your ever-presence. Repeating your words, we pray constantly for your protection and the blessing to carry out our duty to you. This is the only prayer that will satisfy us: “Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the strength to serve you.”

Your aspiring servants in Malaysia.

## Manaus

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our attempt to glorify you is always short of something, since you are such an exalted personality.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are really very special; otherwise how could you have done so much for Kṛṣṇa in places where so many others failed?

Your books, which are a manifestation of your transcendental intelligence, are changing the face of the world, and everywhere we can see the results of distributing your books. According to your books, Kali-yuga is a very degraded age, in which people are almost at the level of animals. But now we can see so many people fighting for the environment, for vegetarianism, for animal rights, and for many other causes. All this is due to your preaching.

On the supreme order of the Lord, you left the spiritual world so that you could save the conditioned souls of the whole material world and even of the whole universe. Your ISKCON movement is making a revolution in the world. Even the people from India are impressed by this movement—by the high standard of Deity worship, by the preaching, by the seriousness of the devotees, etc. And all this is due to your purity.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the whole world is indebted to you, and you are so merciful that you even taught us how to try to repay this debt—by distributing your books!

Your servants in Manaus, Brazil.

## Manchester

**Were We Ever with Him?**

Wonder a time when barely few did know,  
whose glories are renewed this hallowed day,  
for man marks greatness grudgingly and slow,  
with hardened heart that hardly melts away.  
O vision now, that you had graced me then,  
to save long sojourn in the world of men.

See the pilgrims’ line all but unbounded,  
that swells and surges onwards to great halls;  
hear in mighty unison resounded,  
his name as echoes out from marble walls.  
O hear our call from where you ever dwell,  
as first you heard that plaintive call from hell.

Dome on dome, down to a distant skyline;  
a city built upon his bold behest.  
Now gathered up to its most sacred shrine,  
remembering this day a birth so blessed.  
O master, you have opened wide our eyes,  
and in your wisdom you have made us wise.

One law to hold one people, one in creed;  
profoundest, yet the poorest mind perceives;  
with each sure word from all illusion freed,  
as when was first from absolute received.  
O lord, an age ago we read and saw  
deep truth that grows still deeper evermore.

What dreams of one forever free from lust  
shall not in substance soon be manifest?  
As here today much more than dust on dust,  
the evidence of purest love expressed.  
O Prabhupāda, what offering will be  
beyond that which you gave us causelessly?

What limit shall there be to love revealing,  
but that of our deliberate design?  
Age of agony and hatred healing,  
sweeping sea of sentiments divine.  
O lord and master, doubts alone denied  
us vision of this thousand-year divide.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine self and your divine mission, ISKCON.

In the age of quarrel it is hard to find even two people who will completely agree upon anything. But somehow, even amidst such a difficult age, there exists a society that is based upon a single underlying agreement: we all agree that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the *ācārya* and the final authority in all spiritual matters. Surely this is wonderful. It points to the certainty that you are the fully empowered representative of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. The evidence is undeniable.

Of course, the problem now is how to build on that basic agreement. No doubt the disciples of your own Guru Mahārāja will all agree that he was also the empowered *ācārya,* which he surely was. But they have not been able to build an organized society upon that agreement. Faced with this difficult challenge, the tendency for division and separatism is seen all too starkly in our hearts.

But because it is your dearest desire, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we must somehow make it happen. Ultimately, only love for you will make it possible for us to work together. But love means sacrifice. It means a selfless endeavor to satisfy the beloved. We have to ask ourselves the fundamental question: Do I really want to act only for your pleasure? Do I really believe that acting in this way will make me happy, and conversely, acting any other way will not?

Alas, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our answers to those questions are now not in the affirmative. But we are hoping that, if nothing else, our lamenting that fact will somehow attract your mercy and you will give us the strength to change. By constantly reaffirming our faith in you and by meditating deeply on your words to us, we feel there is real cause for optimism.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are *jagat-guru,* the answer to countless heartfelt prayers. You are divine, unfathomable, ever present and ever our well-wisher. You are our only hope of salvation, and we fall helplessly before you today. Please be kind upon us.

Your humble servants in Manchester, England.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa Dharma dāsa)

## Māyāpura

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

To Śrīla Prabhupāda, the life force of the residents of Śrī Māyāpura Chandrodaya Mandir and the embodiment of Gaura-śakti, the potency of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Falling at the feet of Śrīla Raghunātha dāsa Gosvāmī and Śrīla Raghunandana Ṭhākura, we offer to our beloved *ācārya* these eight prayers of glorification:

May Śrīla Prabhupāda, the moon amongst all devotees, who is the king of preachers, appear splendidly in our hearts. His charming, graceful form is as effulgent as gold, and he enchants the minds of all. Sitting on a jeweled seat at the lotus feet of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and His devotees in Śrī Māyāpura, he is absorbed in the mellows of service to Śrī Rādhikā and Mādhava in the groves of Vraja *dhāma.*

May Śrīla Prabhupāda, the moon amongst all devotees, who is the king of preachers, appear splendidly in our hearts. His smile is radiant, and he is both charming and affectionate to all. Chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra* in a voice choked with emotion, he has captured the minds of the people of this world. Beautifully dressed in shimmering saffron garments, he has made the entire country of Bengal famous by his appearance.

May Śrīla Prabhupāda, the moon amongst all devotees, who is the king of preachers, appear splendidly in our hearts. As beautiful as fully blossomed lotus flowers, his loving, warm, and restlessly moving eyes are like pools of nectar, moistened with pure love for Kṛṣṇa. His character and pastimes are very sweet, and his soft lotus feet destroy the sorrows of this world.

May Śrīla Prabhupāda, the moon amongst all devotees, who is the king of preachers, appear splendidly in our hearts. He is the sole maintainer of all the devotees and the destroyer of all impurities born of the Age of Kali. He removes the sufferings of the poor conditioned souls and protects them from the dungeon of material existence. He grants them the unlimited transcendental bliss of the *saṅkīrtana* movement, which glorifies the chanting of the Lord’s holy names.

May Śrīla Prabhupāda, the moon amongst all devotees, who is the king of preachers, appear splendidly in our hearts. He is the crest-jewel of *sannyāsīs,* and by his pristine character he has re-established the principles of religion in the dark Age of Kali. He is the lion of the *saṅkīrtana* movement, destroying the elephantine vices in the hearts of the devotees. Appearing like Varāhadeva, he has picked up this earth from its fallen state and set its inhabitants dancing in waves of *kṛṣṇa-prema.*

May Śrīla Prabhupāda, the moon amongst all devotees, who is the king of preachers, appear splendidly in our hearts. He is the best of the devotees of Śrī Jagannātha Deva, and by his love he has attracted the Lord to appear in countless *tīrthas* around the world. Raising his arms in ecstasy and chanting the holy names in a voice like thunder, his eyes raining tears like a rain cloud, he beseeched the Lord to return to Vṛndāvana and enliven the comatose residents there. And by inaugurating countless Rathayātrā festivals, he has given new life to the comatose souls of the world, bringing a smile to everyone’s face.

May Śrīla Prabhupāda, the moon amongst all devotees, who is the king of preachers, appear splendidly in our hearts. By revealing the glories of Navadvīpa *dhāma* and building a magnificent city here, he has opened the floodgates of *gaurāṅga-prema* and caused all to drink deeply of the nectar. Chanting and roaring the holy names, overcome with ecstatic laughter, and rolling in the dust in all humility, his followers circumambulate the *dhāma* on *parikrama* in the month of Phālguṇa. By encouraging such *parikrama,* both he and Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura have blessed the world with certain liberation and attainment of the precious jewel of love of Kṛṣṇa.

May Śrīla Prabhupāda, the moon amongst all devotees, who is the king of preachers, appear splendidly in our hearts. He is like a golden mountain scattering the jewels of pure love of God. Out of his great mercy he wandered on the earth planet. By instructing the world in the chanting of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s holy names and by flooding the world with transcendental books, he has inundated every country with rivers of pure love of Kṛṣṇa. By the expansion of his fame, the residents of the entire world will become liberated.

May Śrīla Prabhupāda, the moon amongst all devotees, who is the king of preachers, appear splendidly in our hearts and also give us residence in his heart. He is our life and soul.

The residents of Śrī Māyāpura Chandrodaya Mandir.

## Melbourne

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our obeisances. We fall at your feet and beg for your mercy.

It is the duty of the disciple to glorify the spiritual master, the representative of Vyāsadeva, on his appearance day, the celebration of Vyāsa-pūjā. On that day the disciples remember the qualities and pastimes of their spiritual guide and master and make vows to further dedicate their lives to his service.

I remember the first opportunity I got to offer my homage on Vyāsa-pūjā day. It was my first weekend visit to the temple. I had been asked to do the kitchen cleanup at midnight and had been up almost all of the Janmāṣṭamī night. I remember the amazing feeling of exhilaration when I completed all of my rounds after battling with sleep and fatigue, eventually overcoming them by forcing my attention on the holy name. The pleasure I felt from taking out the garbage that morning I had never experienced before. I was amazed one could sleep so little and still function.

It was Sunday, and that afternoon at the feast all the devotees were reading their homages to you. Śikhi-puccha-dhara had asked me to write you an homage. I was very reluctant. All of my morning’s enthusiasm had simply evaporated. What was I going to say? After all, I had never seen you or met you, so what *could* I say? I really wasn’t qualified to speak.

Fortunately, Śikhi didn’t ask me to say anything. He knew I was a new recruit. I was able to relax and listen to the temple devotees reading their offerings to you. As I listened, my appreciation for Kṛṣṇa consciousness again increased. Each of your disciples offered his obeisances and then began to read. What followed filled me with awe. I was startled into seriousness. In his own way each offered his heart, re-dedicating himself to your mission, to the mission of Lord Caitanya, to the messages and desires of Lord Kṛṣṇa, to the sanctification of the planet and all people living within it. I thought to myself, “This is a serious business. What am I doing here?” I knew Kṛṣṇa consciousness was serious business, but this was *really* serious. Each of the devotees was prepared to give up his life for his spiritual master, and I was going to have to be prepared to do the same too. From that day on I knew I had to be aware of my responsibilities as your disciple and follower.

You were always aware of your responsibilities to your spiritual master and his mission. On your first meeting you were encouraged to carry the message of the Lord to the conditioned souls living in the Western world—something you were not able to accomplish immediately but in which you were eventually to excel, a testimony to the strength imbued in one who has strong faith.

*yasya deve parā bhaktir yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

**“Only unto those great souls who have implicit faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the imports of Vedic knowledge automatically revealed” (*****Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad*** **6.38).**

The test of a spiritual teacher or an *ācārya* is how much he can instill that same faith and determination in the hearts of his followers. We have witnessed that same degree of faith in your followers who have also adopted the ways of spiritual life, who have understood the importance of your mission and are working combinedly to carry the message of Lord Caitanya to all corners just as you desired and personally endeavored to achieve throughout your life. When I hear the devotees’ offerings on days like today, I appreciate the success of your mission. I appreciate that it will be successful; all we have to do is take part with the same seriousness and depth of conviction that you displayed.

In order for one’s preaching to be successful, one has to be a dedicated follower. A dedicated follower means one who can practically act on the instructions received from the spiritual master. If we don’t practice, how can we preach? It is the Lord’s desire that we spread his message, and the sincere follower takes up the Lord’s mission to preach. By your example you have encouraged us to take up the Lord’s mission, and now many thousands of followers are preaching the same message that you did.

We pray we may stay in the ranks of such devotees by strictly following your instructions. We pray we may always remember the Lord’s purpose and your dedication in sticking to the satisfaction of the Lord’s desire. By remembering you on days like today in the association of your dedicated followers, we also develop the same qualities. We beg to remain servants of your servants.

Your servants in Melbourne, Australia.

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

As we most joyfully celebrate this blessed occasion of your appearance in this world, we naturally search our hearts to remember and appreciate your truly exalted position.

In the Third Canto of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* you have explained that the Supreme Lord can appear from anywhere and in any form to perform His auspicious, transcendental pastimes. Sometimes He accepts a particular devotee as his father or mother, as Lord Kapiladeva accepted Kardama Muni and Mother Devahūti. Sometimes the Lord appears in uncommon forms and unusual ways, as Lord Varāha appeared from the nostril of Lord Brahmā and as Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva appeared from the palace pillar of Hiraṇyakaśipu. Similarly, the eternal Supreme Lord, in the form of His holy names, manifested as ISKCON, has emanated from you. It may appear to mundane vision that you have created this wonderful Hare Kṛṣṇa movement by ordinary managerial expertise, but the fact is that because of your pure devotion, the Lord has chosen to manifest Himself through you in the form of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

What is the proof of the divine nature of this movement? The proof is that because of this movement the world is changing in many ways that are diametrically opposed to the general trend of this age. In Kali-yuga meat-eating, intoxication, illicit sex, abortions, and gambling are accepted as normal. To live in opposition to these principles is almost impossible. Still, we see that movements against these degraded principles are gaining strength year after year. We also see that entire powerful communist governments are crumbling and that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is spreading in those countries like wildfire. These changes are not coming because of any political maneuvers, changes in the economy, or planning commissions. They are directly caused by this *saṅkīrtana* movement. Without this *saṅkīrtana* movement, all kinds of man-made arrangements to improve the world will ultimately only degrade it. Lord Caitanya’s mission was to destroy the demonic mentality. Similarly, this Hare Kṛṣṇa movement is significantly changing world history by gradually destroying the demonic mentality of the world.

You are so exalted, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that Lord Kṛṣṇa has allowed you to “give birth” to this ISKCON movement, which is fully an incarnation of God. No one could create this movement with any amount of mundane managerial expertise or money. This movement is completely spiritual, and there is no limit to its auspicious influence. You have given the world a wonderful gift in the form of this Hare Kṛṣṇa movement. Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your humble servants in Miami, Florida.  
(written by Dharma dāsa)

## Minsk

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Our center is yet very young, but thanks to your causeless mercy we are developing it and helping others accept Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. Now more and more new devotees are coming to your movement, and we know that this makes you glad.

We get the most pleasure by distributing your books to the ignorant people of this Age of Kali. It is not very easy, but all the same we are very thankful to you for giving us this service. You said, “Distribute books! Distribute books! Distribute books!” And we have made this our life. We know that this is the only way for us to please you.

Your most humble servants in Minsk, Ukraine.

## Moscow

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our beloved spiritual master,

Lord Ananta Śeṣa, with His thousands of mouths, has been recounting the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa from time immemorial, but still He can’t come to the end of those glories. Similarly, who can ever come to the end of your glories? What was done by you single-handedly is unfathomable. You gave us everything we need to perfect our lives. And you keep on giving us everything we need to reach perfection. You do this by giving us spiritual strength through your wonderful books.

It is said that a son brings glory to his father. You have so many great sons (and daughters) who are glorifying you all over the world. And in our country you have so many grandchildren connecting thousands and thousands of people to you by distributing your transcendental literature. For your pleasure we offer you the results of the 1992 Prabhupāda Marathon: about 100,000 big and mahā-big books.

And now your books are being distributed in big amounts. We see how it works. We see the dawn of a genuine Great Russian Revolution as part and parcel of the revolution in the hearts of all human beings. And we pray to you, the most intimate associate of the Lord, to please bestow your mercy upon us so that we’ll be able to serve you as much as our capability allows and even more, remaining eternally in the *saṅkīrtana* movement, so powerfully propagated by you.

Your devotees in Moscow, Russia.

## Munich

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We want to offer our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. Please accept them.

You left your beloved Vṛndāvana, where you were writing your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* commentaries under the direct guidance and inspiration of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, and on a boat you came across the ocean to America. At the age of 69, when most people have retired, you tolerated the difficult conditions on the boat for one and a half months, through seasickness and heart attacks. In America you tolerated so many difficult conditions. You had no friends, no money or facilities, and you lived in the Bowery, the most degraded part of New York. But still you went on with determination, tolerating all this. At 26 Second Avenue you would cook the Sunday feast alone and serve the guests, and afterwards you would sometimes have to wash the pots yourself as well.

Thus you displayed great tolerance in carrying out your preaching activities because of another of your great, outstanding qualities: mercy. And it’s because of your mercy that ISKCON is here today.

The Hare Kṛṣṇa movement has appeared because you were the perfect example of tolerance and mercy. Nobody actually came to the West and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness before you. Had you not come, none of us would be here today. We would still be rotting in the material world, performing obnoxious activities. Therefore we would like to take this opportunity to express our gratitude, glorify you, and offer our obeisances again and again.

Here in Munich we try to share in your mood for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world. Your mood is simple. Once in Africa, when you were working on the first part of the Seventh Canto, one devotee remarked that you always seemed to be absorbed in the mood of Prahlāda Mahārāja, and you replied, “Yes, I am always thinking of how to save these fools and rascals.” Your mood is the mood of the preacher who is always trying to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others; traveling from town to town, village to village, distributing transcendental literature . . . This is what you want done. Our perfection is to simply enter into that mood and always be thinking of how to help others advance in spiritual life.

You were always absorbed in Kṛṣṇa’s philosophy, His words, the preaching. Everything you saw would remind you of some philosophical point which you could explain to your disciples to expand their consciousness and understanding of Kṛṣṇa. People become impressed when they see such saintly qualities and behavior in someone. We can develop a reflection of your good qualities by following your example and preaching. Thus people will think favorably about the devotees of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and make advancement. Sometimes on morning walks you would say “Good morning” to people. That was just so they’d think favorably of you and start to advance in spiritual life. If someone can be so much benefited just by saying “Good morning” to you, then what to speak of one who reads and appreciates your books? When we see people becoming happy when they receive your books, it makes us feel so good at heart that we just want to continue with this activity more and more. This way the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement can expand and grow just like a flowering plant. By following your instructions, we are watering this plant, and automatically it grows.

We owe our lives to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Therefore we want to surrender our lives to you completely. Thus we can satisfy all the previous *ācāryas* and Kṛṣṇa. There is nothing more to be gained in life than this. By satisfying you, we’ll be satisfied and attain perfection. Even though we may be your grand-disciples, if we satisfy you through our own spiritual master, then he’ll be satisfied as well. It doesn’t matter if one is first, second, tenth, or 100th generation down the line, everybody in this movement has a connection to you, for you are the *sampradāya-ācārya*. You are the center of ISKCON. You are its founder and foundation. And our society will remain established in this way so that we can glorify you forever. Therefore we make your books the center and the foundation of this society.

You said, “I am living forever in these books.” And you said that they were not only for distributing but for reading also. So if we are always learning the philosophy from your books, we will be in your association, and thus we will become expert in distributing your books to others. Then we cannot fail to be connected with you and to keep you always in the center of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. This is our desire. Please give us your mercy so that we may fulfill it.

Your humble servants in Munich, Germany.

## Murāri Sevaka

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gauracandra, for ISKCON, for all the nice devotees you created, for reminding us of what we had forgotten, for teaching Vaiṣṇavism in the West, for helping us find our true eternal identity, for *kīrtana,* for defeating our atheism, for your books, for your kindness, patience, and appreciation. Thank you for your affection, commitment, and connection. Thank you for your sincerity, honesty, and compassion. Thank you for showing us the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, for Deity worship, sixteen rounds, and *kṛṣṇa-prasādam.* Thank your for our temple. Thank you for our lives.

Your humble servants at the Murāri Sevaka Farm, Mulberry, Tennessee.

## Nagpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,
