Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, who are the friend of the poor and the savior of the fallen.

We have a confession to make. We have been preaching in Nagpur for almost eight years but we have not achieved the degree of success that we have desired. We have not made many *bhaktas* or distributed many books, and *prasādam* distribution has been lacking. But it does not seem to be the fault of the people of Nagpur. They are friendly and pious and quite favorable. It seems that the fault lies with us. Unfortunately, just as the material scientists cannot conceive that it is their lack of devotion that makes them unsuccessful in understanding God, it is also inconceivable to our puffed-up selves that it is due to our lack of devotion that we are unsuccessful in our preaching attempts. We are always trying to place the blame on somebody else.

So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, this is our position. We ourselves are blind, yet we are trying to open the eyes of others. Yet we know that if we are simply hypocrites our efforts will be completely ineffectual. So please wake us up, smash us, crush us, do whatever it takes to make us serious about Kṛṣṇa consciousness. If sincerity is the qualification for having you answer our prayers, then we are in trouble, because we have none.

We are simply hoping that because we speak your holy name that we will catch your attention. Please prove to the world that you really are the topmost *yogī* and transform this little so-far-unsuccessful preaching center into a dynamic preaching machine manned by surrendered servants.

Waiting for your mercy, we remain  
Your aspiring servants in Nagpur, India.

## Nava-jiyaḍa-nṛsiṁha-kṣetra

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Throwing ourselves at the dust of your lotus feet, we beg again and again for the opportunity to serve you and your ISKCON mission.

You once wrote to a disciple, “Your nice service attitude is most encouraging to me,” and you expressed your satisfaction at his sincere service in worshiping the Deity. In another letter you encouraged a disciple to worship the Deity, quoting from the *Gītā:* “This is the practical application to what we find stated in the *Bhagavad-gītā: man-mana bhava mad-bhakto mad-yaji mam namaskuru*. We do not speculate on what Krishna says, but we follow whatever He says implicitly. I also follow this process.… The Deity is nondifferent from Krishna Himself. This we have to understand.”

In your temples throughout the world, as the Deity curtain opens every morning to the sound of *govindam ādi-puruṣam,* we are blessed by the sight of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, and Śrī Śrī Jagannātha-Subhadrā-Balarāma. These are YOUR Lords, whom you have dared to request to appear in such remote places of the world as New York, Melbourne, and the Bavarian Forest, to receive the awkward and bumbling attempts at worship performed by your disciples.

These, YOUR Lords, smile radiantly at all of us. Despite our failings, because we try sincerely to follow your instructions on how to worship Them, They are patient with us.

As we sing *venuṁ kvanantam …* we cannot forget how, in your personal presence before the Deities every morning, the spiritual world became a tangible fact. There was indeed “no question of separation” between yourself and Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa’s charming countenance seemed even more charming in your presence; His smile seemed brighter. As with everything you did, you worshiped the Deities perfectly. Even your simplest gesture of worship—such as your “bells” ceremony at 26 Second Avenue or your offering of incense to the Pañca-tattva—outshone the most elaborate *arcanā* ceremonies performed in the previous *yuga*. With every curling circle of incense and soft murmur of the *mangalācaraṇa* prayers, you offered everything in full, humble surrender to the Lord.

We pray that we may be of some assistance in your impeccable worship of Their Lordships. Please point out our offenses in Deity worship so that we may not be hindered in our progressive realization that our engagement in the *arcanā* process is your mercy on us, and that these, Your Lordships, are nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa Himself.

Your servants at Nava-jiyaḍa-nṛsiṁha-kṣetra.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa-kṣetra dāsa)

## Newcastle

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on this occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration.

Solitary figure  
on a sidewalk,  
old man from the East  
in New York.  
You came under instruction  
from your authority;  
a spiritual vision  
brought you westward  
on the sea.  
You made the perilous journey,  
only to find  
an ocean of darkness  
and the conditioned mind.

Twenty-six Second Avenue:  
there you made a start,  
planting seeds of *bhakti*  
in everybody’s heart.  
You fed them, you watered them,  
assisted from above,  
and like a good gardener  
nurtured them with love.

The message was simple:  
“You do not belong here;  
material life means  
anxiety and fear.  
Give up your sinful life;  
don’t waste any more time;  
surrender to Lord Kṛṣṇa;  
your life will be sublime.  
Just try to understand:  
serve the Lord in all you do;  
meditate upon His name;  
He will be pleased with you.  
You are not this body;  
this you should know”—  
words from your lotus mouth,  
seeds began to grow.

And so, as predicted  
by Caitanya Mahāprabhu,  
in every town and village  
temples flourished and grew.  
By your intense desire  
it became a success,  
the International Society  
for Krishna Consciousness.  
But all too soon you left us—  
the Lord called you  
to His side,  
mission accomplished,  
our savior and guide.  
You set the example  
for ordinary men.  
Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotee  
never takes birth again.

Your aspiring grand-disciples in Newcastle, England.

## New Dvārakā

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this, your auspicious appearance day, we, the devotees of New Dvārakā, offer our humblest obeisances at your lotus feet. We remember how happy you were when we were able to purchase this temple. Thereafter, with great determination, you strove to direct every aspect of this community to make it the world headquarters for your preaching mission. We remember how you desired that New Dvārakā should become so exemplary as to be the standard for the rest of the world in Deity worship, preaching, and book distribution. We are striving to maintain and even improve upon the standards set when you were personally here.

We are awed when we remember your infinite compassion upon the conditioned souls. Because the number of fallen souls is ever-increasing in this age of Kali, we therefore feel it of utmost importance to keep that mercy coming down. This can only be done by the profuse distribution of your books. Although we are spiritually lame and mentally deficient, and although the demons are constantly aligning themselves against us, we are determined that not only will book distribution go on, but that it will increase. At this time, we implore you with all earnestness to give us just one drop of your mercy so that we can actually accomplish this. With the deepest gratitude, we vow to push forward on all fronts to fulfill your goals and visions for New Dvārakā.

Your servants at New Dvārakā, Los Angeles, California.

## New Gokula

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*jīve sākṣāt nāhi tate guru caitya-rūpe*  
*śikṣā-guru haya kṛṣṇa mahānta svarūpe*

**“Since one cannot visually experience the presence of the Supersoul, He appears before us as a liberated devotee. Such a spiritual master is none other than Kṛṣṇa Himself” (Cc.** ***Ādi*** **1.58).**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

**Lamentation and Tears**

It is with great pain this year that I am attempting to write this Vyāsa-pūjā offering to you. Great pain because, like the other years, I haven’t made much progress. My heart is still too hard to melt, and knowing that Vaiṣṇava humbleness is what will cure me of the disease of material life, I cry day and night for a drop of your mercy so that I can in the near future become a true devotee of the Lord. Up until now I have been a cheater. I am hiding under the garb of a Vaiṣṇava devotee, but no good qualities are to be found in me. As Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda has sung in his Śaraṇāgati, the unhappiness of others makes me happy, and their success makes me unhappy.

My senses are still pushing (*vāco vegam, jihvā vegam, udara vegam*). My mind is still laughing at me (*manasa vegam*). When will I give up the false ego and surrender to your lotus feet? Where is the hope?

There Is Hope: Śaraṇāgati

*bhakata-sevā, parama-siddhi,*  
*prema-latikāra mūla*

The hope is there by serving the devotees. Perfection of love is obtained. This is what ISKCON is all about.

*mādhava-tithi, bhakti-jananī,*  
*jatane pālana kori*

The hope is there by observing with care holy days like Ekādaśī and Janmāṣṭamī, the mothers of devotion. Your gift to our lives.

*gaura āmāra, je-saba sthāne*  
*koralo bhramaṇa raṅge*

The hope is there by always visiting the *dhāma* in the company of ISKCON devotees. Your gift to our lives.

*gaura-bihita kīrtana-śuni’,*  
*ānande hṛdaya nāce*

The hope is there when there is constant absorption in Gaura *kīrtana*. Your gift to our lives.

**Prayer and Request**

O Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura, O ocean of mercy, be merciful to me, your servant, and purify me by the shade of your lotus feet. Your feet I humbly hold.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I have no words to express my desperation to obtain your mercy. But I am still imprisoned by *māyā*. I can see you, I can hear you, I can think of you, but I cannot touch you because of my lack of purity. So I beg all the Vaiṣṇavas, “I am holding onto your feet. Please drag me all the way to His Divine Grace. At least give me some service, just anything, to keep me in line with your desires.”

“Chant . . . . . Chant . . . . . Chant . . . . . Constantly . . . . . Chant . . . . .  
“Don’t waste time . . . . . Don’t waste time.” Your voice echoes!

Preaching is going on forcefully all over the world according to your plans. Vaiṣṇavas from all walks of life are participating enthusiastically in the *mahā-yajña,* like the army of monkeys who worked so bravely to build the bridge over the ocean to reach Laṅka and destroy *māyā*. I, the spider, am pushing my bit in the hard back of Australia.

Question: Am I pushing, or am I hiding? New Gokula Farm, your unknown project. Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda residing and presiding. The temple room is rather empty for early-morning and evening *darśanas,* but Their Lordships are receiving from us the same help that Lord Rāma received from the spider: a few grains of sand. Sometimes we wonder why Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa would come here.

*Śaraṇāgati* is the answer. The Lord of Vraja is here, and by the strength of *guru* we can sing His glories. Complete surrender is the prayer. The shade of your lotus feet is the request.

*kṛṣṇa se tomāra, kṛṣṇa dite pāro*  
*tomāra śakati āche*

Kṛṣṇa is yours, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are able to give Him to us. Such is your power.

Here on the farm we have the hills, just like at Govardhana. We have the cows, like in Vṛndāvana forest. We have Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, just like at Vaṁśīvata. But we are deprived of *bhakti,* and we unfortunately see it all with mundane vision.

**Finally Rejoicing**

Yesterday was Janmāṣṭamī, the most ecstatic and memorable festival of the year. And today, on Nandotsava, you appear and bless us with your presence. Kṛṣṇa consciousness: a gift from you to one and all. We’ve all come to gather around you today, with little petty donations, because what you are giving us by appearing is not repayable by anyone—*janme janme prabhu sei*.

So let us spend the rest of our lives chanting your glories and those of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda by constantly engaging in devotional service.

*janame janame sabe pitā mātā pāya*  
*kṛṣṇe guru nāhi mile bhaja hari ei*

Everyone always gets a mother and a father. But one who gets the benedictions of Kṛṣṇa and Śrīla Prabhupāda can conquer the material energy and return back to Godhead by serving the Lord.

Begging to always stay in your service, we remain  
Your servants at the New Gokula Farm, in Millfield, Australia.  
(written by Yadunātha dāsa)

## New Goloka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances. All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda. On this auspicious day of your appearance, Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, we, your disciples, grand-disciples, and admirers, would like to rededicate ourselves to carefully following your instructions and carrying out your mission.

Every year we feel increased enthusiasm to know you more deeply, to understand your desires, and to realize the depth of your instructions. By reading your books, lectures, and letters and listening to your tapes, we are given the opportunity to awaken our own Kṛṣṇa consciousness and that of all those who come in contact with your movement.

Your transcendental sound vibration is the only remedy for the material infection that covers our consciousness. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us realize how the only shelter in this material world is your guidance and the association of your sincere followers. Please help us take shelter of Lord Kṛṣṇa and His holy names. We pray to you that you give us the determination and strength to give up our petty differences and to unite for your pleasure, so that we may actually spread this *saṅkīrtana* movement. Your personal example is so glorious that by meditating on your life and qualities, we become inspired to carry on in spiritual life.

Actually, it is your spiritual authority alone that is molding every action in our daily lives. You have taught us to be clean in mind and body, rise early, attend *maṇgala ārati,* study the scriptures, and honor *prasādam*. You have instructed us to worship the Deities, preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness, educate our children, and serve the Vaiṣṇavas. Yes, you have given a very large umbrella for all those who desire shelter from the downpour of Kali-yuga.

We hope that you are somehow pleased with our small attempts to serve you here in North Carolina. Please forgive us for our offenses and let us somehow or other remain engaged in your service eternally.

Your servants in the New Goloka Community, Hillsborough, North Carolina.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa-priyā-devī dāsī)

## New Kulīnagrāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances on this day of your divine appearance. All glories to you. All glories to Lord Caitanya’s glorious *saṅkīrtana* movement, which you so nobly represent.

I first truly heard of you through my spiritual master. Although Kṛṣṇa was affording me the opportunity to know of you through your books, the import of your appearance was more appreciated through his glorification of you. Your position as a specially empowered soul in this most degraded age has become more meaningful to all of us who are blessed to be in your association, and daily we see how much you have transformed all our lives.

I have also read in *Teachings of Lord Caitanya,* “Vaiṣṇavas, or devotees, are usually very compassionate upon conditioned souls. Without even being invited, a devotee will go from door to door to enlighten people and bring them out of the darkness of nescience by injecting knowledge of the living entity’s constitutional position as a servant of the Lord. Such devotees are empowered by the Lord to distribute devotional consciousness, or Kṛṣṇa consciousness, to the people in general. They are known as authorized spiritual masters, and it is by their mercy that a conditioned soul gets the seed of devotional service. This causeless mercy of the Supreme Personality of Godhead is first appreciated when one comes in touch with a bona fide spiritual master, who can bring the conditioned soul to the highest position of devotional life.”

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, exemplify all this, and your success is unparalleled, for you have accomplished much more than any other preacher of the modern age. But this was Kṛṣṇa’s plan. Indeed, your great success in preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness shows the effect of an intimate loving relationship between the Lord and His pure devotee. As Kṛṣṇa wanted Arjuna to be glorified, in like manner, He chose to glorify you.

You have imbued in your dedicated servants the potency to reclaim the conditioned souls, but at times we lose sight of this mission and fall prey to our ambitions. Yet you do not forsake us. You keep prodding us on so that we will carry on your mission. On this day of your appearance, we humbly beseech you to help us overcome all impediments so that we will mature into meaningful devotees and thus follow the principles of loving exchanges between Vaiṣṇavas. In this way will better appreciate the rare gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you have bestowed upon us.

Your loving servants at New Kulīnagrāma, Guyana.  
(written by Candrāvalī-devī dāsī)

## New Māyāpura

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you.

This year marks the end of long tribulation. Only by your grace could this situation in New Māyāpura come to an end. In the midst of so much trouble we needed a friend who could help us always remember the goal and never lose our determination to fight for your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in France. Now we have abandoned all false pride and try to satisfy you by following your instructions. It is forever the simplest and surest way to please you.

We have been through so much austerity—all our wealth has been taken away—but you remained our friend, giving us strength and detachment. With almost no place to live or a home of our own, we learned to take shelter in you. The constant presence of insecurity helped us when reading your books and struggling to become true in our vows.

Now that the situation has changed, let the desire once again rise in our hearts to go to all the villages and towns repeating your words and witnessing your mercy as you deliver the fallen souls.

Please do not allow us to make so many mistakes. It is so nice to be able to serve you by preaching and distributing your books. We pray that you will help us remain always pure. Whatever circumstances may come—peace or war, heat or cold—we want to keep fighting on, delivering your message as you asked us.

So on this day please hear our fervent prayer. Beyond this story of New Māyāpura, which appears dark, there is some love for you. Please guide us on the right path and keep us in the fight, and please keep this place away from Māyā’s glare.

Your servants in New Māyāpura, France.  
(written by Nṛsiṁhānanda dāsa)

## New Naimiṣāraṇya

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet.

It is our exceptionally good fortune that we have been blessed with the shelter of your lotus feet, so soothing in the midst of Kali-yuga. Kṛṣṇa is the property of His pure devotees, and His pure devotees can deliver Kṛṣṇa to whomever they please. We are all dull-headed offenders to the holy names, negligent in our practice of devotional service. Still, a glimmer of hope sustains us, for Your Divine Grace, the most ardent follower of Śrī Rūpa Gosvāmī, who has established within this material world the mission to fulfill the desire of Lord Caitanya, are continuously and vigorously pushing on this movement. Certainly the prophecy of Lord Caitanya that His holy names will spread all over the world has come true by Your Divine Grace’s persistent determination, even in the face of so many difficulties. Thus the lotus feet of all-benevolent Śrī Kṛṣṇa, long ago discarded by us, are being approached again.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our realizations of the vast ocean of devotional mellows are very meager indeed, but it is our earnest prayer that we remain tied with the rope of *bhakti* to the anchor of your lotus feet so that we do not sink into this material whirlpool again.

Your disciples and grand-disciples at your beloved New Naimiṣāraṇya Farm, Hyderabad, India.

## New Nandagram

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura is sometimes said to have brought the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement into the twentieth century by using modern technology to spread the ancient tradition of Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavism throughout India. Fulfilling his dream, and the inner desires of the previous *ācāryas,* you preached and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the entire world. You wanted everyone, in every little corner and nook of this planet, to get the opportunity to perform *harināma saṅkīrtana.*

You wrote to Madhudviṣa Swami in January, 1975, when we had a temple in Alexandria, Sydney: “Try to get some nice farm. That will be very good. If we can produce milk products and grain, there will be grand success.” Thus within your marvelous plan to drench all conditioned souls in the universe with the mercy rain of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu was the specific idea for establishing farming communities on this continent of Australia.

Here at New Nandagram, the devotees wish to be counted as your followers and servants. We feel blessed to be part of your mission. The practical work required to actually establish a self-sufficient Kṛṣṇa conscious rural community is massive enough to engage any number of willing, enthusiastic *jīvas* for many lifetimes. It’s awesome. We are just pioneers, and there is so much to do. On this auspicious day of your divine appearance in this world, we fall at your feet and beg for your mercy to dedicate our lives to fulfilling your desires.

The *Vedānta-sāra* states that a person whose head is on fire will naturally run to water. In the same way, one who burns from the flames of birth, death, old age, and disease in the holocaust of material existence must run to a genuine *guru* for relief, submit to him, and be ready to carry out his instructions.

Please therefore shine in the hearts of all of us, so by your grace we can establish a loving association of devotees, where all souls desirous of awakening Kṛṣṇa consciousness can live and work happily and peacefully and feel increasingly encouraged and inspired in their progressive march toward the Absolute Truth, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your humble servants at New Nandagram, Bambra, Australia.

## New Nāradavana

Dear Śrila Prabhupāda

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, which is under your infallible command in modern times!

You will hardly find such unqualified servants as ourselves. Nevertheless, somehow or other we want to cling to your lotus feet with all our strength. Just one thing we know: your cause is the most sublime and will enable the fortunate souls that get in touch with your mercy to experience the striking mercy of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. We are sure that we do not deserve at all to be counted amongst your servitors, who are like swans in a lake in which we, as frogs, are just mispronouncing the holy names.

Please be merciful to us so that someday we may become qualified to spread purely the glories of the holy name. Don’t kick us away, please. We have somehow taken shelter on your magic ship, protected by your sons.

Your naughty grandsons in New Nāradavana, Cochabamba, Costa Rica.

## New Orleans

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Please give us the strength to serve you purely. We pray for your blessings to help us follow the process of *sādhana-bhakti* as you have prescribed it for our benefit. We pray for you to help us chant sixteen rounds nicely and read your books daily. We pray that one blessed day we may become fully qualified members of the ecstatic *saṅkīrtana* preaching movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, which you have so kindly delivered to us. We were wallowing in ignorance, unmindful of our very souls, before the words emanating from your lotus mouth inspired us. We pray to remember this always, and thus to remain meek and humble servants of Your Divine Grace.

Please help us to chant, work, worship the *arca-vigraha* form of the Lord, and preach cooperatively, all in a spirit of love and trust. You told us so many times that this would please you.

In this way we pray to assist you in some tiny way in your all-blissful, all-successful mission of spreading the effulgent glories of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu to all the suffering conditioned souls enmeshed in the snares of this age of quarrel and hypocrisy.

Your servants in New Orleans, Louisiana.

## New Pānihāṭi (Atlanta)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this most auspicious day, we here at New Pānihāṭi feel most fortunate to be the unworthy recipients of your causeless mercy. Lacking sufficient wisdom to take shelter of your lotus feet, we pray that you please continue to bless us with your divine *vāṇī* and bestow upon us the strength to follow your instructions. We pray for your continued patience and ask you to forgive our non-cooperative, non-appreciative material disposition.

We pray that we, who have no qualification to serve you, will someday understand that one can please Kṛṣṇa only by pleasing His pure devotee. We pray that your fame and glory illuminate the darkness of ignorance that cover our character.

It is impossible to repay you, for you have given us the greatest of gifts—devotional service unto the Supreme. Humbly falling at your feet, we beg you to give us the mental fortitude and knowledge to follow your precious last instructions and to remain eternally at your service.

Your sons and daughters at New Pānihāṭi, Atlanta, Georgia.

## New Pānihāṭi (Guyana)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept our prostrated and humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Your appearance on this earthly planet has set a new era in motion, one that will see a revolution in consciousness for the mass populace. This revolution will be effected in the course of the next ten thousand years.

As your grandchildren, we have become very fortunate to have gotten this opportunity to become connected to you through your books and representatives. Already we have experienced such a drastic change in our consciousness and character that certainly we have no doubt about this process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which is the most sublime and effective means to eradicate our demoniac tendencies and thus situate us on the platform of pure love for the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Our only lamentation, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is that so often it seems that we lack the determination to endure the struggle for purification—to persevere amidst the many obstacles on the path of devotional service; and our intelligence notwithstanding, we become victims of material nature.

Our only hope, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is that through our sincere desires and aspirations you will give your causeless mercy to your sincere, struggling followers. Then we will get Lord Kṛṣṇa’s attention and protection. Indeed, such reciprocation can be experienced only by our offering sincere and constant prayers to you, acknowledging our insignificant and fallen position, being completely dependent upon you as our pre-eminent spiritual guide, and taking instructions from your bona fide representative.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious day of your appearance, we pray that through your divine, enlightening instructions we can ultimately become victorious in our spiritual pursuit.

We beg to remain  
Your humble servants at New Pānihāṭi, Berbice, Guyana.

## New Ramaṇ-reti

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete koriyā aikya, ar na koriho mane āśā.

“My only wish is to have my consciousness purified by the words emanating from his lotus mouth.” (*Śrī Guru-vandana,* Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura)

All glories to you, who are our only shelter, the brightest beacon in the darkness of the Age of Kali.

We are trying to accept your orders as our life and soul. As you have written in your Bhaktivedanta purports,

“Satisfaction of the self-realized spiritual master is the secret of advancement in spiritual life” (Bg. 4.34, purport).

By the efforts of your sincere followers, there is more hope now for the rest of us. Your personal guidance is becoming easier by the organization of your voluminous *vāṇī*. This, along with your ever-present mercy, is sufficient in itself to guide us past the obstacles that present themselves from time to time due to our “not being afraid of Māyā” and being “unable to co-operate with one another.”

Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura sings, *tā-sabāra pāda-reṇu mora pañca-grās*: “The dust of the lotus feet of the six *gosvāmīs* of Vṛndāvana is my five kinds of foodstuff.” We similarly pray that your words will be our only foodstuff and that we may take shelter in them until we become eligible to reside eternally as particles of dust at your lotus feet.

All success will be there when your word, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is the last word.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

We beg to remain  
Your aspiring servants at New Ramaṇ-reti, Alachua, Florida (“far from the birthplace of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu”).

## New Remuṇa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances at your lotus feet.

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatam*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross over the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.”** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

We see how strong Kali-yuga is outside the temple, and we also sense our own attraction for this Kali-yuga culture.

To counteract this you have given us ISKCON, with its good association, book distribution, farm communities, Nāma Hatta, and other variegated services to keep us engaged in Kṛṣṇa’s service.

Toronto is a very desirable place to serve Kṛṣṇa. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, were so bold as to rename our fair city New Remuṇa Dhāma. By your influence our large congregation has firm faith in the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. We are seeing that by distributing your books, the devotees have become very credible.

The devotees are very much in demand on high school campuses, in the homes of Indian families, and in various Nāma Hatta centers throughout Ontario. Even though we are getting some credit, we know that your mercy is all that we are made of. If you had not touched us, we would be small-time materialists struggling to make a living.

You once said to us, “Become great by serving the greatest.” We can see how by distributing books, by doing *harināma,* and by teaching kids in high schools how to do the swami step and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, we have become the center of attention. We are being honored because of you.

Yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the devotees accept you as the captain of the ship. You want us to preach—we must preach. You’ve given us exquisite Deities to worship—Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha—and we will worship Them eternally. You’ve told us to distribute books—we obey, whether it’s 20 degrees below or 90 degrees above. You want us to distribute *prasādam*—yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we will feed the homeless and bring Mahā plates to our members’ offices. You’ve told us not to offend devotees—yes, Gurudeva, we will go to their homes and make amends and encourage them.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are fallen, but we know that if we preach there is hope for us. Let us not rest on our laurels. Kali-yuga is advancing, so we cannot be static in our devotional service; we must be ecstatic.

Your servants at New Remuṇa, Toronto, Canada.

## New Tālavana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to your divine service!

We pray to be always engaged as your eternal servitors. Certainly if we displease you, Lord Kṛṣṇa will be displeased with us. Because we are serving you, we are serving Him, but as soon as we do not recognize you, we fall down into the darkness of material nature. Therefore please instruct us, order us, and allow us to serve you.

Your instructions, desires, and teachings are recorded in your divine books and tapes. So there is no need to speculate or dream in order to realize you. Your *vāṇī* is you, and your heart is your books. By following your teachings we are able to be fixed in pure goodness. Eternally satisfied, we feel, think, and want nothing other than more of your devotional service.

Please bless us to become good disciples. Help us not to shame you. Dismantle our false pride and let us drop in the shadow of your divine feet. It is by your grace that we have slightly revived from material conditioning. It is by your teachings that we are remembering our eternal constitutional position. It is by your pleasure that the Supreme Personality has given us a glimpse of Himself and His eternal abode.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Neither rich men, scholars, impersonalists, nor any other nondevotees could have delivered us. You, however, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are empowered by the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa to accomplish Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s divine mission. You have real qualifications. You are very dear to the Supreme Lord because of your character and desire. We are very fallen and full of faults, but because you desire to deliver those who take part in your missionary activities and teachings, Lord Gaurāṅga is helping us.

By the Supreme Lord’s arrangement you exhibited ideal human existence from birth to death, in all *varṇas* and *āśramas* of life. You are the instructing spiritual master for the entire human society. How lucky we are to have actually attempted to serve your teachings. How fortunate we are to be disciples of this world’s purest devotee. You are the chief theologian, the best communicator and educator, the most practical father, an ideal example of studentship and householder life, a fearless and all-knowing *sannyāsī*. You have set the example for all human beings to mold their lives properly.

In our time of mass communication and transportation, an ideal example of Kṛṣṇa conscious human life was essential. The Lord sent you, and we are so lucky to be hearing from you. Never before have so many had such an opportunity to hear the Absolute Truth presented so perfectly. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Thank you again and again. Please let us continue to act as insignificant disciples. Falling in the dust of your divinity, we beg for your mercy.

Your disciples and grand-disciples at New Tālavana, Mississippi.  
(written by Yogīndra-vandana dāsa)

## New York

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, founder /*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness! As the most graceful swanlike devotee of the Lord, he is unparalleled in his humility and total dependence upon the ever-worshipable Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and His eternal consort, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. Being the Lord’s empowered representative, he liberally bestows his blessings upon the lowborn, elevating them to the platform of pure devotional service.

His dream had been to go to New York, the world’s greatest city, to execute the mission of his Guru Mahārāja, and he made New York his home away from home. He was alone—a lone fighter—and so far from Vṛndāvana. Always feeling separation from the land of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes, Śrīla Prabhupāda was himself enacting a chapter of the Lord’s *saṅkīrtana-līlā,* a chapter in which he was the principal player.

Śrīla Prabhupāda was prepared to fight for Kṛṣṇa until the last breath, but he felt unfit to deliver the message of Lord Gaurāṅga. Only by the direct intervention of the Lord could the real purport of “Bhaktivedanta” be fulfilled. Śrīla Prabhupāda prayed and prayed, and his lone cry in the wilderness was heard. Having shown that his faith in the order of his spiritual master to introduce Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the English-speaking countries would not waver, even in the midst of the greatest adversity, he had attracted the attention of the object of his love, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, who, in turn, pleased the mind of Their pure devotee by sending thousands upon thousands of sincere followers to help him achieve his mission.

Because it was his will, the chanting of the holy name then spread throughout every village, town, city, nation, and continent like a tidal wave spreading its influence all over the world.

A high point of his preaching in foreign lands was to install Deities, and thus he invoked good fortune everywhere, like an expanding white lotus, driving impersonalism out of every nook and corner of the minds of his disciples. He brought the festival of Jagannātha to the West, to the most important street in the world—Fifth Avenue—and obtained a skyscraper building in midtown Manhattan to house Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda. Śrīla Prabhupāda placed the Deities in the care of his young disciples, all the while praying to Kṛṣṇa to enlighten them in the correct procedure of Deity worship. Similarly, when Śrīla Prabhupāda left the planet, he entrusted his entire movement to the care of his followers, praying to Kṛṣṇa that they be given the necessary intelligence to preserve the legacy he had passed on to them.

Dear Brothers! Take his outstretched hand and be guided in this grave responsibility. Grab on tightly to his lotus feet and know the joy of full surrender. He is the receptacle of Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mercy, and only by his powerful words can an utterly misguided civilization be saved. When the sinful living entities give up their obnoxious lifestyle and take to the regulative principles of freedom, his pure desire will have been fulfilled.

This Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement holds the torch of knowledge that can light up the whole world, but it can only endure if the dealings amongst its members are based on love and trust. Putting aside personal interest and acting for the good of the whole, each of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants has a vital role to play in propagating Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And now it is time for all of us to re-enter the arena of preaching if Śrīla Prabhupāda is to be a proud father. Each of us can pass out one book—today! Each devotee can improve the quality of his or her chanting—today! We can encourage our children, our friends, our spouses, in their pursuit of Kṛṣṇa consciousness—today! We can renew our vows, read a verse and purport, give a class, perform congregational chanting, offer our service in a thousand ways, and that will breathe new life into this movement. Senior experienced devotees can no longer watch from afar but must answer this call to arms. Acting cooperatively, such a world-wide transcendental CPR unit could revive the programs of His Divine Grace and bring about the revolution in consciousness he had envisioned.

Śrīla Prabhupāda wanted us all to take the credit for this victory, but there can be no victory without perseverance. There can be no growth without conviction. So now let the world take notice of the potency of a pure devotee and see how he enters the hearts of his disciples and inspires them from within to take up this task. Though his mortal frame is no longer visible to us, the memory of our beloved spiritual master can be easily awakened by a single “Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!”

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants in New York City.  
(written by Locanānanda dāsa)

## Nova Gokula

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Today is the auspicious appearance day of our real and eternal well-wisher, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Because of his causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda accepted us as his sons and daughters. On account of these feelings, Śrīla Prabhupāda is always strict with us. As is stated in the *śāstras:*

*lālane bahavo doṣas tāḍane bahavo guṇāḥ*  
*tasmāt putrāṁś ca śiśyāś ca tāḍayen na tu lālayet*

If the father or the *guru* is too lenient with his sons or students, they grow up with so many bad qualities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s strictness is the proof that he loves us. Once, with tears in his eyes, Prabhupāda said that he had left three sons but now Kṛṣṇa had given him three hundred sons. And when a devotee asked him about his natural sons, he replied, “You are all my sons.” Then, when the devotee insisted, “But what about your real sons?” Prabhupāda replied, “Do you think that you are not my real son?”

In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (12.3.42) it is stated,

*na rakṣiṣyanti manujāḥ sthavirau pitarāv api*  
*putrān bhāryāṁ ca kula-jāṁ kṣudrāḥ śiśnodaraṁ-bharāḥ*

**“Men will no longer protect their elderly parents, their children, or their respectable wives.”**

In the purport of that text it is written, “Young children are also tormented in many ways in this age. Suicide among children has increased dramactically in recent years because they are being born not to loving, religious parents but to degraded, selfish men and women. . . . Under such conditions, it is very difficult nowadays for parents to morally guide their children. Generally ignorant of spiritual science, parents cannot lead their children on the path of liberation and thus fail to fulfill their primary responsibility in family life.”

So Śrīla Prabhupāda was not concerned only with his own self-realization. He showed us that the spiritual reality is not empty of such sentiments as love between father and son or daughter. He never acted like a dry *sannyāsī* but like a father. Therefore Śrīla Prabhupāda is a very intimate messenger from the abode of Kṛṣṇa, where all is full of affection and ecstatic love.

Śrīla Prabhupāda fills his purports with such sentiments, which can be relished only by a pure devotee of the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa. And thus by means of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* Śrīla Prabhupāda leads his spiritual children to the divine realm of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

A virtuous son reciprocates the affection of his father. If he does not, he becomes a rascal, a shameless son. If we understand the love Śrīla Prabhupāda felt for us, we will automatically see one another as brothers and sisters and will be able to give up, gradually, lust, envy, greed, attachment, and other unwanted things within us.

In such a condition of mind, to work together, as Śrīla Prabhupāda asked us to do as a proof of our love for him, will be easy and natural.

Therefore, today we must offer our full obeisances at the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda and vow to strictly follow the *sādhana* practices, because only in this way can a tiny soul understand the vast ocean of good qualities that Śrīla Prabhupāda has.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda, our eternal well-wisher!  
All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda, our loving father!  
All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda, the dearest messenger of our best friend, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa!  
And all glories to those great souls who follow Śrīla Prabhupāda with faith!

The eternal servants of Śrīla Prabhupāda at the Nova Gokula farm community, Brazil.  
(written by Guṇeśvara dāsa)

## Nova Vraja-dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet.

On this auspicious day of your appearance, I hope you can grant me the power to declare to the whole world the glories of your holy self.

I had some association with you, and I still remember when, as a *karmī,* I saw you for first time at the airport in Los Angeles. First I saw what seemed like a colorful swarm of bees buzzing the holy name; it was your devotees anxiously awaiting your arrival. When you appeared among them, you seemed like the queen bee, and later at the temple when you started speaking, comfortably seated on your *vyāsāsana,* I actually saw that you were distributing the real royal jelly in the form of *kṛṣṇa-kathā.*

The next time I saw you was the Gaura-pūrṇimā Festival in Māyāpura. I was already a devotee. In the temple I saw you chanting, dancing, and circumambulating. I was in ecstasy just by seeing you jumping and jumping. And then in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class I could hardly see you, but I could hear you perfectly. The temple room was full of your loving disciples, all eager to have your association.

When I gave you the new Portuguese *Śrī Īśopaniṣad,* you said, “Yes, this is what I want; these books printed in all languages and distributed throughout the whole world.”

This instruction I carry within my heart, and I have been trying to follow it, despite the disastrous economy here in Brazil, and despite the fact that there are so many illiterate people here in the northeast part of the country. Please help me to never forget this instruction.

Your servants in Nova Vraja-dhāma, Recife, Brazil.  
(written by Jagat-vicitra dāsa)

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Millions of obeisances unto your lotus feet.

You have bestowed extreme mercy by giving degraded souls like us shelter in your house, ISKCON.

ISKCON is your home, and it is the manifestation of Vaikuṇṭha in this world. We, the living entities born in this Age of Kali, are very forgetful of your authority.

You have established ISKCON simply to make available to the living entities wandering in this material world pure love for Kṛṣṇa and His transcendental abode. That is the highest goal of life and the only way to attain everlasting peace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to become humble servants of your lotus feet. Unless we become your humble servants, it will not be possible for us to assist your disciples who are fully engaged in the service of your lotus feet and the movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we beg at your lotus feet that wherever we may be in this material world we will always remember you and remind others of you as well. Everything about you is transcendental. If we could simply remember you and your activities for one full day, we would become completely surrendered to you and thus be able to serve you and Lord Caitanya.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we humbly pray at your lotus feet to please give us the good intelligence to establish your disciples and servants in our hearts. Your servants and disciples are not separate from your lotus feet.

Hare Kṛṣṇa.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

The servants of your servants in Pandharpur, India.

## Paris

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to your appearance in the middle of this Kali-yuga. You appeared in the life of so many beings fed up with materialistic society and gave them the proper medicine, the chanting of the holy name. Not considering who was fit or unfit to receive it, you gave this medicine to everyone, taking to heart the words of your Guru Mahārāja that the world could not wait for Kṛṣṇa consciousness. The pain was so deep that it seemed a case of terminal disease. You did not mind the rebellious behavior of some of your patients; you were in a war, a big war, and had to go quick. Your expertise in this fight was that you knew the enemy very well and could diagnose every weak point. Victory was not easy, but Kṛṣṇa was on your side, guiding each of your steps. So you succeeded in all aspects, fulfilling your mission. Perfect physician, perfect general, perfect master!!

By bringing the most precious gem of transcendental knowledge to the most degraded and sinful Western world, you brought about a revolution in the sinful lives of millions (*tad-vāg-visargo janatāgha-viplavo*). This revolution is now changing the face of the world. Because we have been granted the mercy to be in contact with you through your books and other instructions, we can now understand the deep meaning of this revolution. The special *śakti* given to you by the Lord because of your pure devotion to Him is now empowering your faithful disciples who took over your mission. Being in contact with them brings the same auspiciousness as being with you, because they are the recipients of the unlimited flow of your mercy.

Even as some temples are struggling to survive because of Māyā’s heavy attacks, some are flourishing under your constant inspiration. The difference between the two is the complete surrender to your book distribution program, the key for success in all aspects of life. Śrīla Prabhupāda, when you see this movement, which has now spread worldwide because of your inspiration, certainly you are pleased, very pleased, and most pleased . . . but never displeased.

Each morning since the last Christmas marathon, all the Paris devotees have prayed to you and the Lord in this way:

“Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, dear Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara, dear Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā, dear Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Śacī-suta, please allow your servants at the Paris temple to increase their *saṅkīrtana* results for Your transcendental pleasure. We also pray to You to send us new devotees to increase the team of Your preachers and distribute Your transcendental books in big quantities.”

So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, although we don’t know what devotion means, allow us to increase your preaching.

*Saṅkīrtana-yajña kī jaya!*

Your servants in Paris, France.

## Perth

Introductory remarks:

Vyāsa-pūjā day is the opportunity for disciples to reflect on the unlimited glorious qualities of their spiritual master. Topmost among all these sublime characteristics of the *guru* is the causeless mercy he manifests in delivering the fallen souls from the ocean of nescience.

Therefore this Vyāsa-pūjā day is also the opportunity to reflect on a few intimate details of how one has become the fortunate recipient of this causeless mercy. A memorable incident, a specific instruction. A glance, a smile, or a wave. Or maybe even the thunderbolt of chastisement!

Here in Perth we have recently been extremely fortunate to hear descriptions of such exchanges from the lips of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s disciples who now travel the globe on his behalf. Mukunda Mahārāja relating remembrances from the early days in London. Prabhaviṣṇu Mahārāja discussing Śrīla Prabhupāda’s expressed desire that books be distributed. Paṅkajāṅghri’s Māyāpura pastimes.We have been fortunate to witness how dearmost sons and daughters have become storehouses of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s causeless mercy. Another such storehouse currently visiting Perth is Prabhava dāsa, who blissfully recounts his association with his spiritual master while he was a *gurukula* boy in Māyāpura and Vṛndāvana. We have asked Prabhava Prabhu, who has been with us in Perth for the last five months, to compile a Vyāsa-pūjā offering on behalf of all the devotees of the Perth *yātrā*. This is yet another way we can further plunder the transcendental storehouse manifested as the remembrance of Śrīla Prabhupāda within the hearts of his beloved devotees.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most auspicious day.

You are the most merciful benefactor of all the fallen people of this age. You have kindly left us with everything we could ever require for our advancement on the path back to Kṛṣṇa. Temples, beautiful Deities, so many wonderful Vaiṣṇavas, and, above all else, the most valuable contribution in the form of your transcendental literature. Nothing is lacking for our steady progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most merciful eternal associate of Kṛṣṇa. You have descended from the spiritual world in order to reclaim the fallen conditioned souls and take them back to Kṛṣṇa. You traveled to all corners of the world establishing temples, farms, *gurukulas,* and of course your big projects in India—Māyāpura, Vṛndāvana, and Bombay.

It is indeed your causeless mercy that you even journeyed to this small city of Perth. Back in 1975 you personally planted the seed of your preaching mission here in Western Australia.

Today we can see the manifested fruits of those initial blessings. Their Lordships Śrī-Śrī Gaura-Nitāi preside at Their beautiful Bayswater temple. Meanwhile, downtown in central city Perth, up to 180 people a day relish sumptuous Kṛṣṇa *prasādam* at the Hare Krishna Food for Life center.

Actually, Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is only by your blessings that this ISKCON movement is harmoniously expanding all over the globe. Please continue to bless us that we may never deviate from the rules and regulations you have kindly given. Please allow us to always remember your vigorous preaching efforts all over the world. And let us remain forever in the dust of your lotus feet.

Your insignificant servants in Perth, Australia.  
(written by Prabhava dāsa)

## Philadelphia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories unto you, who are the most illustrious transcendental savior of this Kali-yuga. As a father carefully instructs his children so they may properly mature, so you are providing to the immature, fallen children of this planet divine instruction by which to see the Absolute Truth, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and regain their natural, sheltered position at His lotus feet.

Your authorized biography tells how you arrived on the shores of America, carrying only a few days’ supply of food, a few hours’ spending money in New York, and some books, yet in your heart you carried a transcendental vision and plan that, when fully manifest, would cover the four corners of this planet and all the space in between with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. The books you carried were no ordinary books but windows to the spiritual world, portals allowing rays of divine light and mercy to shine down from Goloka Vṛndāvana-dhāma.

Coming ashore at Boston pier, you offered prayers to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, requesting Him to make you dance as He liked in His transcendental mission of saving the fallen souls. In your heart you had absolute faith in the purifying power of the holy names of Kṛṣṇa and the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.* You simply wanted to shine the light of the *Bhāgavata* everywhere for the benefit of the suffering *jīvas*.

O divine master, how fallen we were, how fallen we are, so forgetful of our relationship to Lord Kṛṣṇa, so steeped in *rajo-* and *tamo-guṇa,* burning in the fire of lust and greed, staggering after illusions of happiness in the bodily concept of life. But by the power of your devotion to Kṛṣṇa, by the power of the holy names and the process of hearing *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* you are truly bringing us out of darkness and into the light of the *Bhāgavata,* putting us in the cooling rays of the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

We think that if we have some good qualities, surely they are only a dim reflection of your own divine qualities. All glories, all glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Obeisances to you a thousand times again and again, from all eight directions! Please bestow upon us your blessings so that we may always appreciate your mercy and be engaged in your service. We pray that we may find ourselves always trying to follow in your footsteps, working cooperatively in your International Society for Krishna Consciousness, distributing the light of the *Bhāgavata* and the process of chanting the holy names of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

From all the temple residents and congregational members of ISKCON Philadelphia, Pennsylvania.

## Porto Alegre

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances unto your lotus feet.

We would really like to know how to appropriately address such a great soul as yourself in order to glorify you. Unlike many Vaiṣṇavas, or great souls, we are stupid materialists here, yet still we are trying to worship you on this Vyāsa-pūjā day.

No doubt, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a great soul, a *śaktyāveśa avatāra* empowered by Kṛṣṇa to preach the glories of His holy name. You are the founder-*ācārya* of this International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

You saved us from this dark well of material existence, and we are very pleased and happy because of this. And we promise to go on helping your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in each town and village of the world, as Lord Caitanya predicted five hundred years ago. Your spiritual master told you to help fulfill Lord Caitanya’s prediction by preaching the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the English language.

We promise you that we will always chant our sixteen rounds every day, trying to avoid the offenses, and that we will follow the four regulative principles and attend the spiritual programs with full enthusiasm. We promise to somehow or other stay in the association of devotees, living peacefully and without false prestige. And we also promise to try to please you by distributing your books, managing your temples and centers, preaching to guests, worshiping the Deities, cooking, etc.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, free us from sex desire, which is so strong. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please do not kick us away; allow us to be your servants. You have the ability to engage us, to find something in us that can be utilized in Kṛṣṇa’s service. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, do not allow us to be unengaged. Make us work hard for your ISKCON mission. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, free us from envy, laziness, and false ego.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to become your book distributors, accept us as your instruments. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, send us many nice people who like to hear about Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please send us more devotees to help us distribute more books, to buy or build more temples, and to help in your most desirable project of Śrīdhāma Māyāpur. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to be spiritually strong so that we do not fall victim to *māyā*. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to deal nicely with every living entity, especially those who were awarded the human form, and most especially of those who are surrendered unto your lotus feet—your servants and eternal associates.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to always chant the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* at every second of our lives, which are so short and miserable. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, excuse us for all the offenses we may have committed in attempting to glorify you on this most auspicious day of your divine appearance.

We remain  
Your attentive servants in Porto Alegre, Brazil.  
(written by Aṣṭavarga dāsa)

## Portugal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Thank you so much for the instruction of simple living and high thinking. It seems to be the only real way to live a life in devotional service and then go back to join you in Kṛṣṇa’s home at the end of this life, or else to remain here serving you if that’s your desire.

Living in the association of devotees, we’re constantly reminded of this most elevated principle. A devotee’s life is so wonderfully filled with details of a simple lifestyle with highest thought.

“Live Simply and Think of Kṛṣṇa!”

Get up by 4:00 a.m. to start a day dedicated to serving and praising the Supreme Lord.

Dress in simple cloth, *dhotī* or *sārī,* for you are humble servants of the highest master, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Chant the sixteen-word *mahā-mantra* on beads or with simple instruments as the basis of all activity and thought: to always remember Kṛṣṇa.

As to keeping body and soul together, honor simple vegetarian meals, remnants of food eaten by the Supreme Enjoyer.

Work a full day doing the needful and engaging everything in the service of the Supreme Proprietor.

Keep all your everything centered on the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Four premises for a simple life:

“Books are the basis.  
Preaching is the essence.  
Utility is the principle.  
Purity is the force.”

Main rule for high thought:

“Always think of Kṛṣṇa and never forget Him.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we bow down at your lotus feet a million times, and again and again millions of times more, thanking you for the most precious gifts that constitute our simple lives.

Please kindly favor us so that we can always use them to think of Kṛṣṇa while serving Him.

Your insignificant servants in Portugal.

## Potomac

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most glorious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Out of your mercy you appeared in this world to end our suffering, to give us life, to give us love. Thinking of you, we regain our life, our dormant Kṛṣṇa consciousness. While chanting “Jaya Rādhā-Mādhava” with you in the morning, our minds flow to thoughts of the Lord and His associates. We hear your Bhaktivedanta purports and know you have the answer to every question, the inner meaning of life.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught us love, how to give everything to Kṛṣṇa by surrendering to you. By your mercy we receive the benediction of Kṛṣṇa, and without your grace we can make no advancement. On this most blessed day we pray for your mercy upon us, as it is all we are made of.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Your humble servants in Potomac, Maryland, and throughout the Washington, D.C., area.  
(written by Anuttama dāsa)

## Puncak

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Out of your great compassion for the fallen, conditioned souls, you left Vṛndāvana and traveled all over the world preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Wherever you traveled, you brought Vṛndāvana with you. Wherever your lotus feet touched the earth, Kṛṣṇa consciousness has spread, and will spread even more in the future. As you visited Indonesia briefly in 1973, we are confident that Kṛṣṇa consciousness will spread here too, no matter what impediments may present themselves.

You are leading a vigorous two-pronged attack on the forces of Kali-yuga by establishing *harināma-saṅkīrtana* and distribution of Kṛṣṇa conscious literature, and by setting up Kṛṣṇa conscious farming communities based on simple living and high thinking. In our small farming community, we are trying in our small way to push on both these programs which were dear to you.

Although according to mundane vision you passed from this world in November 1977, we know that you are still living. Not only are you with Lord Kṛṣṇa in Goloka Vṛndāvana, but you are also still present within the temples you established, in your rooms in Māyāpura, Vṛndāvana, and Bombay, and wherever your disciples and grand-disciples are carrying out your instructions, especially wherever the devotees are distributing your books. We know that you are still with us, watching our service, that you still love all the devotees of ISKCON, and that you will never leave us alone and unprotected as long as we do not deviate from your instructions. This is our inspiration to push on, despite all obstacles.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for kindly establishing the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement all over the world and delivering all of us from the cycle of birth and death. We cannot offer very much in praise of your transcendental glories. What we can offer you this day is our heads at your lotus feet, and our determination to cooperate with one another to maintain and expand this wonderful ISKCON movement, of which you are the founder-*ācārya*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Your servants at Govinda Kuñja, Puncak, West Java, Indonesia.

## Rādhādeśa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the saffron dust of your divine lotus feet.

You gave us such a wonderful life: simply chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, take *prasādam,* dance, and be happy. Also you told us, “This Kṛṣṇa consciousness is very simple, but it must be understood with a cool head.” So to cool our blazing heads and hearts, you gave the regulated principles of freedom and oceans of nectarean philosophy, which have seen us through times when our faith has been severely tested. You instructed and encouraged us in the basics of your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, and indeed, in whatever way we are able to adhere to these instructions, in that way we have not only been protected but have actually made advancement in understanding ourselves to be eternal servants of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, that very Lord who tells us,

*vedāhaṁ samatītāni vartamānāni cārjuna*  
*bhaviṣyāṇi ca bhūtāni māṁ tu veda na kaścana*

**“O Arjuna, as the Supreme Personality of Godhead, I know everything that has happened in the past, all that is happening in the present, and all things that are yet to come. I also know all living entities; but Me no one knows” (Bg. 7.26).**

No one, of course, except pure devotees of your stature. And

*muktānāṁ api siddhānāṁ nārāyaṇa-parāyaṇaḥ*  
*sudurlabhaḥ praśāntātmā koṭiṣv api mahā-mune*

**“O great sage, among many millions who are liberated and perfect in the knowledge of liberation, one may be a devotee of Lord Nārāyaṇa, or Kṛṣṇa. Such devotees, who are fully peaceful, are very rare” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **6.14.5).**

You created ISKCON, a world-wide society containing unlimited facilities for any sincere individual desiring to understand and achieve the purpose and perfection of life. We are only lamenting that due to our neophyte natures we create difficulties in your “house in which the whole world can live peacefully.” With prostrated obeisances we pray that our more-and-more adherence to your instructions will prevent such disturbances from again arising. Especially you said that our love for you would be shown by how we cooperate with one another after your departure. In this age of quarrel and misunderstanding, only the development of deep, genuine Vaiṣṇava qualities, according to the directions given in your Bhaktivedanta purports, can give us the continuing and ever-increasing impetus and strength to follow this most essential instruction.

There should be no doubt that each and every word you wrote is absolute and perfect. No one should doubt this and reject “that which does not tally with one’s imagination.” What does it matter if the Vedic scriptures contradict modern “science.” The so-called authorities of today, under the illusion of *māyā,* speak, eat, and do “anything damn.” It is already amazing that these so-called intelligent men, dancing dogs in the hands of Māyā, pose themselves as authorities because of their ability to play with the toys Māyā has given them. And even more astounding is the blind acceptance of these authorities by so many other “educated” men. Who cares what these “man-animals” say about anything? We only care about what you say, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you love us truly, and you represent all the most brilliant, perfect entities going all the way back to the Supreme Lord Himself, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. As you have told us, “Only the devotee of the Lord can know what is what.”

*yasya deve parā-bhaktir yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

**“Only unto those great souls who have unflinching faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the imports of the Vedic literatures automatically revealed” (*****Śvetśāvatara Upaniṣad*** **6.38).**

We pray that as we continue to engage all our energies and resources in your service, that our faith, which has been bestowed by you, will grow and develop into pure love for you and Lord Kṛṣṇa. You are showing us how to do away with all *anarthas* and how to make the best use of whatever assets we have acquired “either by hard labor or good fortune,” thus changing us from *kṛpanas* into *brāhmaṇa* Vaiṣṇavas. This is the greatest miracle, and this proves to us that you are none other than the pure representative of God. It is only your mercy, manifesting in so many wonderful ways, that keeps us afloat and protected from the raging ocean of vice that is Kali-yuga. May we become more and more determined and expert in imbibing this causeless mercy and, as you always desire, distributing it to others.

*bhavantam evānucaran nirantaraḥ praśānta-niḥśeṣa-manoratāntaraḥ*  
*kadāham aikāntika-nitya-kiṅkaraḥ praharṣayiṣyāmi sa-nātha-jīvitam*

**“By serving you constantly, one is freed from all material contamination and is completely pacified. Oh, when shall we engage as your permanent eternal servants and always feel joyful to have such a fitting master?” (Śrī Yāmunācārya’s** ***Stotra-ratna,*** **verse 43).**

All glories to Your Divine Grace!  
All glories to your divine appearance!

Your servants at Rādhādeśa, Belgium.

## Riga

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on your appearance day.

We are feeling like lonely, poor, lost souls, since the feeling of separation from you is much deeper here in Riga, Latvia, at the edge of the former Soviet zone. In this whole huge zone there is not a single disciple of yours living permanently. No museums or rooms of yours, and up until recently only one *mūrti* of your likeness.

We are not complaining. We are just appreciating that it is not a cheap or automatic thing to be conscious of you. It requires deliberate effort—reading aloud your pastimes and reminding one another of them.

Most people here do not understand English, and only some of your books are translated. The sound of your recorded lectures rarely penetrates the air due to the language barrier. The glorious *Śrīla Prabhupāda-līlāmṛta* is not yet available in Russian, although the condensed edition recently appeared in Latvian. Our main solace is your books, which we are trying to distribute, and your *sannyāsī* disciples, who are trying to serve when they visit.

Still, we tell all newcomers that before they choose a spiritual master, they should learn to worship you for six months. It seems this instruction is quite abstract to most people, and they quickly choose a physically present spiritual master to fill the vacuum.

Your potency is very much felt, however, through the successful application of your methods. When you arrived in New York, which also seemed strange territory compared to Vṛndāvana, you immediately started the program: chanting, dancing, feasting, and philosophy. With that in mind, we tested this method, with good success. Is there a place in the whole universe where this doesn’t work? After seeing it work here (to the surprise of everyone), we are convinced that chanting, dancing, feasting, and philosophy will conquer everywhere.

Very recently we brought a life-size *mūrti* of you from India. Relief was immediate. With you gazing from the *vyāsāsana,* witnessing all our *kīrtanas,* lectures, and prayers, we all felt much closer to you, and the temple immediately developed a dignified, sober, authorized atmosphere. Thank you for coming. It reminded us of the description in the *Līlāmṛta* when the devotees decorated the storefront for the first time and you came in, appreciated the humble effort with a smile, and then continued in your serious way. We just had a similar experience.

Your books are also amazing beyond comprehension. People like to read, and we are lucky not to have so much bogus presentation of Vedic scriptures here (your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* is the only presentation of the *Gītā* in Latvian). So we can experience the potency of your books. Practically, on a weekly basis people come to the temple who have read “just one page and their lives have been turned.” We did nothing more than try to distribute these books. We did not try to convince them philosophically or dazzle them with “Vedic culture,” etc. They just read the books and walked in the door. Some of them follow four regulative principles and chant rounds, just from reading your books. Amazing! Of course, we could assume that people here are maybe more pious than elsewhere. But no other book is convincing them to change their lives so much. Please help us to distribute more books, to every door and to every person walking the street.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us your mercy. Consider our special case, that we are lonely and desperate for your association in any form. Please send us your disciples and make us all conscious of you, so that we may never forget you.

Your servants in Riga, Latvia.

## Rome

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You showed us how a Kṛṣṇa conscious person can sacrifice everything for the satisfaction of the Lord and, by completely surrendering, become *jagad-guru* and save the whole world.

Your preaching is so effective that it can really soften the hearts of the most hard-hearted. When we see devotees dancing, listening to classes, and taking *prasādam* together, we always think of you, the only one who took the trouble to come here, to the degraded Western world.

After so many years we are starting to feel that the process of *sādhana-bhakti* you taught us is really working, that it is the highest benediction for us and the people in general.

We should realize that you saved us from a hellish condition of life, from the ocean of Kali-yuga filled up with so many dangerous sharks consisting of TV programs, drug sellers, disco and rock music, bars and boutiques, soccer and football games, restaurants and casinos, newspapers, magazines, comics, young attractive girls, prostitutes and homosexuals, sleep, lust, suicide, madness, and so many other things we cannot list.

All we have to do now is to properly follow your instructions, trying to never forget how merciful you were with us.

In this way our life will proceed, and one day, by the will of the Lord, we’ll be able to reunite with you in the eternal *saṅkīrtana* party in the spiritual world.

Your servants in Rome and Naples, Italy.  
(written by Harideva dāsa)

## St. Louis

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We come before you at this most auspicious time of year and, offering *daṇḍavats,* beg to be empowered to speak some words of praise for all that you have given us. Yet what is our ability to glorify you? We are but foolish Kali-yuga *jīvas,* struggling at every second to survive the onslaught of this darkened age with all of its sinful distractions. Just as a worshiper of Mother Gaṅgā will offer *gaṅga-jāl* to her, in the same way we offer back to you your own words of devotion and spiritual insight, for you are their source, and our inspiration.

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

Do you need us?

You, who were so satisfied in Vṛndāvana-dhāma executing the process of *rāgānuga-bhakti—*what could we possibly offer to you? Yet in your humility you said we were sent by your Guru Mahārāja to assist your work in serving him. This was your humility: always appreciating your disciples and their efforts.

Did you succeed because of our help?

Since you are divinely, eternally connected with Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa in Goloka, any activity you perform would be for Their pleasure and fully blessed by Them, and therefore successful. What can we tiny *jīvas* do to assist such a pure and transcendental relationship? Yet you engaged us, with our passionate natures, as your foot soldiers so we would go forth and establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness in new and exciting frontiers. You empowered us to speak on your behalf and give Kṛṣṇa to others, just as you had given Kṛṣṇa to us. How could we have succeeded without your guidance and instruction?

Did devotees join your ISKCON society by our efforts?

Then, as now, the contact the conditioned *jīva* receives with Kṛṣṇa is through your pure and loving teachings. That *jīva* may never have your *vapu,* your personal contact, yet the *vāṇī,* the instruction, is what all *jīvas* strive to receive and require for release from the bonds of material life. You are allowing us to present Kṛṣṇa to so many *jīvas* each day through the means of your books, *harināma,* Kṛṣṇa’s *prasādam,* and His devotees’ association. What is our capability to do these activities without your mercy?

Does your Kṛṣṇa consciousness depend on us?

From your very childhood your life is an unending episode of Kṛṣṇa conscious activities. Worshiping Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa with your sister as a young child, performing Ratha-yātrā with the families of the neighborhood, having Kṛṣṇa appear in your dreams when you were in college and ask you to worship Him, always speaking about Kṛṣṇa to your business associates, publishing *Back to Godhead* during your *gṛhastha-līlā,* and your setting the topmost example of world preacher for Lord Caitanya. In comparison, our own childhood and youth form an unspeakable path. Yet we now are following on the path that has been trod by sages and *sādhus* for centuries. Is this by our own effort? Indeed, our very existence depends on you alone!

Thus we see that your life is complete without us, yet we have no life without you. Therefore we can come to only one true conclusion:

*yasya prasādād bhagavat prāsādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ‘pi*

**“By the mercy of the spiritual master, one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master one cannot make any spiritual advancement.”**

*hā hā prabhu koro doya*  
*deho more pāda-chaya*

**“O master! Be merciful unto us. Give us the shade of your lotus feet.”**

*tomāra karuṇā—sāra*  
*karuṇā hoile kāṇḍiya kāṇḍiya*  
*prāṇa na rakhibo āra*

**“Your mercy is all I am made of. If you are not merciful to me, I can only weep, and I will not be able to maintain my life anymore.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we exist only by your mercy, and we pray that you never withdraw that mercy. Please allow us to continue to serve you. Falling at your feet, begging for deliverance, we remain

Your humble servants in St. Louis, Missouri.  
(written by Caturātmā dāsa)

## San Diego

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

How I’ve been asked to write you an offering of glorification for the New Govardhana *yātrā* is beyond me. You know my many faults. You know what few good qualities I have, gifts only received from you after many years of feeble attempts to serve you. As always, this must be another one of the unlimited ways in which you’ve tried to engage and purify your lowly and mean servant. Since you know my thoughts and prayers, being able to enter into the heart of a disciple (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.5.7), you also know that I’m beginning to realize I’m not able to speak for anyone else except myself.
