For so many years I thought I was quite good at assessing people’s natures, their words, and their deeds, but now by your mercy you’re forcing me to realize that my batting average is so much lower than I thought. So please allow me to pray and reveal my tiny mind as an act of glorification to you.

The ocean of gifts in devotional service that you’ve given and continue to give me and all of your servants is unlimited. Being a tiny servant of small intelligence, I’ll mention only one of them. Foremost in my mind is this society or family of devotees that you’ve given and sheltered me with. The unfortunate quality of a third-class devotee like myself is that I don’t appreciate the Vaiṣṇavas enough. Simply to worship you and your transcendental qualities is not enough. The many thousands of devotees around the world who you’ve made are your servants, your children, and your representatives. Many a time you expressed your gratitude or glorified the devotees’ qualities, and in your every reciprocation even served them. On one Vyāsa-pūjā ceremony for your spiritual master you said you considered us to have been sent by Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī to help you in your devotional service.

This is the mood and mentality I’ve been focusing on lately in my devotional service to you. Seeing everyone, even the devotees, as spirit soul is honestly more than I can do, except for a little exercising on the intellectual platform. But I am finding it much easier to grasp and even practice seeing all the devotees you’ve kindly allowed me to be surrounded by as your servants and representatives. As I grow in this firm conviction and perspective, I find that I dwell much less on their faults and much more on their good qualities. The resultant enthusiasm and purification that comes from this perspective is wonderful! I find that I can more often and to a greater degree take advantage of their guidance and inspiring examples. And this only helps me in my growth of devotional service to you and the Lord.

But as Kṛṣṇa tells me in the *Bhagavad-gītā,* the level of devotional service that I’m trying to practice is not all a bed of roses. The purification is viewed first as poison and then as nectar. So as I develop closer friendships with the devotees and begin to practice revealing my mind and inquiring from them, they kindly open my eyes to the many times I unknowingly and unwittingly have offended them. This can be very painful indeed. But what a blessing you are giving me through the instrumentality of your servants! You’re allowing me to realize I am so less advanced in Kṛṣṇa consciousness than I previously thought. It is so difficult to effectively practice and solidly advance in devotional service if I think that I’m more advanced in Kṛṣṇa consciousness than I really am. So now more than ever I understand that with the inspiration and friendship of the devotees I can advance in serving you much more rapidly than I could without these valuable blessings. What to speak of the possibility of enlivening the devotees by becoming an instrument in your hands.

I thank you from the bottom of my heart for this realization and growing desire, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Help me to grow in devotional service to you, the Lord, and His devotees. Please never abandon or allow your devotees to abandon me despite my many faults and shortcomings in devotional service. If you will only let me remain performing some devotional service in helping your mission, be it small, varied, or big, I know I will increasingly find the true satisfaction for which I’ve eternally yearned.

Your lowly and insignificant servant, Rajendranandana dāsa, on behalf of the wonderful devotees I’m allowed to associate with in  
The New Govardhana Yātrā, San Diego, California.

## Sao Paulo

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Glories to the most blessed of all events! Glories to the most important day of the universe! Glories to the transcendental appearance of the dearest devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa! All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda, the personified distribution of love of Godhead.

*Golokera-prema-dhana Hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana.* There is no doubt that the great founder-*ācārya* and propagator of this movement of Lord Caitanya, which descends from the spiritual world, could not have had any origin besides Goloka Vṛndāvana.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the congenial herald of Kṛṣṇa, transforms the world by fulfilling the desires of his worshipful Lord. Śrīla Prabhupāda, the mercy manifested in this world, personifies the sympathy of the Supreme Lord toward the conditioned souls.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! You are the refreshing and pacifying balsam which fell upon this miserable and ardent hell of Kali-yuga.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories unto you, who haven’t come to this world by accident but by Kṛṣṇa’s plan. Lord Caitanya had expressed the innermost desire of his heart:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi-grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haiba mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**“In all villages and towns all over the world, everywhere, this** ***saṅkīrtana*** **movement will be preached.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you descended from Kṛṣṇaloka, where in supreme bliss you enjoyed loving and innocent merrymaking with Śrī Govinda and His transcendental and beloved attendants. From there you came to this filthy material world to fulfill the prophecy of Lord Gaurasundara.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were the supreme general who marched alone, stepping over the arrogant Western mentality. It was you who danced the famous “swami step” to the sound of the holy names of Kṛṣṇa and walked courageously, elegantly, courteously, vigorously, kindly, decisively, mercifully, smilingly, and always protected by Śrī Kṛṣṇa, in this way gaining hearts, conquering afflicted and sincere souls, placing them in rows, training them, and organizing thus your great fight to establish love of God as the only worthwhile goal of human life. Today this huge established army has numerous headquarters throughout the planet.

These headquarters are true oases in the desert, safe shelters where the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda are worshiped as rare jewels, shelters where many wounded souls may rest. Daily from these headquarters depart volunteer soldiers, worshipers of Śrīla Prabhupāda, faithful servants of the great general who still keeps leading his army, who is present in his instructions, established rules and laws. And this army keeps throwing its bombs—BOOKS OF REAL KNOWLEDGE—which explode everywhere, all over the world, destroying the devilish philosophy of impersonalism, killing off the modern atheistic mentality that dominates the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! In the future those who now comprise this atheistic world will surely venerate you and thank you. I humbly pray before your sacred lotus feet to ever be able to stay as a simple insignificant soldier in the rank and file of your incomparable army full of bright officers and magnanimous active soldiers, following you without reserve.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you, the sublime general who triumphantly command the true salvation army, the only good thing that there is in this dark age of nescience.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda’s ISKCON!  
All glories to the *saṅkīrtana* movement!  
All glories to Jagad Guru Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Your servants in Sao Paulo and Santos, Brazil.  
(written by Jaya Gokula dāsa)

## Sarajevo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

**A Duty**

In the land of heavy industry  
A lot of Muslims are living too;  
Still people are getting Kṛṣṇa,  
Prasādam, magazine, a book.  
A lady is forced to cook meat  
For her Muslim husband’s “piety,”  
But at heart she  
Is Kṛṣṇa’s devotee.

Karma-waves splash over,  
The war is starting soon,  
Your son on time to safety sends us,  
One more causeless boon.

Mercy please grant us  
To go back quick,  
Preaching to continue  
Where ignorance is thick.

We worry when, without spare,  
Your books again we’ll distribute there.  
People will rot in frustration,  
Remaining foolish crooks,  
Without your inspiration,  
Your transcendental books.

Many seeds are sown already,  
But that is not enough.  
We want to flood the ignorance profusely,  
The work’s not done even half.

Our duty is clear; our guru trained us:  
“You must endeavor hard.  
ALL people starve and suffer without him,  
ALL must get Prabhupāda!”

Your servants in Sarajevo, Bosnia-Herzegovina.

## Scotland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Hare Kṛṣṇa. Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to you.

By the mercy of Kṛṣṇa you have come; that is the world’s greatest fortune, our greatest fortune. We are hoping that we will soon catch a glimpse of what your divine association has done for the world. When I say a glimpse, I mean a truly sincere glimpse, one that, once caught, will never to be forgotten. Then we will be able to truly act the way you would have us act. We are at present trying, but we are only too well aware that we can offer much more, and should, and must offer much more. We want to give ourselves in service to you, but due to our foolish material attachments we often feel fear in our filthy hard hearts. But, we are hoping that if we please you our hearts will soften, and then we will be able to offer actual devotional service at your lotus feet. We are so very fortunate that we have this great gift given to us by you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. There is no gift that can compare with such a transcendental treasure. No one has ever before caused such changes in the lives of the suffering conditioned souls. No one can compare with you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your service to you Guru Mahārāja is perfect in every way. We are hoping that we can, by following you instructions, follow in your transcendental footsteps.

We want to take your instructions to heart and try to please you in some way or other. If we can please you by our words and activities, then we know our lives are complete and perfect. Your books are being distributed, although we are sure we could, by your mercy and desire, distribute many more. We like to see your books being distributed in great quantities and to as many souls as possible. We feel this is pleasing to you, so we get pleasure from this service.

More and more souls are reading your books and coming in contact with your movement. Your great work is going on unendingly. You are constantly at work delivering the fallen souls. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Please engage us more and more in your service, as you see fit.

Our real offering to you will be seen in our service over the years, in our continued commitment to your movement, but we want to take your mercy and show improvement in our service and service attitude as soon as we possibly can.

Thank you for everything, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We pray that we WILL surrender at your lotus feet and take great delight in serving you, no matter what comes or goes.

We desire to always remain in your service. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are pleased to be always indebted to you.

Your insignificant servants in Scotland.

## Singapore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

It was sometime in 1972 when you tried to visit this most unholy land, only to be stopped at the airport because of visa technicalities. Yet despite this most uncalled-for insult, you deeply prayed that somehow or other there should be a center here in Singapore. Because of that strong desire of yours, today after twenty years we have this to say: we are on the verge of purchasing a property and installing your beloved Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava-Mohana Deities. We have fifteen devotees who go out for approximately eight hours a day, six days a week, distributing your transcendental books.

This once-forsaken city, this city that rejected your mercy so long ago, is again beginning to breathe Kṛṣṇa consciously—just like a person who had been in coma and is slowly responding to treatment.

We are hopeful that soon your name will be a household word here, and that your pastimes will be widely known. Soon we expect to hear of citizens crying for that offense they committed at your lotus feet by not letting you into Singapore twenty years ago.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly continue to bless us, although we have taken so much time here to fulfill your transcendental desire. Please help us on this most auspicious day. Kindly bless us to continue fulfilling your desires eternally.

Always praying at your lotus feet, we remain  
Your most unworthy servants in Singapore.

## South London

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

The Vedic scriptures enjoin, *tad vijñānārthaṁ sa gurum evābhigacchet:* “To understand the goal of life one must approach a bona fide spiritual master.” In this day and age of so many cheaters and unscrupulous “*gurus,*” we are so lucky to have found you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are the true Jagad-guru—your fame is spread all over the three worlds. You have brought the eternal religious principles within the reach of everyone, and you have set the pace for the spiritual advancement of the entire universe.

We can never come close to repaying our unlimited debt to you. In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* Prahlāda Mahārāja has said, “How can I leave the service of my spiritual master, who has brought me to the transcendental position of direct association with the Lord? My duty is to serve Him.” We beg you to accept our insignificant and worthless attempts at trying to spread your divine mission and propagate this *saṅkīrtana* movement. We pray that by Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s grace our desire to render service unto you will become pure and unmotivated. As Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī states, we should take up the words of the spiritual master as our life and soul, not caring for our personal benefit or loss. In this respect, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the perfect *ācārya:* you have shown the greatest determination in your untiring effort to fulfill the desire of your spiritual master.

After transmigrating through millions of births in the higher and lower species, we have been given this rare and precious opportunity to be accepted as servants of your servants. But we are not even intelligent enough to fully appreciate and utilize this wonderful opportunity. However, you are so merciful that although we keep committing countless offenses by not strictly following your instructions, you are so kind and forgiving that you do not deny us your association.

This association continues in the form of your eternal instructions. In your purports in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* you have said that when the spiritual master appears he brings order to chaos and when he leaves the chaos returns. But as long as we constantly look to your instructions and always remain faithful to you, you will always remain with us, and by your presence in the form of your *vāṇī,* the order you have established will continue for thousands of years.

We are so useless that we are unable to glorify you despite all that you have done for us. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are always full of compassion for the fallen conditioned souls. We are very fallen souls and need your mercy constantly. Please forgive us for our innumerable offenses and mistakes and give us the benediction of continued service unto you.

Your insignificant grandchildren at the ISKCON centers in South London and Rochester, England.  
(written by Rādhā-dāmodara-devī dāsī)

## Stockholm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances to your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

At the time life when Vrajavāsīs generally make their vows never to leave Vṛndāvana, you made your way over the ocean to the countries of the meat-eaters to save the lowest of the lowest. Leaving Vraja-mandala, you brought the holy *dhāma* to us, not caring for physical difficulties. Disregarding your advanced age and the obstacles put in your way, fresh and radiant, you appeared in the midst of the most confused souls. Your youthful smile and enthusiasm swept away the misery of your ignorant listeners. Amazed, and aroused from their long, long sleep, they warmed up in the shower of your nectarean words. Simple but sublime, your message entered their hearts and turned them into gardens of *bhakti*. Then, like an enthusiastic gardener, with the power of your devotion you eliminated

the weeds in the hearts of your followers, bringing them closer and closer to the Kṛṣṇa sun.

Your enthusiasm could never be checked as you, like a young dancer, entered the dark stage of our material existence, turning it into the most radiant place. We were young and you were in advanced age . . . but it was just the opposite. You pushed us out of our introverted moroseness and showed us the freedom of surrendering to Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Not caring for boundaries of material existence, you are leading us to the truly free existence of unconditional life. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the youngest of us all, beyond the influence of time.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I don’t want to get old, even though my body may be aging. Śrīla Prabhupāda, I want to remain close to your lotus feet, even though they may move fast all over the creation. Clinging to your preaching mission and seeking eagerly the company of your enthusiastic disciples and grand-disciples, I hope to avoid the worst, the nostalgia of old age, the “seniority” coming to me. I want to be amongst your *saṅkīrtana* fanatics, invading people’s homes and hearts with the knowledge of your books, reflecting the powerful light of mercy you bestowed upon us. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, don’t allow me to settle down in dark corners of my mind, becoming a so-called “mature” old man. I want to keep on running behind you, not caring for social conventions. I want to be the blissful Hare Kṛṣṇa devotee, not the over-intelligent “wise guy” who lost the bliss of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Holding tight to the new *bhaktas,* seeing them reflecting your dynamic preaching spirit, I may also stay young and alive.

You preach to us in so many ways. You speak from your books, but you may also appear in the heart of any sincere *bhakta*. The life can be found where the fire of preaching flairs up highest. There, Śrīla Prabhupāda, I want to be. Avoiding *dharma, artha, kāma,* and *mokṣa,* I want to run fanatically for your mercy. Avoiding all kinds of mundane social or altruistic concepts, I want, Śrīla Prabhupāda, always to stay under your wings, which you spread out so mercifully so we may safely fly home. As long there is your BBT, as long as your books are going out, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as long as there are ISKCON temples inviting the conditioned souls, there will be life. And your ever-fresh voice will be heard through the pages of *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* bringing the most revolutionary message to the miserable souls. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you took such nice care of me until now, giving me the most merciful association of such nice Godbrothers, and I am sure you can save anybody from old age and death if he takes shelter in your preaching mission.

Living in the most peaceful preaching atmosphere, which was created by your most dedicated disciples, seeing tons of your books being published, and seeing the increasing kindness of your grand-disciples, I can only say, Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for this blissful life.

Your servants in Stockholm, Sweden.  
(written by Maṇidhara dāsa)

## Sydney

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Thank you  
for coming to this place  
and saving us all  
from Māyā’s embrace.

Thank you  
for the light in the storm:  
the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa  
in His Deity form.

Thank you  
for the words you spoke,  
the temples you built,  
and the books you wrote.

Thank you  
for showing us the way  
to practically serve  
the Lord each day.

Thank you  
for all your love divine  
and the gift you brought  
of a life sublime.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please forgive us  
for being slow;  
though trying to follow,  
we’ve a long way to go.

Please forgive us  
for our casual neglect  
of the precious jewels  
we’re meant to protect.

Please forgive us  
for all our offense,  
our ignorant manner  
and devotional pretense.

Please forgive us,  
and allow us still  
to remain in your service  
and to live in your will.

Dear Father,

We hope one day  
to make you proud  
in the fulfillment of your vision  
and all that we vowed.

We hope one day  
to hear you say,  
“You can come home now,  
back home to stay.”

Until then,  
though we stretch your patience,  
please tolerate and forgive us  
and accept our obeisance.

*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!*

From all your devotees in Sydney, Australia, on this most auspicious day of your appearance.

## Tampa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

What we have received from you is more valuable than all the riches of the world. People work so hard and sacrifice their lives for wealth and the objects of their enjoyments, but they are ignorant, they cannot see. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are giving freely the most valuable object of our love and happiness—Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Just like Lord Caitanya, you are giving *kṛṣṇa-prema* freely, without considering who is qualified or not. You have developed a network for attracting the conditioned souls to spiritual life, knowingly or unknowingly, and we are amazed at your success.

We at ISKCON Tampa, in Florida, are trying helplessly to fulfill your mission of spreading Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. Just like the angry bird who was trying to dry up the ocean with its beak, we feel very tiny and helpless in this giant task of preaching in Kali-yuga America. Just this Tampa-St.-Petersburg area of Florida seems overwhelming to preach in, what to speak of the entire world, which you tackled single-handedly.

By all our life’s effort we are trying to do what you did with one finger—you pointed to us and said, “Go preach Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission in every town and village,” and you gave us the power and ability to do it. Your accomplishment is no less than what Kṛṣṇa did with one finger when He lifted Govardhana Hill. To both feats we say, “How did He do that? Amazing!” We are trying so hard and having so much difficulty with what you did so easily. You tricked us to surrender to Kṛṣṇa by showing us your easy, simple Ganges-like flowing pure devotional service.

How do you do it, Śrīla Prabhupāda? Please reveal your mercy to us. We are very fallen and unqualified, simply stumbling, babbling, conditioned souls trying to do what you do.

We want to serve you and fulfill your mission to make ISKCON grow. Please give us the strength. At the time of our death we want to look back and say, “Yes, we did something for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda; we didn’t waste our valuable human life selfishly.”

On this most treasured day of your appearance amongst us, we want to rededicate our lives to you in your service.

Your fallen servants in Tampa, Florida.

## Tokyo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

You have kindly given us this opportunity to remember some of your transcendental qualities and pastimes. And as we remember something about you we can also remember Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, because you are his intimate associate. “The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord because he is the most confidential servant of the Lord.” So to glorify you is our actual goal in life, and to do so is natural and sublime.

By your transcendental association and pure chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* (Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare) you have begun a spiritual revolution, and thus the otherwise unfortunate inhabitants of Kali-yuga are giving up their sinful lives and becoming bright-faced devotees. The conditioned souls are becoming purified by the touchstone of your association.

Someone heard you speak in New York in 1966, another remembered the wonderful taste of the *halavā* at a Sunday Feast, another carries your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* wherever she goes . . . How many souls have heard the holy names? How many have your books in their homes? Yes, certainly your association is the most valuable asset we have, and we should share this association with whomever we meet. “Indeed, the pure devotees of Lord Viṣṇu wander throughout the universe not for their personal selfish interest, but to purify all the conditioned souls.”

You taught us how to approach the spiritual master, how to inquire from him submissively, and how to render service unto him. You taught us how to love the spiritual master. After you first met Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī in 1922, your friend asked you, “What do you think of him?” You replied, “He’s wonderful. The message of Lord Caitanya is in the hands of a very expert person.” You accepted him as your spiritual master immediately. Not officially but in your heart. You were thinking you had met a very nice saintly person. “Accepting the bona fide spiritual master as one’s life and soul and worshipable deity, the disciple should learn from him the process of pure devotional service.” When you took initiation from Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta he remarked, “Yes, he likes to hear. He does not go away. I have marked him. I will accept him as my disciple.”

You are always glorifying the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa, by writing your Bhaktivedanta purports in accordance with and for the pleasure of the previous *ācāryas*. So many books in so many languages have been published. Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta said, “Whatever he writes, publish it.” So please send more cassettes, so we can print and distribute more of your books. My only wish is to have my consciousness purified by the words emanating from your lotus mouth. Of course, your books form the basis of our daily lives: “Read them and distribute them.” This is our motto.

Being a resident of Vṛndāvana and an intimate follower of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, you have shown us how to practically apply the instructions of Rūpa Gosvāmī throughout the whole world. “Things should be accepted for the Lord’s service and not for one’s personal sense gratification.” This was also the example shown by your own spiritual master, who at the time of your initiation instructed you to study Rūpa Gosvāmī’s *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu*. Thus now we have the *Nectar of Devotion,* the complete science of *bhakti-yoga,* your summary study of *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu*. And like the six Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana, you are now being honored all over the three worlds and are worth taking shelter of because you are absorbed in the mood of the *gopīs* and are engaged in the transcendental loving service of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

You taught us to get up by 4 a.m. and attend *maṅgala-ārati* (instead of sleeping in ignorance during the best part of the day), take a shower (instead of being dirty), wear a *dhotī* or *sārī* (instead of wearing *karmī* clothes), chant Hare Kṛṣṇa (instead of singing our own glories), read *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (instead of reading about mundane love affairs), eat *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* (instead of eating who knows what), shave our heads (instead of having long hair, considering ourselves very attractive), mark our bodies with *tilaka* (instead of wearing cosmetics and oils), associate with devotees (instead of staying up all night with our “friends”), etc., etc., etc. You saved us from a typical Kali-yuga sinful life and have given us an ecstatic transcendental way of life based on serving and remembering Kṛṣṇa.

You single-handedly created a worldwide movement of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in countries such as England, France, Russia, and America, thus fulfilling the prayer of Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura: “Oh, when will that day come?” You single-handedly introduced BTG, your “feeble attempt” to “explain in English our thoughts and arguments.” You single-handedly went out to distribute *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and beat the *bṛhat mṛdaṅga,* having been inspired by the words of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhanta: “I had a desire to print some books. If you ever get money, print books.” You single-handedly introduced the Sunday Love Feast, using ordinary ingredients: vegetables, beans, flour, fruits, rice, milk, etc., and making from them *sabjīs,* fancy rice, *dāl, samosas,* apple chutney, sweet rice, *halavā, gulabjāmuns,* etc. As Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta remarked, “He will do. When the time comes, he will do everything himself. You don’t have to recommend him.”

Having heard from Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta “I have every hope that you can turn yourself into a very good English preacher,” you went all over the world to tell people about Kṛṣṇa. You went to New York and told the hippies that by chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa they could get higher than by taking LSD. “You don’t need to take anything for your spiritual life. Your spiritual life is already there.” You went to London and when asked what you were trying to teach, you replied, “I am trying to teach what you have forgotten.” When asked if you had a concept of hell in your religion, you replied, “Yes, London is hell. It is always damp, cloudy, and raining. In India the sun is always shining.” You went to Sydney with Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa Deities to install. You named them Rādhā-Gopīnātha, and when leaving you prayed to Rādhā-Gopīnātha, “Now I am leaving You in the hands of the *mlecchas*. I cannot take the responsibility. You please guide these boys and girls and give them the intelligence to worship You nicely.”

We know that to adequately glorify even a fraction of your pastimes is not possible, but having been given this rare opportunity, we are feeling your divine presence here in Tokyo.

Your devotees in Japan.  
(written by Jagadvīra dāsa)

## Tucson

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

The first time I saw you, I slept through your class  
At Golden Gate Park—I laid on the grass—  
So covered over, no greater offense  
Than wallowing in ignorance at my soul’s expense.

I finally awoke after what seemed like years,  
To witness your dancing. My eyes filled with tears.  
Your hands upraised, beckoning all to perceive  
Lord Caitanya’s mercy for the most fallen to receive.

You changed the stonelike hearts of all who came—  
Hippies, students, drop-outs, and even the mentally insane.  
Your physical presence was such a powerful force  
To bring the misguided *jīvas* back on proper course.

It’s been eighteen years since I first saw your divine form,  
And since then I’ve weathered a number of storms;  
A devotee in name, but simply pretending,  
Callous to your words, I go on offending.

Despite my stubborn nature and restless mind,  
And temporal gratification to which I’m most inclined,  
You’re always with me as guide, friend, and master,  
Saving me from sure spiritual disaster.

The more I contemplate, the more I realize my debt.  
Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, don’t let me ever forget  
To sacrifice this life as you have shown,  
Not wasting this chance, like others I’ve blown!

Out here in the desert, the preaching is sweet.  
The shade of your mercy shields us from the heat.  
It’s sincere dedication that’ll please Your Divine Grace.  
Anything less and we’ll be out of the race!

So dear Gurudev, you can count on this crew  
To stay fixed in your service and see this project through.  
We pray for the ability to please you this way;  
Following in your footsteps, let us never go astray.

Your servants in Tucson, Arizona.  
(written by Sandaminī-devī dāsī)

## Udhampur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus like feet. All glories to your divine personality, and all glories to your appearance day.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you appeared on this planet to deliver fallen souls like ourselves from the clutches of deadly Māyā.

We were wandering like dogs in this material world and hankering for sense gratification without knowing the purpose of this human life. So you appeared on this planet the day after the appearance of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You enabled us to purify our senses by engaging ourselves in the different activities of your great Society, ISKCON—namely, book distribution, street chanting, Food for Life, and so many others. This great ISKCON is the only shelter for those who want to be saved from the thrashings of Māyā and in the end go back home, back to Godhead.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you built a house in which the whole world can live. This house is transcendental and so purifying that no sooner does one enter it that he is purified. This is only because of your causeless mercy upon fallen souls like ourselves. So this, your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, is a very important movement, but we are so dull that we don’t take this movement seriously.

We are all begging for your mercy on this most auspicious day, Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, so that we can understand the significance of your movement in this lifetime and go to the eternal abode of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Goloka Vṛndāvana dhāma. Your causeless mercy can arouse a desire in us to serve your lotus like feet by taking this movement very seriously.

In the end, we fall at your lotus feet and beg for your mercy.

Your servants in Udhampur, India.

## Uppsala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Covered by darkness,  
Heart full of pain,  
Crying for help,  
Feeling almost insane,

Lost in the world,  
Becoming rascal and fool,  
No other choice  
Than to be Māyā’s tool,

No hope in sight,  
As far as I could see,  
Unhappy, lonely,  
Forever to be.

Prayers in agony:  
“Please, please help me  
Out of this madness  
And deep misery.”

I have almost forgotten,  
So tight was I bound  
Before, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Your mercy I found.

Not worthy or qualified,  
A worm in stool.  
Still, your mercy  
You gave to this fool.

You brought me along  
And gave me the chance  
To glorify Kṛṣṇa  
With song and dance.

Father and guide,  
Most dear friend,  
You, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Material struggle did end.

Your mercy is still here,  
Though your vapu is gone.  
Through your instructions  
You keep leading us on.

Who can understand?  
A mystery to man.  
The fallen are saved  
By your Glorious Plan.

Your servants in Uppsala, Sweden.  
(written by Prapūjaka dāsa)

## Vancouver

Dear Śrīla Prabhupada,

Please accept our most insignificant obeisances at your lotus feet, which can award one the perfection of all life. In making any offering, we are reminded of our most fallen position, which is covering the ability to make a perfect offering full of pure love. But we proceed nevertheless, drawing courage by appealing to your inconceivable mercy.

It is impossible to describe the glories and mercy of the *mahā-bhāgavata* devotee due to the limitations of any language, and especially in our case, since we are full of unlimited faults and volumes of *anarthas*, devoid of any proper feelings of love. But we know that a pure devotee only accepts the good quality, irrespective of how small and insignificant it may be, and magnifies it.

We maintain the hope that our offering will bring you some satisfaction.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as a *śaktyāveśa-avatāra,* an incarnation of the Supreme Lord, you have so liberally distributed the science of *bhakti-yoga* to the inhabi-tants of this planet and boldly declared the supremacy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and taught that the ultimate goal of life is *kṛṣṇa-prema*.

As an ideal follower of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, you have established the *saṅkīrtana-yajña* in every town and city all over the world for the next ten thousand years, thereby fulfilling the prophecy of your most beloved master.

This *harināma-saṅkīrtana* movement is the only means of bringing sanity, peace, and real uninterrupted happiness for all living entities who are plagued with endless suffering and oppression due to activities dictated by the modes of passion and ignorance.

Each one of us was being uncontrollably blown, hurled, and tossed about in all directions by the insurmountable material energy, and yet our deep-rooted desire to lord it over the material energy and imitate the supreme enjoyer did not become exhausted to any degree. This would have been our fate for all eternity if it were not for you, dear Śrīla Prabhupada.

Just as mother Earth once became dislocated and fell down from her orbit into the waters of the Garbhodaka Ocean, so we had all fallen from our original position in the spiritual world into a bottomless pit of conditioned life. But somehow we have received the greatest boon: we have met a *mahātmā* and, as the Earth was delivered by Lord Varāha, been delivered by you from the clutches of Māyā.

However, the undeniable difference between the rescue of mother Earth and us is that the former submitted heartfelt prayers along with the demigods, whereas we were obstinate, envious, proud, and absolutely and irrevocably determined not to admit our pitiful, lamentable position. In other words, we were fiercely opposed to entertain any notion that we needed rescuing!

So, Śrīla Prabhupada, in these adverse circumstances you met us, but with compassion and genuine concern you defeated and smashed all our foolish ideas, disarming our false pride and ego, which for a long time had successfully convinced us that we were beyond reproach and reproof and and that we should look upon religion with abhorrence. You revealed the knowledge of our real *svarūpa* as eternal servants of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

You did all this and more by initiating the cleansing process of devotional service (beginning with *śravaṇam* and *kīrtanam*), creating an international society of devotees, and planting the seed of *bhakti* in countless hearts.

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Antya-līlā* 4.46-47, it is said, “Glorious is that devotee who does not give up the shelter of His Lord, and glorious is the Lord who does not abandon His servant. If by chance a servant falls down and goes somewhere else, glorious is the master who captures him and brings him back by the hair.”

So today and every single day of this life and the next, we humbly beg that you may not abandon us but fix us at your beautiful lotus feet, overlooking our disqualifications, and, even if we protest, engage us in the service of the servant of the servant of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

This movement is inseparable from you, and we beg Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madanamohana to give us the intelligence and strength to push on this movement so that others can also be rescued by you. We acknowledge that we can never repay the debt we owe you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but we hope that our participation in the movement you created can bring some satisfaction to Your Divine Grace.

Your servants in Vancouver, Canada.

## Vienna

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet, where we pray to eternally remain.

We want to take this most auspicious opportunity to thank you from the core of our hearts for allowing us to spend our insignificant lives in the shelter of your wonderful society for Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which is the only protection from the burning fire of material existence.

Along with your wonderful books, you also left qualified disciples who surrendered their lives unto your mission and now guide us according to your desire. As we surrender our lives unto them, we can see your plans gradually unfolding as your unparalleled teachings start affecting people all over the world in so many transcendental ways. It is a great privilege to be a member of this movement, which is changing the face of the world.

Although since your physical departure ISKCON has gone through some difficult phases, it is undoubtedly still your movement and successful in so many fields. We are sure you are very pleased by seeing the successes, but you are also trying to warn and correct us by making us realize how tragic are deviations of any kind. However, we are convinced that you want us to always go forward, transforming the whole planet before it’s too late.

This means you still expect us to relentlessly infiltrate every possible place with your books, the holy name, and *prasādam* until such big changes take place that we cannot even imagine, the most positive changes this world has seen in Kali-yuga. The whole secret of success is simply to please you by understanding your mission and then carrying it out in the way you wanted us to.

We know that we are only tiny soldiers in your gigantic army, but we are confident that you will give us your unfailing protection as long as we sincerely stick to your mission, spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness without any self-motivation.

Falling again and again in the dust of your radiant lotus feet, we beg to eternally remain in the service of your glorious servants.

Your servants in Vienna, Austria.

## Villaggio Hare Kṛṣṇa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept our humble obeisances. We are your spiritual grandchildren, and you are a very special and merciful grandfather.

What would have happened to us if you had not saved us?

In the material world, sometimes a drowning man is saved but then his savior goes away and leaves him to face all the many problems of life. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, not only saved us from the ocean of material sufferings; you also gave us proper clothes and proper food and taught us how to speak, behave, and think in the right way, like real ladies and gentlemen.

You filled us up with spiritual wealth, knowledge, and purity. Furthermore, you are still leading us, making us servants of your servants.

Every day you protect and tolerate your spiritual grandchildren and guide them with loving care so that they can reach the supreme abode of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Thanks to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the prophecy of Lord Caitanya is being fulfilled: the world is now dotted with ISKCON temples, farms, and communities, and the name of Lord Caitanya is being heard in every corner of the world.

Thanks to your mercy, a wonderful future is unfolding for every devotee.

We are no longer Italians or Indians or Americans. We hold a new passport, we have another status, another nationality—we are now ISKCON devotees, and this is our real identity.

We thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, billions of times.

Your humble servants at Villaggio Hare Kṛṣṇa, Bergamo, Italy.

## Vilnius

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day, the anniversary of your appearance.

You are the Lord’s *āśraya-vigraha,* a merciful manifestation of the Lord whom one must take shelter of. You have full faith in Kṛṣṇa and absolute faith in your spiritual master, and you are blessed by Lord Caitanya. Thus you are fulfilling the will of Lord Caitanya. You are the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, and by your example you show the way to spiritualize the world. With patience, kindness, and unbroken force, with Kṛṣṇa in your heart, you save the fallen souls of this material world. Your confidence in your disciples was justified, for they are continuing the mission of Lord Caitanya—organizing the distribution of your transcendental books and setting up preaching centers. Not just towns and cities but even countries and whole continents are now ablaze with Kṛṣṇa consciousness! The wishes of the spiritual masters in the *paramparā* are coming true. To serve your disciples, our spiritual masters, is the aim and core of our lives. Like you, they arrive in the ISKCON centers, teach, and correct the faults of the devotees, thus enabling them to proceed in spiritual life. That is your causeless mercy.

In these hard times for our country, full of economic difficulties and ungodliness, we strive with complete devotion to dedicate our lives to the *saṅkīrtana* mission of Lord Caitanya, and we ask for your blessings.

Your devotees in Vilnius, Lithuania.

## Vitoria

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble and prostrated obeisances unto your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious day in the whole world, when we celebrate your appearance, I feel extremely honored to be able to write something to glorify you. I feel this way even though I am unable to do so properly, since there is no limit to your glories and to recount them all would be impossible for anyone, especially such an insignificant being as myself. Despite my shortcomings, however, I shall try to describe a small fraction of your unlimited opulences.

By coming to the Western world to preach, you showed a lot of strength, knowledge, renunciation, power, beauty, and wealth. You manifested these opulences of the Supreme Absolute in big proportions, and doubtlessly you would have manifested even more of them if you had deemed it necessary, for you were empowered by Kṛṣṇa.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for showing us the Kṛṣṇa conscious world. You have been so merciful, and you still are so tolerant to allow people like myself to participate in this wonderful International Society for Krishna Consciousness. Indeed, it is well known that even the demigods pray to have this rare opportunity.

Since I was a child I felt the need to know who God was. As time went by this feeling increased even more, to the point that I once went to church and fell prostrate before Jesus Christ and beg him to reveal God to me, if He existed.

Right when I left the church I got a book by Your Divine Grace entitled *Beyond Birth and Death*. In this small book I found an ocean of information about God. I felt as if that book had been written especially for me. All I needed to know was in that small book; the ideas about such things as reincarnation and the living entities being part and parcel of God did not seem new to me; rather, this knowledge seemed to be coming back to my memory. All these topics seemed to revive something I already knew.

Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, could have at that exact moment answered my appeal and saved me from forgetfulness. For this reason and for many others I am absolutely sure that you were sent by God, and that nobody can substitute for you, and that my debt to you is eternal.

I can never repay you for all the mercy you have shown me. I humbly pray that you are happy to see me trying to serve your servants, especially my spiritual master, eternally.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

The servants-of-your-servants in Vitoria, Brazil.  
(written by Kamalā-kaṇṭhā-devī dāsī)

## Vṛndāvana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Early-morning *maṅgala ārati*  
in your samādhi in Vṛndāvana …  
*Vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*.  
No place is more suitable;  
here you are so much present.

On a plane with a swami  
who wants to glorify you  
in a hundred thousand different ways,  
in a meeting with a chairman  
who is taxing his brain,  
in a *harināma* with new devotees  
enthusiastic to preach …  
I pray to be in the association  
of your devotees all the time.

Being fully absorbed  
in serving Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa,  
you have become devotion personified.  
Just by serving you,we can realize all levels of devotional service.

May we never forget  
that we were residents of hell,  
quite at home as incorrigible sinners,  
and miserable also,  
which is natural in a hellish condition.  
Yet somehow you saved us.  
Who has the power to repay you?

You are most pleased  
by even a tiny bit of devotional service,  
but still more by those who continue your  
mission to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness  
everywhere.

Alas, with sadness  
we observe your unfortunate sons  
who have lost faith in ISKCON today.  
We’ve seen weakness in individuals,  
mistakes have been made.  
Yet as the Ganges purifies herself  
with fresh water from the source,  
ISKCON is full of devotees  
who are dedicated to you.

Macdonalds and Kentucky Fried  
for Kali’s lunch and breakfast,  
a game of Gulf War for sports,  
earthquakes taking the land away from the “lords,”  
gamblers trying to beat the odds…  
Who will not get AIDS?  
Yet *saṅkīrtana* soldiers  
distribute millions of your books.  
No doubt: *kṛṣṇa sūrya sama*;  
it is the beginning of the golden age.

We sit in your room in Vṛndāvana,  
in front of your *mūrti* behind the desk.  
We just chant Hare Kṛṣṇa  
round after round  
and become again invigorated  
to take up the glorious task.

We take the dust from Vṛndāvana  
and place it on our head.  
We circumambulate Govardhana  
and visit Rādhā-kuṇḍa.  
Your inconceivable mercy—  
we need every single drop of it.  
Yet most of all the dust of your lotus feet,  
for without it, even in the Dhāma  
we’d wander aimlessly.

Spiritual life for everyone:  
not until every germ is delivered  
can we rest.  
Please accept us, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
as you watch our childish attempts;  
please bless us to do better  
and serve you again and again.

Your servants in Vṛndāvana, India.  
(written by Kadamba Kānana dāsa)

## Warsaw

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

This Vyāsa-pūjā day is very auspicious because it makes us remember you and understand our debt to you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most merciful person and the most perfect follower of Lord Caitanya because you, without any consideration, give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to those who are not fit to get it. We are born in a very low part of the world. By the vedic standard we are the most fallen, without any culture or religion. Yet you gave us this Kṛṣṇa consciousness and you forced us to take it. This is the biggest miracle in the world.

In your books you left us everything we need for our enlightenment. And through your desire and the desire of your sincere followers, all your books have been available in our language for three years. Please empower us with the strength and determination to give out these books and this knowledge to as many people as possible in order to continue your mission.

Although you never visited Poland, everybody can feel your presence through your books. Day by day more and more people join your movement. In every big town there is a center or temple. As people appreciate your movement, they become more and more pious. This is all the manifestation of your mercy. Please allow us to be worthy to fully represent your mercy.

We bow at your lotus feet and thank you for saving us from material existence. We humbly pray at your lotus feet, Please let us absorb our minds in fulfilling your desires. In this way there will be no place for material desires, and without obstacles we will be able to go back to Kṛṣṇa.

Your grand-disciples in New Ramaṇa Reti, Warsaw, Poland.

## Wellington

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

O window to the spiritual world, as a fragrant lotus blossom’s from mud,  
ISKCON has appeared in this world by your efforts.

In a hospital full of weak or feverishly delirious patients,  
Your book distributors and Food-for-Lifers, etc., are doctors administering relief,  
Their moonlike faces shining rays  
On Kali-yuga’s poor, miserable, anxious, and envious faces.

Amid the big cities’ ugrakarmic clamor and clatter, screeching and honking,  
There sometimes arises a sweet and timeless sound—  
Ching ching CHING … ching ching CHING.  
Just as Vāmana’s toe cracked the universal coverings,  
Letting in spiritual waters,  
The holy name joyously and jubilantly appears.

Among the world’s impersonal philosophies, semi-religions, and superstitions,  
The Vaiṣṇava philosophy is like a woodman with an axe stepping into a forest  
Of weeds and moss-hung thorn trees, able to fell anything  
With a well-aimed blow.

Your books, Deities, *prasādam*, farms, and schools …  
Why cite their mundane counterparts?  
A wish-granting gem vs. a pellet of goat stool can only hint at  
the gulf of difference.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have put the spiritual world right in front of our eyes. And even though we shamelessly walk around with a bead-bag on our hands, inattentively carrying on like insane elephants in a flower garden, still, we have caught a glimpse of that world and hope to get a decent look, so to speak, in the not-too-distant future.

Please bless us to be eternally the servants of your servants.

Your grand-disciples in Wellington, New Zealand.

## Wicklow

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Vyāsa-pūjā is the day when your disciples and followers try to gather their thoughts, feelings, and appreciation for you. You have given us all the gift of transcendental knowledge regardless of our past activities and condition. Today is the day when we should be able to offer appropriate honest words, words that are supported by our actions throughout the year, words that are supported by our genuine attempts at cooperating and doing some positive service to push on your mission.

We here in this small community have to admit our failings in trying to serve you as you desire and as you have instructed in your teachings, but we will continue to try.

We are trying to cooperate, at least among ourselves, to associate in a Kṛṣṇa conscious way, and to educate our children in an environment which will help them become and remain Kṛṣṇa conscious. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were able to do everything perfectly, while we cannot do anything perfectly. Still, we beg that you give us the strength and intelligence to engage in Kṛṣṇa conscious activities as you have taught us. Then we may be of some help to you in your mission and at the same time save ourselves and our dependents from the cycle of birth and death, which we seem to know so well.

Finally, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us thank you again not just by easily written words but by action and meditation and now once more re-dedicate ourselves to you.

Your servants, the devotees at Śāntivana Dhāma, Wicklow, Ireland.

## Wroclaw

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Establishing a spiritual movement in the material world is never an easy undertaking, especially in Kali-yuga. Moreover, establishing a spiritual movement in Europe and America, which are the homelands of the most materialistic civilization ever to appear on the face of the earth, would seem to be an utterly impossible task. Few devotees would even dream of trying such a thing, which is no less than a frontal assault on the best-fortified citadel of Māyā, what to speak of actually making the attempt, and what to speak of actually succeeding. Practically speaking, it is an impossible task. As proof of the difficulty involved, it is important to recall that more than fifty years ago Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura sent several of his *sannyāsī* disciples to Europe to preach the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. They were unable to make any tangible progress. The Westerners were addicted to the pillars of sinful life, such as illicit sex, meat-eating, gambling, and intoxication, and were therefore uninterested in the sublime message of love of God.

Pure devotees typically prefer to spend the last years of their life in the holy land of Vṛndāvana, and many devotees vow never to leave this most sanctified place. But a devotee who has immersed himself in the mood of Vāsudeva Datta and Prahlāda Mahārāja cannot be happy simply with his own spiritual advancement. He is tasting the unlimited pleasure of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and he laments to see the conditioned souls foolishly trying to squeeze a few drops of pleasure out of the material world, which is a place not of pleasure but of pain.

But even though a devotee may desire to help the conditioned souls, only a greatly advanced devotee can actually awaken Kṛṣṇa consciousness within the heart of one who has ignored Kṛṣṇa for millions of lifetimes. In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* it is stated that only a devotee who has been empowered by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu can spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. This is further confirmed by Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, who said that one can tell how advanced a devotee is by how many conditioned souls he has brought to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa.

By these authorized statements from the *śāstras* and the great *ācāryas* in our disciplic succession, we can see that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are one of the most merciful and advanced servants of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Who can calculate how much your activities have pleased Him? It is therefore clear that, even among spotlessly pure devotees, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, occupy a unique position. Who else would leave Vṛndāvana to come to New York? And who else would tolerate the abominable conditions in the Bowery, where you chose to live in order to reach the most receptive class of Americans? The *Līlāmṛta* has depicted in detail the many abominable conditions you tolerated to start this movement in New York. On one occasion, the boy you lived with, David Allon, went crazy due to his drug habit and almost physically assaulted you. Even among pure devotees, who would have tolerated that and remained in New York? And after the first initiation ceremony, the devotees left you to clean up after the festivities. That you tolerated these and similar affronts proves your unlimited love and compassion for the conditioned souls and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Yet despite your obvious qualifications, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you took the humble position and said that “if I have any qualification, it is that I never deviated from the order of my spiritual master.” To be empowered as a *śaktyāveśa-avatāra* means that one has pleased the Lord to such an extent that the Lord has invested one with some of His own superhuman power. But how do we please the Lord? It is only by becoming an unflinching servant of his pure devotee. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, certainly pleased your spiritual master by rigidly adhering to his instructions, and because you did so you were empowered by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to an incredible degree. It is greatly to your credit that you fully took up the mood of your spiritual master and tirelessly worked to satisfy his great desire to spread this movement all over the world.

As devotees here in Poland, we aspire to push on your movement more and more by distributing as many of your books as possible, as well as by engaging in other preaching programs, such as large festivals, college preaching programs, and Sunday Feast programs. Please bestow your causeless mercy upon us so that we can always remain pure and fixed in serving your great mission.

Your servants in Wroclaw, Poland.

## Xalo

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet!

Let us take advantage of this most auspicious day to intensify our remembrance of, appreciation of, and gratitude for your divine appearance, radiant as the sun, which dispelled the dark clouds of *māyā* in this age of forgetfulness of Kṛṣṇa. Being the personification of Lord Caitanya’s mercy, you distributed the message of love of Kṛṣṇa everywhere and to everyone, without making any distinction as to who was qualified and who was not. In a mood similar to that of Vāsudeva Datta, who offered himself to suffer for all the sins of all the living entities in the universe if in this way they had a chance to go back to Godhead, you were prepared to take any risk if thereby the mission of Kṛṣṇa consciousness would be spread.

According to Vedic rules and regulations, lowborn persons like myself are not allowed even to study the Vedic scriptures, and certainly it is unthinkable that they would have anything to do with translating or publishing those sacred texts. Thus, today, looking back more than two decades, it still, and maybe more than ever, strikes me as a miracle how you stepped into my life and, from the very beginning, offered me the opportunity to perform devotional service in connection with the publication of your translations of the most important Vedic literatures, such as *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.*

I remember well those first feeble attempts at translating your transcendental books. The devotees had asked me to translate *The First Step in God Realization,* a stapled paperback edition of the first chapter of the Second Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* probably more with the idea of purifying my miserable existence than to obtain a useful translation. At that time, still completing the last few months of military service, I was sitting in a tiny room in a tower writing by hand on the back of a pad of Morse code message paper. I was struggling to understand the unusual language and its contents, a far-out philosophy, and then to put it into my own language, an attempt which undoubtedly was less than mediocre. Glancing at a picture of Muralī Manohara once in a while and muttering the *mahā-mantra,* I was begging Kṛṣṇa for inspiration, but, not being a very qualified recipient, I felt that to translate your words was really a difficult task.

Nonetheless, by the sublime combination of your resolute determination to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness and your infinite causeless mercy to accept even the most insignificant offering that would help to accomplish this goal, you agreed to utilize my insignificant contribution, and in this way you allowed me to somehow or other participate in your mission.

And today still, and maybe more than ever, it strikes me as a miracle that you have not kicked me away a long time ago, seeing my unworthiness, but that you are still keeping me connected with the sublime service of publishing your books. Although I am only an insignificant screw in the BBT machine, every time a new book arrives from the printer I feel that special joy that comes from being able to offer you a newly published title. You made no secret of your pleasure upon seeing a new volume printed in any language of the world, and therefore I feel assured that my life will be successful if I have participated, even to the minutest extent, in giving you that satisfaction.

Books are the basis, and there can be no doubt that as long as your books are here, those who publish them, those who distribute them, those who receive them, and those who read them will all stand on a solid basis from where to fight against *māyā* and come closer to the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

With your books you are giving us Kṛṣṇa and His pure devotee(s). What more can we ask for…?

Your servants in Xalo (Alicante), Spain.  
(written by Vedavyāsa dāsa)

## Yogyakarta

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances upon your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

On this day of your appearance, you are worshiped as the representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, the compiler of the Vedic literature. You are certainly Śrīla Vyāsadeva’s self-effulgent representative, as no personality in history has ever published and widely distributed so many volumes of Vedic literature in so many languages of the world.

Yogyakarta is supposed to be a center of education in Indonesia. However, as you have taught us, mundane education without *rāja-vidyā,* the king of knowledge or education, is simply *avidyā,* or ignorance, and may be compared to a long row of zeros, the sum total of which is also zero. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are teaching the world what real education is—i.e., to know the spirit soul, the material nature, and the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who is the controller of both, and to engage fully in His devotional service.

It was your desire that Kṛṣṇa consciousness be taught in a systematic way to the intelligent class of men. So we have established the Nārāyaṇa Smṛti Educational Foundation to further your desires in this field. We hope to educate the people in general, and students and intellectuals in particular, regarding the purport of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* verse 2.1.6, which states:

*etāvān sāṅkhya-yogābhyāṁ sva-dharma-pariniṣṭhayā*  
*janma-lābhaḥ paraḥ puṁsām ante nārāyaṇa smṛtiḥ*

**“The highest perfection of human life, achieved either by complete knowledge of matter and spirit, by practice of mystic powers, or by perfect discharge of occupational duty, is to remember the Personality of Godhead at the end of life.”**

We are by no means qualified to preach this message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness as you have done. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the real preacher of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. All that we can do is to follow your instructions and try to assist you in pushing on the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. On this holy day of your appearance, we dedicate ourselves once again to your service, and we pray always for the shelter of your lotus feet and the association of your disciples and grand-disciples in this wonderful ISKCON society, which you have created.

Your servants in Yogyakarta, Indonesia.

## Zurich

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

I would like to thank you for allowing this rascal to still take part in your transcendental mission of distributing books and for allowing me to inspire and associate with and be inspired by so many great souls all over the world who have taken up your book distribution mission as their life and soul. All of your sincere followers know that it is your greatest pleasure to see and hear that your books are nicely distributed in big quantities all over the world. And it is your pleasure which keeps us alive.

I never forget the big smile you had in England on your Vyāsa-pūjā day when Tamāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami, who stood by your side, spoke about the glorious mission you got from your Guru Mahārāja, and how you brought this mission to the West and distributed it single-handedly. Such a great sacrifice, such deep compassion you had in order to save all of us fallen souls! This we all realized there. We could see that book distribution is your life and soul—and a wave of intense love emanating from you overflooded all of us. Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Please let us drown more and more in these ecstatic waves, because to drown in the waves of your unlimited love of Kṛṣṇa means the end of material existence. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glory and victory to your transcendental mission!

Your humble servants in Zurich, Switzerland.  
(written by Rohiṇīsuta dāsa)

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from the Publishers

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, Bombay

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

We are fortunate to have met you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because one must accept a genuine *guru* in the line of disciple succession. Otherwise we would have remained in ignorance, without a clue as to what is the Supreme Absolute Truth.

The other day I saw a book about World War II. It was a big book with many pictures. One part was about the atomic bomb exploding in Hiroshima, Japan. There were pictures drawn by an artist who survived the bombing. The heat of the explosion was so great that people literally burst into flames. Their hair and clothes were completely burned off. The people ran for the river, for this was the only relief.

Similarly, our heads are on fire with the flames of this holocaust of birth, death, old age, and disease. The cooling effect of the lotus feet of the *guru* are our only relief from this burning heat. This cooling effect can be attained only by satisfying the qualified *guru,* and finding such a *guru* is very rare. You are such a *guru,* Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you are an ocean of mercy. You were staying in Vṛndāvana, engaging in blissful service to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. What was the need for you to come to America at such an advanced age? Still you came to give mercy to others.

In his *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Ādi* 1.45) Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja defines the position of a genuine *guru:*

*guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rupe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees”.**

We are unable to see Kṛṣṇa directly, so Kṛṣṇa has sent you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, His direct manifestation. Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja writes,

“The living entity is bound around the neck by the chain of *māyā* because he has forgotten that he is eternally a servant of Kṛṣṇa. If the conditioned soul engages in the service of the Lord and simultaneously carries out the orders of his spiritual master and serves him, he can get out of the clutches of *māyā* and become eligible for shelter at Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have been wandering all over the universe like lost children. Now that we have come in contact with the shelter of your lotus feet, our good fortune is unlimited and unfathomable. You have made it possible to remember what we have forgotten for so long—that we are the eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa.

Just as a qualified *guru* is very rare, so is a qualified disciple. My situation is like that of a man in a half-conscious state—sometimes awake and sometimes asleep. I’m always struggling to hear the words of the *Upaniṣads: uttiṣṭha jāgrata prāpya varān nibodhata / kṣurasya dhārā niśitā duratyayā durgaṁ pathas tat kavayo vadanti:* \[*Kaṭha Upaniṣad* 1.3.14\] “O living entities, you are sleeping in this material world! Please get up and take advantage of your human form of life! The path of spiritual realization is very difficult. It is sharp like a razor’s edge. That is the opinion of learned, transcendental scholars.”

We here at the BBT are trying to publish all of your books in various Indian languages. We hope that by this endeavor you will be pleased. Then the mission of our human from of life will be complete.

Your servants at the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, Bombay

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, Indonesia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we offer our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Your appearance in this world, both in your *vapu* and *vāṇī* forms, has created, and is continuing to create, auspiciousness for all the fallen conditioned souls in this dark Age of Kali. So many souls are being enlightened by reading your Bhaktivedanta purports and taking to the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

No words can adequately glorify you, nor will any amount of service ever be sufficient to repay you for your unlimited mercy upon us. Our words are only worthwhile if we repeat what we have heard from you. We pray that you will continue to accept our humble offerings of service to your Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, just as Mother Ganges accepts a little Ganges water offered to her in one’s palms.

We are amazed and enlivened by seeing the inconceivable service done by so many of your disciples and grand-disciples in the field of book publication and distribution. In comparison to them, we are doing very little. We simply pray for their mercy so that some day we may also be able to do some significant service that is pleasing to you in publishing and distributing your books.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your fallen servants at the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, Indonesia.

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, Mediterranean

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

It is very difficult to find the appropriate words to glorify Your Divine Grace on this auspicious day of your appearance. The only thing to do is to try to please and satisfy you by promising to be always engaged in devotional service at your lotus feet.

The prominent feeling that we have to keep in our hearts is the sentiment of gratitude. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for saving us from the dangers of the material world and from the ocean of birth and death. Thank you more and more for allowing us to serve you in the way you like most: by producing and printing your wonderful literature.

We will never be able to repay the eternal debt we owe you for giving us your wonderful books. The only way to even attempt to clear this debt is to publish and distribute your books in bigger and bigger quantities. In this way we will be sure to spread your glories and the glories of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Please keep us under the shelter of your lotus feet and let us be the instrument to expand your mercy.

Your servants at the Mediterranean BBT.

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, North America

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you on this holiest of days, your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please forgive me for having offended you and having caused you displeasure. Forgive me for also having offended your disciples. Intoxicated by false pride, selfishness, and insensitivity, I have offended you, who are the greatest of personalities, full of compassion for all souls who have drifted from their eternal devotion to Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have tried to dedicate our lives to you and fulfill your mission—that every man and woman in the United States get one of your books. Your books alone give the real happiness that one is searching for in life. Therefore, the more profusely your books are distributed, the more we can change the mood of this Age of Kali. We seem to have fallen short of that lately, but we want to be given the opportunity to endeavor to fulfill your mission and give pleasure to you throughout this life and the next. You are everything to us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You teach us that Kṛṣṇa is kind, and we have to learn to be kind to each other. Please be kind to us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and let us join you in your eternal *līlā*. Don’t kick us away.

Your servants at the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, North America.

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, Sweden

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of you divine lotus feet.

This Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and your Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, which you so carefully founded and organized, are a full manifestation of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. They are expansions of Goloka Vṛndāvana, the spiritual world.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given the standard knowledge of self-realization for a long time to come. You are not just a great religious teacher—you are the savior of all humanity. We can see very clearly that without the knowledge presented in your books, modern society is heading for a complete disaster. Sudden wars, mass hysteria, drastic changes in politics, economy, climate, etc., have become a daily routine in the last few years. Forgetfulness of Kṛṣṇa is the cause of all these problems. But most people are unaware of this fact. They will never understand unless they read your books.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are given ample facilities for the production of your books. No longer can we blame technology or a lack of funds; the only cause for delays in your book production mission is our own insincerity. We pray for a drop of your mercy so that we can go on until all of your books will be published in each and every one of the 2,000+ languages of the world. (And when that task is accomplished, we shall do them all over again.)

Thank you for engaging us in that wonderful mission.

Your worthless servants at New Rādhā-kuṇḍa, the BBT temple in Sweden.

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from Other Sources

## Arabic Preaching in North Africa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

I remember when I joined. The devotees told me that you said that you would take the dust of the feet of anyone who opened a temple in an Arabic country. Since I was born in an Arabic country, these words had a fantastic meaning to me, and I was inspired to do something to please you.

Since then I have meditated on these words of yours, and now fourteen years have passed. Just last year I went with another devotee from Morocco, and we did our first *saṅkīrtana* book distribution. It was a wonderful experience because the Arabs were taking your books enthusiastically.

I could not believe my eyes. I thought it would be difficult to preach, but now I see it is so easy; we have only to go to the front.

I understood that this was happening because you are behind it—you are showering your mercy on the most fallen people on the planet. All the credit goes to Your Divine Grace, by whom the most difficult area in the world has become an inspiring field that is fertile for planting the *bhakti-latā-bīja*. There is no doubt that the success of Arabic preaching is due to you.

When you came to the USA, you came through the Straits of Gibraltar. You passed by Tangier and Port Said, cities in the Arabic countries of Morocco and Egypt, respectively. You may be interested to know that we met people in these countries who are already chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa because somehow or other they have read your books.
